Abstract: Earth-observation (EO) foundation models have become exceptionally effective at learning se mantic, high-dimensional geospatial embeddings, while modern weather and climate models have demonstrated that Earth-specific geometry, spherical operators, meshes, and hybrid physical solvers can materially improve prediction. Yet these two advances are not equivalent. A conventional latent embedding has no inherent physical transformation law, whereas scalar fields, tangent polar-vector fields, axial/pseudovector quantities, covectors, and higher-order tensors transform differently under rotations, reflections, and changes of local coordinate frame. This proposal asks whether a general purpose Earth foundation model should preserve those distinctions explicitly, or whether standard embeddings plus augmentation already learn everything that matters. The central contribution is therefore not a more complicated architecture by assumption, but a staged falsification program. A compute-conscious ERA5 dry run first compares conventional, augmentation-matched, typed equivariant, and Hodge/Helmholtz variants under spatial, temporal, orientation, and low-data shifts. Only if explicit geometric typing yields reproducible improvements does the program advance toward a multimodal Earth foundation model in which semantic embeddings coexist with physically typed fields. The proposed gap is narrower and more defensible than claiming that current models ignore geometry entirely: several systems already respect spherical domain geometry, and emerging work explicitly learns scalar/vector fields on spheres. The unresolved question is whether foundation-scale, multimodal, parity-aware field typing produces practical gains beyond those existing approaches.

</details>


### [67] [SkyAnchor: Updating Metric-scale Aerial 3D Gaussian Scenes from Unposed Ground-View Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13903)
*Zhuoxiao Li,Xinyi Liu,Taoyu Wu,Yinrui Ren,Tongyan Hua,Ou Jing,Shuai Zhang,Dongli Wu,Rongjun Qin,Ge Lin Kan,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SkyAnchor 将已有航拍 SfM 与 3DGS 场景作为固定支架，把无位姿的地面图像序列配准并增量更新到该场景中，实现街道级外观补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航拍场景已有可靠的度量 SfM 重建与预训练 3DGS，但后续采集的地面序列无位姿、无全局尺度；单帧跨视角定位脆弱，长轨迹易漂移，联合从头重建又代价高。

Method: 先对短组连续地面帧在几何验证过的航拍支撑下定位，得到稀疏锚点位姿；再用锚点约束的子图恢复完整地面轨迹，在增量配准与光束法平差中固定前后锚点位姿；最后插入过滤后的地面高斯并保持航拍视角，再做轻量联合精修。

Result: 在七个真实航拍—地面场景上取得准确的度量地面轨迹，并得到航拍与地面视角渲染质量均较强的更新后 3D 高斯场景。

Conclusion: 以航拍场景为锚的增量式配准与高斯插入可有效完成跨视角场景更新，避免脆弱的单帧定位与长轨迹漂移。

Abstract: We study how to update a pre-built aerial scene with a newly captured, unposed ground-view sequence. The aerial scene already contains a reliable metric Structure-from-Motion (SfM) reconstruction and a pre-trained 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model, whereas the ground-view sequence is collected later to add street-level appearance but has unknown camera poses and global scale. Registering this sequence to the aerial SfM reconstruction is challenging because single-image cross-view localization is brittle and long trajectories are prone to drift. To address these challenges, we present SkyAnchor, which treats the existing aerial scene as a fixed scaffold for ground-view registration and scene update instead of jointly reconstructing aerial and ground imagery from scratch. It first localizes short groups of consecutive ground frames against geometrically verified aerial support, producing sparse anchor poses. It then recovers the full ground trajectory with anchor-constrained submaps, fixing the front and rear anchor poses during incremental registration and bundle adjustment. Finally, it inserts filtered ground Gaussians while preserving the aerial view, followed by lightweight joint refinement. Experiments on seven real aerial--ground scenes show accurate metric ground trajectories and updated 3D Gaussian scenes with strong aerial- and ground-view rendering quality.

</details>


### [68] [BLInD: Learning Driver Intent as a Distribution over Future Ego Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13941)
*Flavian Pegado,Ronit Hire,Shreyas Rajesh,Soham Phade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BLInD 仅用车端状态历史（速度、曲率、转向灯、车型）预测 top-k 未来自车轨迹分布，无相机/激光/地图/目标轨迹输入，跨数据集泛化，Orin 上 0.87–2.9 ms，并在 AEB 触发任务显著降误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式轨迹/意图分布模型依赖场景上下文（相机、LiDAR、地图、目标轨迹），而"blind"仅状态输入的模型通常退化为单一路径。作者希望验证：仅靠自车状态历史是否足以学到有用的多模态近期自车轨迹分布，并借助低延迟特性服务于安全关键场景（如 AEB）的可控意图采样接口。

Method: 提出 BLInD 紧凑网络，输入近期车辆状态历史（速度、曲率、指示器、车型等），输出未来自车轨迹的 top-k 分布。对比两种分布架构：自回归（AR）与 flow-matching。在混合平台开源数据与 Wayve 数据上训练，不做数据集特定适配，直接跨域评测（Wayve/Waymo/nuScenes）并接入 AEB 触发任务评估。

Result: flow-matching 模型 top-k ADE/FDE：Wayve 0.15/0.37 m，Waymo 0.15/0.36 m，nuScenes 0.28/0.59 m；AR 达到相当覆盖率。AEB 触发任务中，全候选策略将误报从 1.51% 降至 0.11%（AR，13.7×，94.9% TP）和 0.06%（flow-matching，25.1×，98.7% TP）。推理耗时：AR 头 0.87 ms、flow-matching 头 2.9 ms（NVIDIA DRIVE Orin）。

Conclusion: BLInD 同时具备"学习式、盲输入、跨域"三特性（此前工作未展示的组合），证明纯车辆状态历史即可提供可控且合理的意图采样分布，可作为下游系统的接口，AEB 是其中一种实例化；低延迟满足车规 ECU 实时部署。

Abstract: We present BLInD (Blind Learned Intent Distribution), a compact network that maps recent vehicle-state history (e.g. speed, curvature, indicator, and vehicle type) to a top-k distribution of future ego trajectories, with no camera, LiDAR, map, or object-track inputs. We find that vehiclestate history alone is sufficient to learn a useful multimodal distribution over near-term ego trajectories, and its low-latency nature makes it well-suited for safety-critical deployment. We investigate two distribution architectures, autoregressive (AR) and flow-matching, and train on both mixed-platform opensource and Wayve datasets. Both generalize without datasetspecific adaptation; the flow-matching model achieves best topk ADE/FDE of 0.15/0.37 m on Wayve, 0.15/0.36 m on Waymo, and 0.28/0.59 m on nuScenes, with the AR model reaching comparable coverage. Integrating the distributions into an AEB trigger task, a strict all-candidates policy reduces false positives from 1.51% to 0.11% with AR (13.7x reduction, 94.9% TP) and to 0.06% with flow-matching (25.1x reduction, 98.7% TP) compared to a 1-CTRV policy with 100% true positive score. BLInD runs in 0.87 ms with the AR head and 2.9 ms with the flow-matching head on an NVIDIA DRIVE Orin ECU making it compatible with real-time deployment on automotive ECUs. While existing learned distribution models rely on scene context and blind vehicle-state models typically collapse to a single path, BLInD is learned, blind, and cross-domain simultaneously, a combination not demonstrated by prior work. These results show that such a distribution provides a controllable and plausible intent sampling interface for downstream systems, with AEB as one instantiation.

</details>


### [69] [SGWIB:Sliced Gromov-Wasserstein Information Bottleneck for Video Highlight Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13966)
*Hanjuan Huang,Yung-Chieh Yeh,Hsing-Kuo Pao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构感知信息瓶颈框架SGWIB用于视频高光检测，通过Sliced Gromov-Monge Gap正则在压缩表示时保留片段间时序结构，并结合上下文解耦模块降低体育场景特定偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频高光检测需要既有判别性的片段表示，又需保留近邻与远距离片段间的时序关系。信息瓶颈原理能学习紧凑且任务相关的表示，但直接套用传统形式会忽略片段间关系结构，并在压缩时破坏高光相关的时序组织。

Method: 提出Sliced Gromov-Monge Gap（SGMG），一种结构感知正则项，度量给定源到瓶颈映射相对于最优切片结构对应关系所产生的超额关系扭曲。基于SGMG构建SGWIB信息瓶颈框架，在单模态视频高光检测中学习紧凑瓶颈表示并保留片段间时序结构。另引入Home-Away-Related上下文伪标签与上下文解耦模块，分离高光导向信息与上下文模式。

Result: 在MrHiSum与MoSu数据集上，SGWIB在所比较的单模态方法中取得最佳Kendall's tau、Spearman's rho、mAP@50与mAP@30。在MrHiSum上，视觉模型相比最强先前结果在这四项指标上分别提升0.031、0.031、0.87与0.75。

Conclusion: 结构感知的信息瓶颈正则化结合上下文解耦可提升片段级高光预测性能，验证了在压缩中显式保留时序关系结构的有效性。

Abstract: Video highlight detection aims to identify temporally important segments that capture the most informative or engaging events in a video. Reliable prediction therefore requires not only discriminative segment representations but also preservation of the temporal relationships among neighboring and distant segments. The information bottleneck principle has proven effective for learning compact and task-relevant representations, yet it has not been explored for video highlight detection, and applying conventional formulations directly would overlook inter-segment relational structure and distort highlight relevant temporal organization during compression. We therefore introduce the Sliced Gromov-Monge Gap (SGMG), a structure aware regularizer that measures the excess relational distortion induced by a prescribed source-to-bottleneck mapping relative to an optimal sliced structural correspondence. Building on SGMG, we develop SGWIB, an information-bottleneck framework for single-modal video highlight detection that learns compact bottleneck representations while preserving inter-segment temporal structure. We further introduce Home-Away-Related Contextual Pseudo-Labels and a contextual disentanglement module that reduce sports-specific contextual bias by separating highlight oriented information from contextual patterns. Experiments on MrHiSum and MoSu show that SGWIB attains the best Kendall's tau, Spearman's rho, mAP@50, and mAP@30 among the compared single-modal methods on both datasets. On MrHiSum, the visual model improves the strongest previous results by 0.031, 0.031, 0.87, and 0.75 on these four metrics, respectively. These results show that structure-aware information-bottleneck regularization combined with contextual disentanglement improves segment-level highlight prediction.

</details>


### [70] [Zero-Shot Cross-Material Ptychographic Phase Reconstruction Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13969)
*Wen-Chun Lin,Yu-Chee Tseng,Jen-Jee Chen,Nan-You Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种直接式"局部到全局"学习框架，用于4D-STEM叠层衍射相位重建，逐衍射图预测局部包裹相位块，再借助标定扫描位置与高斯加权拼接得到全场景相位图，实现免迭代推理、跨材料零样本迁移，速度较ePIE约提升10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统叠层相位重建被建模为迭代反问题，需反复更新物体与探针，面对大规模4D-STEM数据计算开销巨大；同时已有学习型方法多依赖特定训练域，跨材料泛化能力不明。

Method: 将重建解耦为局部预测与全局拼接：网络以单张衍射图为输入，用sin-cos（sine-cosine）表示输出局部包裹相位块，规避相位跳变；推理阶段依据标定扫描位置，用高斯加权拼接将局部块合成全场景相位图，全程无迭代精修。泛化评估采用零样本设定，模型仅在一材料上训练后直接用于另一材料。

Result: 在AuPd与MoS₂上双向跨材料迁移均保持一致效果；在学习型方法中取得最优全场景MSE、PSNR与MS-SSIM；端到端重建时间较迭代ePIE约缩短10倍。

Conclusion: 免迭代的直接局部到全局框架可在保持重建精度的同时显著提速，并展现跨材料零样本迁移潜力，为高效、可迁移的叠层相位重建提供可行路径。

Abstract: Ptychographic phase reconstruction is commonly formulated as an iterative inverse problem, requiring repeated object-probe updates and resulting in substantial computational cost for large-scale 4D-STEM data. We present a direct local-to-global learning framework that reconstructs full-field phase maps from diffraction measurements without iterative refinement during inference. The proposed network predicts local wrapped-phase patches from individual diffraction patterns using a sine-cosine representation, and the predictions are assembled into a full-field reconstruction using calibrated scan positions and Gaussian-weighted stitching. To evaluate generalization beyond the training domain, the model is trained on one material and directly applied to another in a zero-shot setting without target-domain fine-tuning. Experiments on AuPd and MoS$_2$ demonstrate consistent cross-material transfer in both directions, with the proposed method achieving the best full-field MSE, PSNR, and MS-SSIM among the evaluated learning-based methods. Compared with the iterative ePIE approach, the proposed direct local-to-global pipeline reduces end-to-end reconstruction time by approximately 10x, demonstrating its potential for efficient and transferable ptychographic reconstruction.

</details>


### [71] [Mind2Cloud: EEG-to-Point Cloud Generation with Two-Granularity Diffusion Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13991)
*Yongyi Lu,Xiongfeng Huang,Zhijing Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mind2Cloud 提出双粒度扩散解码的 EEG-to-point-cloud 生成框架：以时间感知解码器在高噪声早期阶段用全局 Transformer 捕获物体结构，在后期阶段用 PVCNN 细化局部几何，并通过可学习融合掩码跨扩散步自适应，另加对抗精修模块提升几何真实性与语义一致性，在 EEG-3D 全部 12 名被试上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 EEG-to-3D 重建方法统一使用同构扩散解码器，忽视了 EEG 表征与扩散去噪过程本身在语义粒度上的层级演化特性，导致解码器空间粒度与去噪轨迹不匹配。

Method: 核心为时间感知解码器：全局 Transformer 分支负责早期上采样阶段的高不确定性全局结构建模，局部 PVCNN 分支负责后期局部几何细节细化，二者通过可学习融合掩码在时间步间动态融合；并引入对抗精修模块增强几何真实性与语义一致性。在 EEG-3D 数据集 12 名被试上评测几何精度与语义对齐。

Result: 在 EEG-3D 数据集全部 12 名被试上，Mind2Cloud 在几何精度与语义对齐两项指标均超越先前工作，成为 EEG-to-point-cloud 生成的新基准，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 按扩散去噪由粗到细的轨迹动态调节解码器空间粒度，是提升脑信号驱动 3D 生成质量的有效归纳偏置，为脑机视觉解码与 3D 生成交叉方向提供了新基线。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D objects from brain signals offers a promising avenue for understanding human visual cognition. While prior work has shown initial success using EEG signals for 3D reconstruction, existing methods typically employ a uniform diffusion decoder, overlooking the evolving semantic granularity of both EEG representations and the diffusion denoising process. In this paper, we propose Mind2Cloud, a novel EEG-to-point-cloud generation framework based on two-granularity diffusion decoding. The core of Mind2Cloud is a time-aware decoder that integrates a global Transformer branch and a local Point-Voxel CNN (PVCNN) branch across diffusion timesteps through a learnable fusion mask. Specifically, Transformer layers are incorporated into the early upsampling stages to capture global object structure under high uncertainty, while PVCNN modules are used in later stages to refine local geometric details. Inspired by the hierarchical nature of EEG-based visual representations, this design dynamically adapts its spatial granularity in accordance with the coarse-to-fine trajectory of diffusion denoising. We further introduce an adversarial refinement module to enhance geometric realism and semantic consistency. Extensive experiments on the EEG-3D dataset across all 12 subjects demonstrate that Mind2Cloud outperforms prior work in both geometric accuracy and semantic alignment, setting a new benchmark for EEG-to-point-cloud generation. Our source code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [72] [CirrGuide: A Deep Cascaded Framework for Liver Cirrhosis Segmentation and Severity Classification from T2-Weighted MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14010)
*Muntaqim Ahmed Raju,Ruizhe Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CirrGuide：级联的肝硬化肝脏分割与严重度分级框架，用预测的软掩膜作为解剖先验引导分类分支，在CirrMRI600+ T2W 2D 上取得89.83% Dice与69.58%分级准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝硬化引起肝脏进行性结构改变并可导致严重并发症，严重度评估对疾病监测与治疗规划很重要；但影像模式细微、空间变异大、相邻分期相似，使分级困难。作者希望通过显式地把定位与分类关联起来缓解该问题。

Method: 深度级联框架：ResNet50编码器+Attention U-Net解码器先预测软肝硬化肝脏掩膜；该掩膜作为解剖先验输入ResNet50分类分支，分支融合全局多尺度特征与掩膜引导的注意力池化区域特征，输出Mild/Moderate/Severe三级分类。

Result: 在官方CirrMRI600+ T2W 2D split上，分割89.83% Dice、84.14% mIoU；分级69.58%准确率、61.55% macro F1。与仅分割、仅分类及多任务基线相比，定位与分级均有提升。

Conclusion: 将预测的肝硬化肝脏掩膜作为解剖先验可同时改善定位与严重度分级，验证了显式连接分割与分类的级联设计有效性，属医学影像领域的专用方法。

Abstract: We present CirrGuide, a deep cascaded framework for cirrhotic liver segmentation and severity classification. Cirrhosis causes progressive structural changes in the liver and can lead to serious clinical complications, making severity assessment important for disease monitoring and treatment planning. However, severity classification is challenging because imaging patterns are often subtle, spatially variable, and similar across adjacent stages. CirrGuide addresses this by explicitly linking localization with classification. A ResNet50 encoder with an Attention U-Net decoder first predicts a soft cirrhotic liver mask, which is then used as an anatomical prior in a ResNet50-based classification branch. This branch combines global multi-scale features with mask-guided attention-pooled regional features to classify Mild, Moderate, and Severe cirrhosis. On the official CirrMRI600+ T2-weighted (T2W) 2D split, CirrGuide achieves 89.83% Dice and 84.14% mIoU for segmentation, 69.58% accuracy and 61.55% macro F1-score for severity classification. Compared with segmentation-only, classification-only, and multi-task baselines, CirrGuide improves both localization and severity classification, demonstrating the benefit of using predicted cirrhotic liver masks as anatomical priors for cirrhosis analysis.

</details>


### [73] [Quantum-Gated LiteSSD: A Parameter-Efficient Lightweight Hybrid Quantum-Classical Framework for Forward-Looking Sonar Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14025)
*Niloy Kumar Mondal,Poulomi Sarker Puja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Quantum-Gated LiteSSD，一种参数高效的混合量子-经典前视声呐目标检测器，将 QuCNet 式多电路量子处理重构为以恒等为中心的通道门控机制用于空间特征调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前视声呐目标检测对水下感知很重要，但嵌入式平台部署需要极紧凑模型，因此探索量子计算以在参数量与精度之间取得平衡。

Method: 基于 LiteSSD，将 QuCNet 风格的多电路量子处理重新表述为以恒等映射为中心的通道门控机制，用于空间特征调制，构建混合量子-经典检测器。

Result: Watertank 数据集上达 90.84% mAP50，参数比 YOLO26s 少约 62 倍、比 SSD-VGG16 少约 164.3 倍；UATD 上达 70.37% mAP50，仅 0.150M 参数，比 SSGA-YOLO 小约 4.1 倍。

Conclusion: 该方法在前视声呐检测中实现了有效的参数-精度权衡，验证了量子门控机制在极轻量水下检测中的可行性。

Abstract: Forward-looking sonar object detection is essential for underwater perception, yet deployment on embedded platforms requires highly compact models. To address this challenge, we explore quantum computing and introduce Quantum-Gated LiteSSD, a parameter-efficient hybrid quantum--classical detector that reformulates QuCNet-style multi-circuit quantum processing as an identity-centered channel-gating mechanism for spatial feature modulation. Experiments on the Marine Debris Watertank dataset and UATD forward-looking sonar benchmarks demonstrate an effective parameter--accuracy trade-off. The proposed detector achieves 90.84% $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ on Watertank with approximately $62\times$ fewer parameters than YOLO26s and $164.3\times$ fewer parameters than SSD-VGG16. On UATD, the model achieves 70.37% $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ with only 0.150M parameters, making it approximately $4.1\times$ smaller than SSGA-YOLO while retaining meaningful multi-class detection capability.

</details>


### [74] [LPA-CWM: A Learned Physical Adjudicator for Motion Reasoning with Counterfactual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14073)
*Kunwei Wu,Xiang Liu,Guocai Yao,Junming Chen,Zhikang Chen,Min Zhang,Pengwei Wang,Sen Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LPA-CWM 用轻量学习型物理裁判器(LPA)对反事实世界模型的多候选运动响应做可靠性加权聚合,并给出完整性感知的评测协议 CMC,在 DAVIS/Kinetics 上大幅超越均匀聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实世界模型(CWM)通过对比事实预测与干预预测来提取运动,但不同目标帧掩码下生成的响应可靠性差异大,而现有做法用均匀权重聚合,把不可靠响应与可靠响应等同对待,造成运动估计质量下降。核心动机是把"响应聚合"重新表述为候选可靠性学习问题。

Method: 提出 LPA-CWM:冻结 CWM 预测器与干预生成器,仅训练 3.0M 参数的轻量 Learned Physical Adjudicator(LPA)。LPA 在无序候选集上比较视觉上下文与响应结构,预测各候选的相对权重;加权后的响应经窗口化定位与一次配对重评估以恢复运动。训练数据为稠密 MOVi-F 轨迹。另提出 Completeness-aware Motion Correspondence(CMC)评测协议,以真值为锚,联合衡量定位、轨迹完整性、可见性与连续性,并将可见动态点上的缺失预测计为失败。

Result: 在 DAVIS 与 Kinetics 子集上,LPA-CWM 相对 Uniform CWM 将 DCA_avg 分别提升 60.0% 与 29.0%,并在 TAP-Vid First 指标下提升跟踪精度。LPA 仅 3.0M 参数,训练与推理开销低。

Conclusion: 可靠加权聚合比均匀聚合更有效地利用反事实响应,轻量裁判器加一次重评估即可显著提升运动提取质量;CMC 协议提供了更严格、完整的评测视角。整体属视频世界模型运动提取方向的通用 CV/ML 方法,缺乏机器人或具身场景的实机验证。

Abstract: Counterfactual world models (CWM) extract motion from pretrained video predictors by comparing factual and intervened predictions. However, responses generated under different target-frame masks vary in reliability, while uniform aggregation weights them equally. We formulate response aggregation as candidate reliability learning and propose LPA-CWM with a lightweight Learned Physical Adjudicator (LPA). Trained on dense MOVi-F trajectories, the 3.0M-parameter LPA compares visual context and response structure across an unordered candidate set to predict relative weights, while the CWM predictor and intervention generator remain frozen. The weighted responses undergo windowed localization and one paired re-evaluation to recover motion. We also introduce Completeness-aware Motion Correspondence (CMC), a ground-truth-anchored evaluation protocol that jointly measures localization, trajectory completeness, visibility, and continuity, counting missing predictions as failures on visible dynamic points. On the evaluated DAVIS and Kinetics subsets, LPA-CWM improves $\mathrm{DCA}_{\mathrm{avg}}$ over Uniform CWM by 60.0\% and 29.0\%, respectively, and also improves tracking accuracy under TAP-Vid First. A quick overview is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [75] [Adapting Open-Weight MLLMs to Generate Point Prompts for Electron Microscopy Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14080)
*Samia Mohinta,Albert Cardona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作训练LoRA适配器，使开源多模态大模型（MLLM）根据自然语言请求为冻结的microSAM分割器生成点提示，在三个线粒体电镜数据集上验证可行性，Qwen3-VL微调后AP50从0.247升至0.736，可迁移至未见数据集与独立EM体数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可提示分割模型（如microSAM）依赖人工点提示，自动化需要无用户输入地生成提示。作者探究开源多模态大模型能否从自然语言请求生成点坐标，交给冻结的分割器，从而建立可审查、语言驱动的定位-解码链路。

Method: 将三个线粒体数据集的掩膜转换为"图像+指令→质心坐标"训练样本；冻结MLLM主干与microSAM，仅训练LoRA适配器；结合监督微调与奖励优化；与监督式质心热图检测器对比，并测试跨数据集迁移、请求表述鲁棒性和第二分割器的坐标复用性。

Result: Qwen3-VL经微调与奖励优化后AP50达0.736（未适配为0.247），自动提示生成（APG）基线为0.773；另两个MLLM也达到或超过APG。监督质心热图检测器AP50为0.904，但Qwen3-VL在标注点集与实例计数上更接近真值。两数据集训练可迁移到未见第三数据集，三数据集训练可迁移到独立EM体数据；未见请求表述下性能稳定，坐标可被第二分割器复用。

Conclusion: 这是首个将开源MLLM作为电镜点生成器的可行性研究，证明语言指令可驱动冻结分割器完成自动分割，提供可检查、语言导向的定位接口，但精度仍低于专用监督检测器，属可行性验证阶段。

Abstract: Promptable models such as microSAM segment electron microscopy (EM) images from point prompts, but automation requires generating prompts without user input. We ask whether open-weight multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can generate them from natural-language requests by returning coordinates to a frozen segmenter. To that end, we convert masks from three mitochondria datasets into training examples, pairing images and instructions with centroid coordinates, then train LoRA adapters while freezing the MLLM backbone and microSAM. We find that Qwen3-VL reaches segmentation AP$_{50}$ $0.736$ after supervised fine-tuning and reward optimization, up from $0.247$ without adaptation, while automatic prompt generation (APG) achieves $0.773$. In addition, two other MLLMs improve, reaching or exceeding APG. When compared with a supervised centroid-heatmap detector that reaches AP$_{50}$ $0.904$ for this mitochondria task, Qwen3-VL more closely matches the annotated point set and instance counts. Moreover, training on two public datasets transfers to an unseen third, while training on all three transfers to an independent EM volume. Robustness tests show stable performance under unseen formulations of the natural-language request, while the coordinates can be reused by a second segmenter. To our knowledge, this is the first feasibility study of open-weight MLLMs as EM point generators, providing an inspectable, language-directed link between localization and mask decoding.

</details>


### [76] [Bridging the Synthetic-to-Real Gap for Few-Shot Cryo-ET Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14097)
*Siddhant Bharadwaj,Ashish Vashist,Rashi Singh,Pranav Vinodh,Nishanth Artham,Runmin Jiang,Xingjian Li,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可学习变换模块的合成到真实域适应框架，在输入与特征两个层面弥合cryo-ET合成与真实子断层图的域差，少样本设置下优于现有迁移学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: cryo-ET子断层图分类受限于标注样本稀缺；虽可用模拟器生成无限合成数据，但合成与真实数据间存在显著域差，直接使用难以奏效。

Method: 设计可学习的变换模块（learnable transformation module），在输入端与特征端同时进行合成到真实的域适应，配合少样本学习框架。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提方法在少样本设置下持续优于现有迁移学习基线。

Conclusion: 通过双层域适应可有效缓解cryo-ET合成数据与真实子断层图之间的域差，为标注稀缺场景下的子断层图分类提供可行方案。

Abstract: Subtomogram classification in cryo-electron tomography (cryo-ET) is a challenging problem due to the scarcity of labeled examples. While cryo-ET simulators can be adopted to generate unlimited synthetic data, the substantial domain gap between synthetic and real subtomograms hinders its practical utilization. In this work, we propose a novel synthetic-to-real adaptation framework with a learnable transformation module, bridging this gap at both the input and feature levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing transfer learning baselines in few-shot settings.

</details>


### [77] [RA-CoA: Training-free Fashion Image Captioning via Retrieval-Augmented Chain-of-Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14100)
*Abhirama Subramanyam Penamakuri,Shreya Shukla,Anand Mishra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RA-CoA 是一个免训练的两阶段框架，先从产品知识库检索相关属性集，再通过属性级推理生成时尚图像描述，在冻结 VLM 上平均提升 26.3% METEOR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚图像描述（FIC）需细粒度视觉推理与领域术语知识，且库存与词汇更新快，需免训练方案；通用指令微调 VLM 虽零样本能力强，但属性覆盖与精度不足，易幻觉或误判细粒度时尚细节。

Method: 提出 RA-CoA（Retrieval-Augmented Chain-of-Attributes），免训练、模型无关：将 FIC 解耦为（i）从产品知识库检索相关属性集，（ii）属性级推理生成最终描述；与冻结 VLM 配合，无需微调。

Result: 在多个 VLM 模型家族、不同提示范式下评估，描述质量显著提升，相较零样本描述平均 METEOR 增益 26.3%，代码已公开。

Conclusion: 免训练检索增强+属性链式推理可有效提升时尚图像描述的细粒度精度，具备可扩展性与实际适配性。

Abstract: Fashion Image Captioning (FIC) plays a vital role in enhancing user experience and product search in e-commerce platforms. Unlike natural scene image captioning, FIC requires fine-grained visual reasoning and knowledge of domain-specific terminology to capture subtle attributes such as neckline and closure types, graphic patterns, and dress silhouettes. Moreover, as fashion inventories evolve rapidly with new trends, styles, and frequently emerging vocabulary, developing training-free captioning solution becomes essential for scalability and real-world adaptability. Instruction-tuned vision-language models (VLMs) offer a promising solution to fashion image captioning dueto their strong zero-shot capabilities and natural language fluency. However, these general-purpose models often lack attribute-level coverage and precision, and tend to hallucinate or misidentify fine-grained fashion details, making them less suitable for high-fidelity applications like product cataloging or personalized recommendations. To address this, we propose RA-CoA (Retrieval-Augmented Chain-of-Attributes), a novel, training-free framework that disentangles fashion image captioning into two interpretable stages: (i) retrieval of relevant attribute sets from a product knowledge base, and (ii) attribute-level reasoning to generate the final caption. RA-CoA is a model-agnostic approach that works with frozen VLMs to improve fine-grained attribute precision in product captions without the need for fine-tuning. Extensive evaluations across diverse VLM model families under different prompting paradigms demonstrate that RA-CoA significantly improves caption quality, achieving an average gain of 26.3% METEOR score over zero-shot captioning. We make our code publicly available.

</details>


### [78] [A Voxel-Spacing-Aware Extension of PyRadiomics for Anisotropic Texture Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14103)
*David Corral Fontecha,Juan Miranda Bautista,Pablo Menendez Fernández-Miranda,Andrea Trapote Fernandez,Lara Lloret Iglesias,Jose A. Vega*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出并验证了一种"体素间距感知"的PyRadiomics扩展，在不对灰度插值的前提下，将各向异性间距信息融入GLCM、NGTDM、GLRLM、GLDM、GLSZM计算，并在标准与各向同性场景下保持与原始PyRadiomics完全一致，各向异性下与常见重采样方法数值上明显不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射组学纹理特征通常从未配准的各向异性CT/MRI体素网格中提取，相同体素偏移对应的物理距离并不一致，导致常规PyRadiomics计算存在几何偏置；而现有做法多依赖最近邻/线性/B样条插值，会引入灰度值失真与信息损失。

Method: 在PyRadiomics中实现跨Python前端、C封装与计算后端的间距感知扩展：GLCM采用各向异性相对的特征级角度聚合，NGTDM采用各向异性相对加权邻域平均，GLRLM/GLDM/GLSZM基于原始各向异性网格导出有限体积零阶保持表示；用合成3D体模进行软件验证，并与最近邻/线性/B样条重采样对比，做了运行时/内存剖析、有限体积取整敏感性分析及不同binWidth下的稳健性检验。

Result: 关闭间距感知模式时与标准PyRadiomics完全一致；在各向同性间距下对75个纹理特征等价；各向异性条件下选择性改变部分纹理族，且数值上区别于三种重采样策略；运行时间与内存有中等程度上升；有限体积取整效应用敏感性分析量化，间距感知差异在不同binWidth设置下持续存在。

Conclusion: 该框架为后续在异构医学影像数据集上评估间距感知放射组学提供了向后兼容的技术基础。

Abstract: Radiomic texture features are commonly extracted from anisotropic CT and MRI acquisitions, where identical voxel offsets may represent different physical distances. We implemented and validated a voxel-spacing-aware extension of PyRadiomics that incorporates spacing information without generating interpolated gray levels. The framework operates across the Python frontend, C wrapper, and computational backend. GLCM uses anisotropy-relative feature-level angular aggregation, NGTDM uses anisotropy-relative weighted neighborhood averaging, and GLRLM, GLDM, and GLSZM are computed on a finite-volume zero-order-hold representation derived from the native anisotropic grid. Synthetic 3D phantoms were used for software validation. The modified implementation reproduced standard PyRadiomics exactly when spacing-aware mode was disabled and remained equivalent under isotropic spacing across 75 texture features. Under anisotropic spacing, the method selectively modified texture families and was numerically distinct from nearest-neighbor, linear, and B-spline resampling. Computational profiling showed moderate runtime and memory increases, while sensitivity analyses quantified finite-volume rounding effects and confirmed that spacing-aware differences persisted across binWidth settings. The framework provides a backward-compatible technical basis for future evaluation of spacing-aware radiomics in heterogeneous medical imaging datasets.

</details>


### [79] [Accelerating HKTex without Mesh Eigensystems: Local Unfolding and Randomized Thermal Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14105)
*Zhewen He,Junyi Hu,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HKTex 用 50 个全局 Laplace-Beltrami 特征分解和 [50,V,256] 常驻基评估各向异性热核表面外观，本文提出两条互补路线消除该瓶颈：LocalHK（半径受限 hinge 展开 + 解析 log-map 核）与 ThermalRF（GPU 稀疏 Chebyshev + 随机 range finding 构造全局低秩热因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HKTex 的内禀各向异性核外观表达依赖 50 个全局 LB 特征分解与形状为 [50,V,256] 的常驻基，初始化与内存开销大，在超大网格上不可行；作者希望在保持训练器、GeodesicOpt、密度控制与合成流程不变的前提下移除该瓶颈。

Method: 两条互补路线：(1) LocalHK 利用已训核的局部性，以半径受限 hinge 展开与解析 log-map 核替代谱评估；(2) ThermalRF 保留离散各向异性热半群，用 GPU 稀疏 Chebyshev 作用与随机 range finding 构造全局低秩热因子而不显式存储网格规模特征向量，并以编译评估器混合 4 个邻域热响应。

Result: LocalHK 在 10 个 Objaverse 网格上平均视角 PSNR 由 31.35 升至 32.00 dB，在 8 网格低模 holdout 上由 29.82 升至 30.76 dB，初始化加速 40.5 倍，并支持 749,570 顶点代理运行（谱基线在此失败）。ThermalRF 在 spot 与 thin-stem 挑战场景上端到端耗时降低 29.3% 与 24.4%，所有表面/atlas/视角 PSNR 变化在 0.12 dB 内，训练显存分配约减少 90%。

Conclusion: 两条路线揭示了"最大局部性与尺度"与"对热 PDE 保真度"之间的设计权衡；更大规模热特征评估与真实大场景仍属未来工作。整体为面向神经表面外观表示的效率与可扩展性改进，缺少下游机器人与真实场景验证。

Abstract: Heat Kernel Textures (HKTex) represent surface appearance with intrinsic anisotropic kernels, but evaluate them using 50 global Laplace-Beltrami eigendecompositions and a resident basis of shape [50,V,256]. We study two complementary ways to remove this bottleneck while leaving the trainer, GeodesicOpt, density control, and compositing unchanged. LocalHK exploits the measured locality of trained kernels and replaces spectral evaluation by radius-bounded hinge unfolding and an analytic log-map kernel. On 10 Objaverse meshes and an 8-mesh low-poly holdout, it changes mean view PSNR from 31.35 to 32.00 and from 29.82 to 30.76 dB, respectively, while reducing initialization by 40.5 times and enabling a 749,570-vertex proxy-backed run where the spectral baseline fails. ThermalRF instead preserves the discrete anisotropic heat semigroup: GPU sparse Chebyshev actions and randomized range finding construct global low-rank heat factors without mesh-sized eigenvectors, and a compiled evaluator mixes four neighboring thermal responses. On spot and a thin-stem challenge, ThermalRF reduces end-to-end preprocessing, initialization, and 5,000-step optimization by 29.3% and 24.4%, with every surface, atlas, or view PSNR change within 0.12 dB and training allocation reduced by about 90%. The two routes expose a useful design choice: maximal locality and scale versus fidelity to the thermal PDE. Broader thermal-feature evaluation and real large scenes remain future work.

</details>


### [80] [Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Foundation Models for Liver Tumor Segmentation in CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14106)
*Ramtin Mojtahedi,Mohammad Hamghalam,Jacob J. Peoples,Richard K. G. Do,Amber L. Simpson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估 SAM 在结直肠癌肝转移 CT 肝肿瘤分割中的参数高效微调，比较 LoRA、QLoRA、Conv-Adapter 与自研 DiSCo，在多种提示（无提示/单点/多点/框）下呈现精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学分割依赖大模型但全量微调成本高、标注数据有限，需要验证 PEFT 在 SAM 上的可行性与性价比。

Method: 冻结 SAM 主干，仅训练适配器：LoRA、4-bit QLoRA、Conv-Adapter，以及 DiSCo（基于行归一化权重的 SVD 谱基、秩门控谱系数、逐输出幅值偏移与谱增益，可选推理期 Top-K 秩选择，0.14M 可训练参数）；在五种提示策略及 IoU 0.50/0.75 下基准测试。

Result: Conv-Adapter 与 LoRA 精度最高（整体 Dice 0.793/0.792，单点 0.795/0.792，HD95 约 32 mm）；QLoRA 接近（Dice 0.766/0.768，HD95 36.41 mm，0.91M 参数，120 ms，4.9 GB）；DiSCo 单位可训练参数 Dice 最高（4.66），整体 Dice 0.653，单点 0.698，HD95 49.53 mm。

Conclusion: 结果体现明确的精度-效率权衡；在算力与标注数据受限时，PEFT 可作为肝肿瘤分割的低成本适配方案。

Abstract: We evaluated parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of the Segment Anything Model (SAM) for liver tumor segmentation in abdominal CT of colorectal liver metastases. We compared Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), 4-bit Quantized LoRA (QLoRA), a convolutional adapter (Conv-Adapter), and our Directional Spectral Top-K adapter (DiSCo), training only adapters while freezing the SAM backbone. DiSCo derives spectral bases from singular value decomposition of row-normalized weights and learns rank-gated spectral coefficients, per-output magnitude offsets, and a spectral gain, with optional Top-K rank selection at inference and 0.14 M trainable parameters. We benchmarked five prompting regimes: no prompt, single-point, multi-point, and bounding boxes at intersection over union 0.50 and 0.75. Conv-Adapter and LoRA achieved the highest accuracy (overall Dice 0.793 and 0.792; single-point Dice 0.795 and 0.792; 95th-percentile Hausdorff distance (HD95) 32 mm). QLoRA was close (overall Dice 0.766; single-point Dice 0.768; HD95 36.41 mm), with 0.91 M trainable parameters, 120 ms latency, and 4.9 GB peak memory. DiSCo achieved the highest Dice per million trainable parameters (4.66), with overall Dice 0.653, single-point Dice 0.698, and HD95 49.53 mm. These results show an accuracy-efficiency trade-off and support PEFT for liver tumor segmentation with reduced adaptation costs when compute and labeled data are limited. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [81] [Talking to Me or Someone Else? Rethinking Talk-to-Me Detection in Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14118)
*Feiyu Du,Xi He,Jia Li,Yapeng Tian,Weili Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将 egocentric "talk-to-me" (TTM) 任务从离线片段级二分类重构为在线帧级多状态预测，并构建约 90 万帧标注数据集及多模态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 TTM 研究多为离线片段级识别，与在线交互不对齐，且忽略了 egocentric 视频中自然存在的多种非 TTM 说话状态（如与他人交谈、自言自语、背景）。

Method: 将 TTM 重新表述为在线帧级预测任务，在多样化非 TTM 状态背景下建模，而非相对单一负类的二分类；扩展 Ego4D social interaction benchmark，构建含 406 个 egocentric 视频片段、约 900K 帧级标注的 Online TTM Dataset；在该 benchmark 上评估 5 个适配基线，并提出融合音频、视觉与语音语义线索的多模态模型。

Result: 多模态模型（联合利用音频、视觉和语音语义线索）在 TTM 上达到 75.5% 帧级 F1，优于强基线，并支持对说话状态如何影响 TTM 识别的系统性分析。

Conclusion: 论文提出了在线帧级 TTM 新范式、配套数据集与多模态方法，为 egocentric 社交交互的在线理解提供了基准与系统分析基础。

Abstract: Online understanding of who is talking to the camera wearer is a key capability for egocentric social interaction. However, existing talk-to-me (TTM) studies are commonly formulated as offline clip-level recognition, which is poorly aligned with online interaction and overlooks the diverse non-TTM speaking states that naturally arise in egocentric videos. In this paper, we revisit this problem by reformulating it as an online, frame-level prediction task. Instead of treating TTM as a binary problem against a single negative class, we model it in the presence of diverse and previously underexplored non-TTM states, such as talking-to-others, self-talking, and background conditions. To support this new formulation, we construct an Online TTM Dataset consisting of 406 egocentric video clips with approximately 900K annotated frames, each labeled with frame-level social interaction categories (e.g., background, TTM, talking-to-others, self-talking), by extending the Ego4D social interaction benchmark. In this benchmark, we evaluate five adapted baselines and develop a new model that integrates social cues across modalities. Experimental results show that our multimodal model, which jointly leverages audio, visual, and speech-semantic cues, achieves 75.5% frame-level F1 on TTM, outperforming strong baselines and enabling a systematic analysis of how different speaking states affect TTM recognition.

</details>


### [82] [SignMimic: Robust High-Quality Sign Language Motion Generation via Human-Shape-Oblivious Pose Transfer Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14122)
*Zhewen He,Junyi Yu,Haomian Huang,Zhenhua Li,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SignMimic将手语视频模仿分解为SE(3)刚性规范化、规范空间非刚性适配与姿态补全，再接条件视频扩散，提升手型与时空一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语视频模仿管线将刚性运动、非刚性形变与视角相关补全纠缠在单一生成器中，导致手型漂移与时空不稳定，需要显式解耦并注入几何与语言学先验。

Method: 提出SignMimic三阶段因子化框架：(i) 基于TNet的SE(3)刚性规范化以稳定全局姿态；(ii) 在规范空间中通过NIF2D进行非刚性适配，保留手/脸等细粒度关节与协同发音；(iii) 在条件视频扩散前使用Pose-MAE风格的补全模块。

Result: 在ASL 50K、How2Sign、CSL News等大规模数据集上，SignMimic在视频质量、身份相似度和帧连续性方面达到SOTA水平，并在生成视频的回译（SLT）上取得最小损失；消融验证了刚性规范化、非刚性适配与补全三部分的有效性。代码、模型权重与视频样例将公开。

Conclusion: 显式的刚性/非刚性/补全因子化结合几何与语言学先验可有效缓解手型漂移与时空不稳定，为身份保持的手语视频模仿提供了可复现的强基线。

Abstract: We study the challenge of sign language video mimicking: given a driving video and a single reference frame, synthesize a video where the target signer reproduces the source motion while preserving identity and linguistic form. Prior pipelines entangle rigid motion, non-rigid deformation, and view-dependent completion in a monolithic generator, causing handshape drift and spatio-temporal instability. We present SignMimic, which (i) applies a TNet-based model to study SE(3) rigid canonicalization to stabilize global pose, (ii) performs non-rigid adaptation in a canonical space to preserve fine-grained articulators (hands/face) and coarticulation via NIF2D, and (iii) uses Pose-MAE-style completion before conditional video diffusion. This factorization injects geometric and linguistic priors, yielding shape and spatio-temporal consistency. On several large-scale datasets (ASL 50K, How2Sign, CSL News), SignMimic achieves state-of-the-art-level performance on video quality, identity similarity, and frame continuity while also achieving minimal loss when performing back translation (SLT) on generated videos. Ablations confirm the role of rigid canonicalization, non-rigid adaptation, and completion. Code, model checkpoints, and video examples will be released.

</details>


### [83] [3D Gait-Based Autism Classification Using Attention-Enhanced Deep Learning with Cross-Fold Statistical Stability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14159)
*Md Nadim Mahamood,Md Arif Shahriar,Md Parvej Sikder,Md Rasul Islam,Md Shafi Ud Doula,Md Ashraful Alam,Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种注意力增强的Transformer框架，用于基于步态的孤独症谱系障碍（ASD）自动筛查，并在两种结构不同的三维步态特征表示（预计算统计描述符与地面反作用力原始测量）上进行了评估，同时补充了逐折稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD早期诊断依赖主观、耗时且需专家参与的临床评估。步态作为非侵入式行为生物标志物具有潜力，但现有研究存在三方面不足：仅在单一数据集上评估、以卷积架构为主、仅对交叉验证性能作描述性汇总而未正式检验折间稳定性。

Method: 以注意力增强的Transformer为分类主干；在两种结构不同的三维步态特征表示上评测：预计算统计步态描述符，以及原始生物力学地面反作用力测量；采用五折交叉验证，并引入折间稳定性（逐折差异）分析。

Result: 公开Kinect基准上五折交叉验证达到99.00%准确率、99.02%精确率、99.00%召回率、99.00% F1与99.00%特异度，优于所比较的现有最先进方法；在独立私有测力板数据集上，均值分别为95.00%准确率、93.81%精确率、96.67%召回率、95.13% F1与93.33%特异度。

Conclusion: 注意力增强Transformer在两类异构三维步态表示上均能实现高精度ASD筛查，且跨数据集性能差异体现出表征与采集方式的影响；折间稳定性分析补充了以往仅报告汇总指标的不足。该方法属于医学诊断类行为生物标志物应用。

Abstract: Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) is a neurodevelopmental condition whose early diagnosis remains challenging because conventional clinical assessments are often subjective, time-consuming, and require expert evaluation. Gait provides a promising non-invasive behavioral biomarker for auto- mated ASD screening; however, existing studies have primarily relied on single-dataset evaluations, convolutional architectures, and descriptive summaries of cross-validation performance without formally assessing fold-to-fold stability. This study addresses these gaps with an attention-enhanced Transformer framework for ASD classification, evaluated on two structurally different 3D gait feature representations: precomputed statistical gait descriptors and raw biomechanical ground-reaction- force measurements. Under five-fold cross-validation, the proposed framework achieved 99.00% accuracy, 99.02% precision, 99.00% recall, 99.00% F1-score, and 99.00% specificity on the public Kinect-based benchmark, exceeding the performance of the compared state-of-the-art methods. On the independent private force-plate dataset, it achieved mean values of 95.00% accuracy, 93.81% precision, 96.67% recall, 95.13% F1-score, and 93.33% specificity.

</details>


### [84] [Bi-Level Routing and Sparse Spatial Attention based Multi-View BEV 3D Object Detection for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14185)
*Jing Zhang,Jiaqi Liu,Zibo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出改进的BEV多视图3D目标检测算法Sparse-BEVNet，通过引入双层路由注意力、级联组注意力和稀疏空间交叉注意力，降低计算量并提升检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV多视图3D目标检测面临计算复杂度高、多尺度特征提取困难以及稠密2D到BEV视图变换效率低等问题。

Method: 在图像特征提取网络中引入双层路由注意力（BRA）机制以减轻骨干网络计算负担；在特征融合模块中采用级联组注意力（CGA）增强不同层级特征的深度交互且不增加额外开销；采用稀疏空间交叉注意力机制替代传统稠密视图投影流程。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上取得45.2% mAP和54.5% NDS，相较基线分别提升3.6%和2.8%。

Conclusion: Sparse-BEVNet通过注意力机制与稀疏投影的协同设计，在精度与效率上均优于基线，验证了稀疏化视图变换在BEV 3D检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Bird's Eye View (BEV)-based multi-view 3D object detection suffers from challenges of computational complexity, multi-scale feature extraction, and efficiency of dense 2D-to-BEV view transformation. To address these problems, this paper proposes an improved BEV 3D object detection algorithm Sparse-BEVNet. Firstly, a Bi-Level Routing Attention (BRA) mechanism is introduced into the image feature extraction network to reduce the computational burden of the backbone. Second, Cascaded Group Attention (CGA) is employed in the feature fusion module, which enhances deep interaction across features of different hierarchical levels without introducing additional computational overhead. Furthermore, a Sparse Spatial Cross-Attention mechanism is adopted to replace the conventional dense view projection pipeline. Experimental results on the public nuScenes dataset demonstrate that the proposed method achieves a mean Average Precision (mAP) of 45.2% and a nuScenes Detection Score (NDS) of 54.5%, corresponding to 3.6% and 2.8% improvements relative to the baseline model, respectively.

</details>


### [85] [MorphoStyle: Motion Style Transfer with Morphology Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14189)
*Xin Feng,Eleonora D'Arnese,Mohan Sridharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MorphoStyle，在形状条件 FSQ-VAE 上通过对比式风格编码、文本引导风格路由与流形保持风格调制，实现形状感知的人体运动风格迁移，兼顾体型控制与风格解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动风格迁移方法假设标准体型，而形状感知生成器又缺乏显式风格控制，二者割裂导致非标准体型难以获得风格化运动；简单拼接风格迁移模块与形状感知生成器会引入风格参考中的动作内容泄漏，并破坏与体型一致的动力学。

Method: 以形状条件化 FSQ-VAE 为骨架，将风格迁移建模为模块化隐空间解耦：(i) 对比式风格编码器提取与内容解耦的风格嵌入；(ii) 文本引导的风格路由机制在文本-运动特征空间定位风格相关关节；(iii) 流形保持风格调制器以时序门控低秩偏移的形式将判别性风格嵌入注入内容特征。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MorphoStyle 在体型控制与运动风格迁移两方面均优于对比基线，并同时提供定量可控的体型调节能力。

Conclusion: 将风格与体型在隐空间显式解耦，并结合文本引导的关节级路由，是解决非标准体型风格化运动生成中内容泄漏与运动学不一致问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Human motion may be viewed as a combination of action content, style, and body morphology. Existing motion style transfer methods transfer a reference style onto a content motion while assuming a canonical body, whereas shape-aware motion generators adapt motion to a target shape without explicit style control. This separation of motion style and shape (morphology) makes it difficult to generate stylized motions for non-canonical bodies; naively combining a style transfer module with a shape-aware generator often leaks action content from the style reference and disrupts shape-consistent kinematics. We present MorphoStyle, a framework for shape-aware motion style transfer that is built on a shape-conditioned Finite-Scalar-Quantization Variational Auto-Encoder (FSQ-VAE). The key contribution is to pose the desired style transfer as modular latent disentanglement comprising: (i) a contrastive style encoder that extracts content-decoupled style embeddings; (ii) a text-guided style-routing mechanism that locates style-relevant joints in a text-motion feature space; and (iii) a manifold preserving style modulator that injects discriminative style embeddings in content features as a temporally-gated low-rank offset. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that MorphoStyle outperforms competing baselines in terms of both shape control and motion style transfer, while simultaneously providing quantitative shape control. For more details, please see project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [86] [What Input Resolution Is Required for Bird Species Identification, and What Is Its Latency Cost on an Edge Device? A Study of 14 Input Resolutions and Six Architectures with On-Device Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14247)
*Takeshi Nishikawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文通过2,520个checkpoint、5,040次评估的大规模析因实验，系统研究鸟类撞击缓解分类器中输入分辨率N（16–224）、架构、训练/评估范式与随机种子对精度与Jetson Orin Nano推理时延的影响，给出四点核心结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电场鸟类撞击缓解需在远端识别仅数十像素的目标，输入分辨率N成为设计变量而非固定规格，但N、架构、精度格式与部署引擎之间的联合权衡缺乏系统量化。

Method: 对14种边长N（16–224）、6种架构、2种训练/评估范式、30个随机种子做析因设计，得到2,520个checkpoint与5,040次评估；在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上测时延，并考察FP16/FP32、图分割等部署因素。

Result: (1) 0.90由ResNet50@N=112达成（估计1.85 ms，测试0.8980），0.95由DINOv2-L@N=144达成（12.70 ms）；换模型比提高N增益更大（+5.93 vs +2.33）。(2) 降N收益依赖预处理路径：224→80在单文件解码下省13.5%，4K整帧一次解码下省46.2%，Pareto集从20增至23。(3) 精度须在部署引擎上测：FP16使ViT-S/16在N≥96损失4–7点而CNN≤0.1点，176个目标中26个选择不同；损坏的FP16引擎可能更快且时延不可辨。(4) ViT-L规模模型激活超出FP16范围，按transformer块边界分割图将FP32限制在受影响段，DINOv2-L链比单引擎快1.85倍。

Conclusion: 输入分辨率、架构与数值格式必须在目标精度、预处理路径和实际部署引擎下联合优化；模型选择比单纯提高N更有效，精度测量须在部署引擎上进行，混合精度图分割可显著加速大规模ViT推理。

Abstract: Bird-strike mitigation at wind farms requires identifying distant birds that span only tens of pixels, so the classifier's input resolution N is a design variable, not a fixed specification. We study it with a factorial design over 14 side lengths N (16 to 224), six architectures, two training and evaluation regimes and 30 random seeds -- 2,520 checkpoints and 5,040 evaluations -- plus latency measured on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano. Four results. (1) The selected N depends on the target: 0.90 is met on validation by ResNet50 at N=112 in an estimated 1.85 ms (0.8980 on test) and 0.95 by DINOv2-L at N=144 in 12.70 ms; changing the model buys more accuracy than raising N (+5.93 versus +2.33 points at N=112). (2) The benefit of lowering N depends on the assumed preprocessing path: N=224 -> 80 saves 13.5% when each individual is decoded from its own file but 46.2% when the detector decodes the 4K frame once; the Pareto set grows from 20 to 23 configurations. (3) Accuracy must be measured on the deployed engine: half precision costs ViT-S/16 alone 4 to 7 points at N>=96 while the CNNs stay within 0.1 points, and with selection held at validation the choice differs at 26 of 176 targets. A broken FP16 engine can run faster than a correct one, undetectable from latency; admitting 14 ViT-S/16 FP32 configurations moves the recommendation over the 0.931-0.938 band and under the 10 ms budget. (4) ViT-L-scale models fit this device, but activations exceed the FP16 range; splitting the graph at transformer-block boundaries confines FP32 to the affected segments, making the deployed DINOv2-L chain 1.85x faster than the single-engine build. We also quantify how the regime-difference sign stabilises with seed count; a sensitivity split removing some forms of group sharing preserves all 14 non-trivial signs at the selection boundary.

</details>


### [87] [SPARK: Representation-Level KV Memory Alignment for Safer Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14258)
*Mohd Azfar,Izhar Dad Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SPARK 两阶段框架，在预填充阶段直接修复多模态 KV 记忆以抵御跨模态越狱，通过诊断适配器定位有害方向、投影剔除并学习轻量残差修复，并用图像结构先验锚定键表示，按头级子空间能量自适应混合修复/原始记忆，在四个 VLM 上降低多模态攻击成功率且保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态越狱攻击将有害意图分散在文本与图像中，单模态安全机制难以覆盖；本文希望在不修改推理期模型参数的前提下，直接在预填充阶段形成的多模态 KV 记忆中进行干预，以缓解此类脆弱性。

Method: SPARK 为两阶段 KV 记忆修复框架。阶段一：用一次性的诊断适配器识别多模态 key/value 表示中与危害相关的方向。阶段二：将这些方向投影剔除，学习轻量残差修复，并以图像结构先验锚定修复后的键以保持视觉落地。干预不做统一施加，而是依据与干预相关的子空间能量 E_h 计算头级系数 g_h*，对修复记忆与原始记忆进行混合，推理时无需显式有害分类器。

Result: 在 LLaVA-OneVision-7B、Chameleon-7B、Qwen2-VL-7B、InternVL2-4B 上，SPARK 降低多模态攻击成功率并保持通用能力。LLaVA-OneVision-7B 上纯图像越狱攻击成功率降至 4.7%，MMMU 仅下降 0.6 分（47.8 vs 48.4），语言质量接近基线；MM-SafetyBench 上攻击成功率由 39.2% 降至 12.4%；白盒自适应联合提示-图像攻击下攻击成功率限制在 20.3%（未防御模型为 54.6%）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在多模态提示的下述证据由视觉模态承载时，通过在预填充阶段选择性修复与干预相关的 KV 子空间，可显著缓解多模态越狱行为，且无需推理期参数修改与显式分类器。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreaks that distribute harmful intent across text and images, making unimodal safety mechanisms insufficient. We investigate whether this vulnerability can be mitigated directly in the multimodal key-value (KV) memory formed during prefill, without modifying model parameters at inference time. We introduce SPARK, a two-stage framework for targeted KV-memory repair. Stage 1 uses a disposable diagnostic adapter to identify harm-associated directions in multimodal key and value representations. Stage 2 projects out these directions, learns a lightweight residual repair, and anchors repaired keys with an image-structural prior to preserve visual grounding. Rather than applying the intervention uniformly, SPARK mixes repaired and original memory using a head-wise coefficient g_h* determined by intervention-relevant subspace energy E_h, requiring no explicit harm classifier at inference. Across LLaVA-OneVision-7B, Chameleon-7B, Qwen2-VL-7B, and InternVL2-4B, SPARK reduces multimodal attack success while preserving general capability. On LLaVA-OneVision-7B, image-only jailbreak attack success falls to 4.7%, while MMMU remains within 0.6 points of the undefended model (47.8 vs. 48.4) with near-baseline language quality. On MM-SafetyBench, attack success decreases from 39.2% to 12.4%. Even under white-box adaptive joint prompt-image attacks, attack success is limited to 20.3%, compared with 54.6% for the undefended model. These results suggest that multimodal jailbreak behavior can be substantially mitigated by selectively repairing intervention-relevant KV subspaces at prefill, particularly when harmful evidence is carried by the visual modality.

</details>


### [88] [Sparsity-Adaptive Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14274)
*Shiryu Ueno,Yoshikazu Hayashi,Kunihito Kato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出稀疏自适应锐度感知最小化(SA-SAM)，通过保持平均绝对扰动近似不变来导出与稀疏度相关的扰动半径，另评估幅度加权Hessian(MWH)重要性准则，在多数据集高稀疏度下提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署场景要求模型紧凑且鲁棒，但高稀疏度下标准剪枝流程会损害corruption鲁棒性，现有锐度感知训练/剪枝方法带来的鲁棒性收益有限。

Method: SA-SAM：随稀疏度增大，保持基于ℓ1的均值绝对扰动近似不变，从而推导依赖稀疏度的SAM/ASAM扰动半径；作为补充选项，从二阶去除路径分析导出Magnitude-Weighted Hessian(MWH)，重要性正比于Diag(F)_i·|w_i|，其中Diag(F)为对角经验Fisher曲率代理。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C、CIFAR-100-C、ImageNet-100-C上，80–90%稀疏度时corruption鲁棒性优于所比较的剪枝基线，同时保持干净准确率；并报告了部署相关稀疏度下实测推理吞吐量以量化鲁棒性-吞吐权衡。

Conclusion: 通过稀疏度自适应的扰动半径设计，可在高稀疏度部署条件下同时维持紧凑性、干净精度与corruption鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deploying deep neural networks in real-world settings requires models that are both compact and robust to common corruptions. However, at deployment-relevant high sparsity, standard pruning pipelines often degrade corruption robustness, and existing sharpness-aware training/pruning approaches provide limited robustness gains. We address this issue by introducing Sparsity-Adaptive Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SA-SAM), which derives a sparsity-dependent SAM/ASAM perturbation radius by keeping the mean absolute perturbation (an $\ell_1$-based proxy) approximately invariant as sparsity increases. As a simple complementary option, we evaluate Magnitude-Weighted Hessian (MWH), derived from a second-order removal-path analysis, yielding an importance proportional to $\mathrm{Diag}(F)_i\,|w_i|$, where $\mathrm{Diag}(F)$ is the diagonal empirical Fisher used as a curvature proxy in our implementation. Across CIFAR-10-C, CIFAR-100-C, and ImageNet-100-C, our approach achieved stronger corruption robustness than the considered pruning baselines at 80--90\% sparsity, while preserving clean accuracy. We additionally quantify the robustness--throughput trade-off by reporting measured inference throughput under sparse execution at deployment-relevant sparsity levels.

</details>


### [89] [SpermYOLO: A Coordinated YOLO-Based Detector for Accurate and Efficient Sperm and Impurity Detection in Microscopic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14278)
*Shengqi Chen,Zilin Wang,Xingyu Pan,Wenting Yu,Pengchao Deng,Guohua Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SpermYOLO——基于 YOLOv11 的紧凑协同检测框架，通过 C3k2-IDB、D2SEM、MFM、DESD Head 四个模块，在显微图像中联合检测精子与杂质，SVIA 上精子 AP 97.2%、杂质 AP 75.4%，跨场景 SDTB 上 mAP50 74.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显微精子图像存在目标密集、视觉相似伪影与类精子杂质，通用检测器与专用精子检测模型精度不足；同时需兼顾轻量化以适配计算机辅助精液分析场景。

Method: 以 YOLOv11 为基线，引入四项改进：C3k2-IDB 通道级判别特征提取、D2SEM 空间—光谱语义增强、MFM 自适应多尺度特征融合、DESD Head 细节增强共享预测头，实现精子与杂质的联合检测。

Result: SVIA 基准：精子 AP 97.2%、杂质 AP 75.4%，较基线分别提升 1.6、10.0 个百分点，mAP50 与 mAP50:95 提升 5.8、2.7 个百分点，且保持轻量规模；SDTB 睾丸活检跨场景评估中，在极小目标与复杂组织背景下取得最高 mAP50 74.8%、mAP50:95 31.2%。消融实验与定性分析验证各模块贡献。

Conclusion: SpermYOLO 在困难显微成像场景下兼具有效性与效率，可作为精子检测的实用方案；但工作属医学显微图像领域 CV，未涉及机器人、3D 重建或具身智能等核心方向。

Abstract: Accurate sperm detection is essential for computer-assisted semen analysis, yet it remains challenging in microscopic images due to dense distributions, visually similar artifacts, and sperm-like impurities. In this paper, we propose SpermYOLO, a coordinated and compact YOLOv11-derived framework for joint sperm and impurity detection in microscopic images. SpermYOLO introduces four architectural improvements: C3k2-IDB for channel-wise discriminative feature extraction, D2SEM for spatial--spectral semantic enhancement, MFM for adaptive multi-scale feature fusion, and the DESD Head for detail-enhanced shared prediction. Experiments on the SVIA semen microscopic imaging benchmark show that SpermYOLO achieves 97.2\% sperm AP and 75.4\% impurity AP, outperforming generic detectors, dedicated sperm detection models, and improved YOLO variants. Compared with the baseline model, SpermYOLO improves sperm AP, impurity AP, $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$, and $\mathrm{mAP}_{50:95}$ by 1.6, 10.0, 5.8, and 2.7 percentage points, respectively, while preserving a lightweight model scale. Cross-scene evaluation on the SDTB testicular-biopsy microscopy benchmark shows that SpermYOLO remains effective with extremely small sperm targets and complex tissue backgrounds, achieving the highest $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ and $\mathrm{mAP}_{50:95}$ of 74.8\% and 31.2\%, respectively. Ablation studies and qualitative analyses further support these improvements by demonstrating the contributions of the proposed modules and showing more focused feature response patterns than the baseline model. These findings suggest that SpermYOLO is an effective and efficient approach for sperm detection in challenging microscopic imaging scenarios.

</details>


### [90] [Vision-Language Models for Criterion-Level Grading of Handwritten Examinations in Outcome-Based Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14284)
*Md Khalid Syfullah,Asif Hasan Tonmoy,Saad Ahmed,S. M. Jahangir Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了20种视觉语言模型配置在手写考试答案的准则级评分任务上的表现，发现Qwen2.5-VL结合LoRA达到最佳QWK（0.727），优于人工评分者之间的一致性（0.551），但聚合可靠性与逐项重复性之间存在明显分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准则级评分（criterion-level grading）能将考试表现与学习成果关联，但人工评分存在工作量大和评分者间差异的问题。研究希望验证视觉语言模型能否用于手写答卷的成果导向评估，并系统刻画其准确性、与人工一致性、重复运行可靠性、错误分布及解释质量等多个维度。

Method: 使用485份本科生考试答案中提取的1,982条准则级记录，比较20种配置，涵盖Qwen2.5-VL、InternVL3、Pixtral、Donut基线以及级联集成。评估设置包括零样本提示、少样本提示、部分微调与LoRA。两名独立教师对所有291条测试准则重新评分以提供人工一致性基线；另采用注意力引导删除与随机掩蔽对照，并由四名教师评审模型解释的有用性。

Result: Qwen2.5-VL配合LoRA取得QWK 0.727、平均绝对误差0.435分，而人工评分者对的平均QWK为0.551。LoRA在所有三个指令微调的VLM上均优于部分微调；少样本提示在所有有有效提示分数的配置中均降低QWK。聚合可靠性（组内相关系数0.790–0.874）与精确重复性（50.2–63.6%的准则在五次采样中改变分数）出现分歧。注意力引导删除相对随机掩蔽无显著优势，四名教师对解释有用性也未达成共识。

Conclusion: VLM可用于准则级评分，但需要针对评分细则的校准、可重复的评分流程、对高风险错误的复核，以及对解释质量的独立验证。所发布的评估协议支持准则级评估研究与带教师监督的评分工具开发。

Abstract: Criterion-level grading connects examination performance to learning outcomes, but manual marking introduces workload and variation between markers. This study evaluates vision-language models (VLMs) for handwritten outcome-based assessment across five dimensions: accuracy, human agreement, repeated-run reliability, error concentration, and explanation quality. Using 1,982 criterion-level records from 485 undergraduate examination answers, we compare 20 configurations spanning Qwen2.5-VL, InternVL3, Pixtral, a Donut baseline, and a cascade ensemble. Evaluation setups include zero-shot prompting, few-shot prompting, partial fine-tuning, and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). Two independent faculty markers regraded all 291 test criteria, providing a human agreement baseline on the same assessment materials. Qwen2.5-VL with LoRA achieved Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) of 0.727 and mean absolute error of 0.435 marks against the examiner, compared with mean human-pair QWK of 0.551. This comparison reflects calibration to the examiner's training marks. LoRA outperformed partial fine-tuning for all three instruction-tuned VLMs, while few-shot prompting reduced QWK in every configuration with valid prompted scores. Aggregate reliability and exact repeatability diverged: intraclass correlations ranged from 0.790 to 0.874, yet 50.2-63.6% of criteria changed marks across five sampled runs. Attention-guided deletion showed no statistically significant advantage over random masking, and four faculty reviewers reached no consensus on explanation usefulness. These findings highlight the need for rubric-specific calibration, repeatable scoring, review of consequential errors, and separate validation of explanations. The released evaluation protocol supports criterion-level assessment research and grading tools with teacher oversight.

</details>


### [91] [Robust low-rank tensor completion via factorized weighted tensor schatten-p norm minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14307)
*Binghao Wang,Feng Zhang,Wendong Wang,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两种加权Schatten-p张量分解模型(WSpTFI/WSpTFII)，在t-product框架下实现分量特异的加权正则，避免昂贵SVD并支持秩分量剪枝，配以IRew-ADMM与IRLS-BSUM算法及KKT/收敛性分析，在多类张量补全任务上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩张量分解是处理不完整、被污染多维数据的有力框架，但现有方法存在两个缺陷：（1）无加权谱正则对奇异分量施加统一收缩，会过度衰减主导低秩分量；（2）分解式变体要么缺乏分量级加权，要么需反复进行代价高昂的SVD。因此需要在保持低计算开销的同时引入分量特异的加权收缩。

Method: 在t-product张量框架下构造两个加权Schatten-p张量分解模型：WSpTFI基于因子化加权张量Schatten-p范数恒等式，允许灵活（可非对称）的因子指数；WSpTFII由变换域列对能量构造正则项，实现无SVD的主因子更新，并附带列剪枝机制以削减冗余秩分量。优化上分别采用迭代重加权ADMM方案与IRLS-BSUM方案，并从理论上建立加权因子化关系与条件极限KKT刻画，以及固定维度尾部的渐近正则性与驻点性质。

Result: 理论层面给出了WSpTFI的加权因子化恒等式及条件极限KKT表征；对WSpTFII证明了阻尼二次块更新对固定δ平滑目标具有定量充分下降机制，进而得到渐近正则性，且尾部每个聚点均为驻点。实验上在合成张量补全、彩色图像恢复、高光谱修复与印刷电路板缺陷检测四类任务中，于多种退化条件下取得有竞争力的重建质量与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过分量级加权Schatten-p正则与无SVD更新，所提两种模型在精度与效率间取得更好折衷，具备可解释的优化理论支撑，适用于一般多维数据的低秩补全与恢复任务。其应用域属通用张量优化与图像/信号恢复，未涉及具身智能、机器人操作或三维重建等核心方向。

Abstract: Low-rank tensor factorization provides a flexible framework for completing multidimensional data from incomplete and corrupted observations. However, unweighted spectral regularizers impose a common shrinkage profile across singular components, which may excessively attenuate dominant low-rank components, and factorized variants either lack component-specific weighting or require costly singular value decompositions (SVDs). This paper proposes two weighted Schatten-$p$ tensor factorization models, termed \WSpTFI{} and \WSpTFII{}, under the tensor-tensor product (t-product) framework to address these limitations. \WSpTFI{} is motivated by a factorized weighted tensor Schatten-$p$ norm identity and permits flexible, possibly asymmetric factor exponents. \WSpTFII{} constructs a regularizer from transform-domain column-pair energies, yielding SVD-free main factor updates and a column-pruning mechanism for reducing redundant rank components. This paper further develops an iteratively reweighted alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM)-type scheme for \WSpTFI{} and an iteratively reweighted least squares (IRLS)--block successive upper-bound minimization (BSUM) scheme for \WSpTFII{}. Theoretical analysis establishes the weighted factorization relation and provides a conditional limiting Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) characterization for \WSpTFI{}. For \WSpTFII{}, the actual damped quadratic block updates yield a quantitative sufficient-decrease mechanism for the fixed-$\delta$ smoothed factor objective. This implies asymptotic regularity, and every accumulation point of the fixed-dimensional tail is stationary. Experiments on synthetic tensor completion, color-image restoration, hyperspectral inpainting, and printed-circuit-board defect detection demonstrate competitive reconstruction quality and robustness under various degradation conditions.

</details>


### [92] [DTI-Guided Volumetric Spherical Harmonics Regression for Single-to-Multi-Shell dMRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14312)
*Binghua Li,Christina Andica,Tong Liang,Ziqing Chang,Chao Li,Wataru Uchida,Kaito Takabayashi,Qibin Zhao,Toshihisa Tanaka,Zhe Sun,Shigeki Aoki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DTI-SHNet，在实对称球谐系数域内用3D U-Net实现单壳到多壳dMRI合成，以DTI方向无关参数图与脑掩膜为先验，并引入信号一致性正则，UK Biobank与Cam-CAN上视觉质量具竞争力且下游扩散指标保持更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多壳dMRI微结构建模更强，但采集时间长，难以用于大队列与时间受限的临床；从单壳合成未观测壳本质上是病态问题，且源与目标梯度方向集不匹配造成协议失配，进一步加大难度。

Method: 在实对称球谐（SH）系数域做空间感知的体回归：由源壳估计DTI，取方向无关参数图与脑掩膜作为条件先验，引导3D U-Net从源壳SH系数回归目标壳SH系数；并加入信号一致性正则，在随机采样的规范方向上由预测系数重建信号并在信号域施加一致性约束。

Result: 在UK Biobank与Cam-CAN数据上进行b=1000到b=2000的dMRI合成，DTI-SHNet视觉质量与先进方法相比具竞争力，同时更好地保持下游扩散度量。

Conclusion: 在SH系数域做单壳到多壳合成可缓解协议失配与病态性，兼顾系数精度与信号保真，为大规模队列和临床场景提供低成本多壳dMRI替代方案。

Abstract: Multi-shell diffusion MRI (dMRI) unlocks more expressive microstructural modeling than single-shell scans, yet its longer acquisition time hinders deployment in large-scale cohorts and time-constrained clinical settings. Synthesizing an unobserved shell from a single-shell input is fundamentally ill-posed and further complicated by protocol mismatch, where source and target gradient direction sets may not align. We propose DTI-SHNet, a single-to-multi-shell synthesis framework that operates in the real symmetric spherical harmonics (SH) coefficient domain and performs spatially aware volumetric regression. Given a source shell, we estimate diffusion tensor imaging (DTI) and use direction-agnostic parametric maps along with a brain mask as conditioning priors to guide a 3D U-Net regressor from source-shell to target-shell SH coefficients. To couple coefficient accuracy with signal fidelity, we introduce a signal consistency regularization that reconstructs signals on randomly sampled canonical directions from predicted coefficients and enforces agreement in the signal domain. Experiments on UK Biobank and Cam-CAN data for b=1000 to b=2000 dMRI synthesis show that DTI-SHNet achieves competitive visual quality compared to advanced methods, while better preserving downstream diffusion measures. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [93] [S3-Tracker: Self-Supervised Surgical Tissue Tracking With Contrastive Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14313)
*Jiaming Zhang,Zijian Wu,Mehran Armand,Septimiu Salcudean*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自监督Track-Any-Point方法，利用无标注内窥镜视频，通过全局像素对应与对比随机游走学习点轨迹，性能接近半监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜视频中的鲁棒点跟踪对计算机辅助干预和自主机器人手术至关重要，可实现术中视频与术前影像的持续配准。但监督跟踪依赖大规模标注数据，而手术条件下可靠轨迹标注极为困难。

Method: 自监督Track-Any-Point方法：在无标注手术视频上建立全局像素对应关系，通过对比随机游走（contrastive random walks）推断点轨迹，无需任何标注即可训练，并隐式处理软组织形变。

Result: 无标注训练下，性能与现有半监督方法相当，并能隐式处理组织形变。

Conclusion: 证明了自监督点跟踪在手术环境中的可行性，有望减少对标注数据的依赖。

Abstract: Robust point tracking in endoscopic videos is essential for computer-assisted intervention and autonomous robotic surgery, enabling continuous registration between intraoperative video and preoperative imaging despite soft tissue deformation. However, supervised tracking methods depend on large annotated datasets, while surgical conditions make reliable trajectory annotation challenging. We propose a self-supervised Track-Any-Point approach that learns from unlabeled surgical videos by establishing global pixel correspondences and inferring point trajectories through contrastive random walks. Trained without annotations, our method achieves performance comparable to existing semi-supervised approaches while implicitly handling tissue deformation. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of self-supervised point tracking in surgical environments and its potential to reduce reliance on annotated data.

</details>


### [94] [Learning Continuous Source Responses For Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14316)
*Manni Cui,Ruiqi Liu,Zijian Yu,Hao Tan,Zibo Wei,Zian Wang,Ziheng Qin,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jun Lan,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CuRe框架，将AI生成图像检测的训练任务从二分类重构为"真实-生成混合比回归"，并选取紧凑源响应子空间以抑制捷径线索，在10个公开基准上平均平衡准确率89.7%，超出次优方法5.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器在域内表现良好，但跨生成器泛化与鲁棒性有限；普遍归因于对捷径线索的过拟合。已有抑制捷径的方法多数仍沿用二分类训练目标，未反思任务本身如何塑造表征。

Method: CuRe将主干适配重表述为回归真实-生成混合比例（连续源响应），提供比二分类端点分离更细粒度的监督，促使模型捕捉真实性相关变化；随后选取紧凑的源响应子空间以压制无关扰动变异，并限制最终分类器接触潜在捷径线索。

Result: 在十个公开基准上平均平衡准确率达89.7%，比第二名高5.2个百分点；在多种视觉主干上取得一致的泛化提升，并对常见图像退化表现出较强鲁棒性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 从训练任务设计的视角重新审视真实性检测，用连续混合比回归替代二分类可有效缓解捷径学习、提升跨生成器泛化能力，为AI生成图像取证提供了新的任务级范式。

Abstract: Advances in image generation have made synthetic images increasingly difficult to distinguish from real photographs, raising concerns about the trustworthiness of visual media. Existing AI-generated image detectors often perform well on in-domain data, but their robustness and cross-generator generalization remain limited. These limitations are commonly attributed to overfitting to shortcut cues. Although many methods seek to suppress shortcut learning, most retain binary classification as the training task without reconsidering how the task itself shapes the learned representations. We introduce CuRe, a framework for learning Continuous Source Responses that revisits authenticity detection from the perspective of the training task. CuRe reformulates backbone adaptation as regression of real-generated mixing ratios, providing finer supervision that encourages the model to capture authenticity-related variation beyond binary endpoint separation. We further select a compact source-response subspace to suppress nuisance variation and limit the final classifier's access to potential shortcut cues. Across ten public benchmarks, CuRe achieves an average balanced accuracy of 89.7%, exceeding the second-best method by 5.2 percentage points. Further experiments demonstrate consistent generalization gains across visual backbones and strong robustness to common image degradations. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [95] [Mobile CT Services for Rural, Regional, and Remote Areas: Current Practice and Future Integration with Telehealth and Regulatory-Authorised AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14347)
*Zhicheng Lu,Md Zahid Islam,M Mamun Huda,Kristie Sweeney,Shayne Chau,Oliver Mulcock,Corey Hemopo,Catherine Keniry,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了移动CT、远程医疗与AI辅助CT三类技术在神经、胸部、心血管、腹部、肿瘤、肌骨及介入影像中的应用，指出三者各自较成熟但整合部署与验证仍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农村、偏远及资源受限地区对CT影像获取与专家解读的可及性严重不足；移动CT、远程医疗与AI的进步为将高级影像服务延伸至这些地区提供了机会，但缺乏对三者整合现状与瓶颈的系统评估。

Method: 文献综述：1) 部署于卡车、拖车、救护车等平台的移动CT系统；2) 支持CT诊疗的远程医疗技术；3) 已获监管授权或已临床部署的CT人工智能。按筛查诊断、患者监测、风险预测、干预/治疗决策支持四类临床功能，覆盖神经、胸、心血管、腹部、肿瘤、肌骨与介入影像，并比较监管状态、部署状态与估计技术成熟度(TRL)。

Result: 移动CT、远程医疗与常规CT的AI各自相对成熟，但三者的完全整合在临床场景中的部署与验证仍不广泛。主要障碍包括监管差异、域偏移(domain shift)、连接性要求、成本、工作流整合、网络安全及患者层面获益证据不足。

Conclusion: 未来研究应优先开展整合式CT系统在真实世界与欠服务临床环境中的前瞻性、多中心评估。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) plays an essential role in clinical workflow to improve patient outcomes. However, access to CT imaging and specialist interpretation remains limited, particularly in rural, regional, remote (RRR), and other resource-limited settings. Recent advances in mobile CT, telehealth, and artificial intelligence (AI) provide opportunities to extend advanced imaging services to populations in RRR settings. This review examines: 1) mobile CT systems deployed in trucks, trailers, ambulances, and other mobile platforms; 2) telehealth technologies supporting CT-based healthcare; and 3) AI for CT that has received regulatory authorisation or is currently deployed in clinical practice. Applications are evaluated across four clinical functions: screening and diagnosis, patient monitoring, risk prediction, and intervention or therapeutic decision support. The review covers neurological, thoracic, cardiovascular, abdominal, oncological, musculoskeletal, and interventional imaging, with particular attention to stroke, cancer, and other image-guided treatment. Other factors such as regulatory status, deployment status, and estimated technology readiness (TRL) level are compared. Current evidence indicates that mobile CT, telehealth, and AI for conventional CT are individually relatively mature, but fully integration of these technologies remains less widely deployed and validated in the clinical settings. Key barriers include regulatory variation, domain shift, connectivity requirements, cost, workflow integration, cybersecurity, and limited evidence of patient-level benefit. Future research should prioritise prospective, multicentre evaluation of integrated CT systems in real-world and underserved clinical settings.

</details>


### [96] [Two-Stage Mixture-of-LoRA for Multi-Task Medical Vision-Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14350)
*Zhanghao Chen,Yuanyuan Li,Zhenyu Lu,Shuo Gao,Guangquan Zhou,Yikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Two-Stage Mixture-of-LoRA框架（基于MedGemma-1.5-4B），用共享LoRA+6个任务专家LoRA与两阶段训练缓解异构输出、梯度冲突和数据不均衡，在FLARE 2026 Task 3取得分类0.85、多标签micro-F1 0.48、检测F1 0.79、回归MAE 17.39。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉-语言模型需单模型覆盖诊断分类到报告生成等多任务，但联合适配面临输出格式异构、任务梯度冲突与训练数据不均衡三大障碍，直接联合微调效果受限。

Method: 共享-专用Mixture-of-LoRA：1个共享LoRA+6个任务专家LoRA。Stage 1在所有任务上联合训练共享与全部专家LoRA；Stage 2冻结主干、共享LoRA与非目标专家，逐任务精修单一专家；分类与回归额外进行模态均衡续训，重复小模态组样本以匹配最大组的采样量。

Result: FLARE 2026 Task 3测试集：分类平衡准确率0.85，多标签分类micro-F1 0.48，检测F1 0.79，回归MAE 17.39。

Conclusion: 两阶段共享-专用LoRA解耦可有效缓解多任务医学VLM适配中的梯度冲突与数据失衡，是参数高效的多任务适配方案；但属于医学领域CV，与通用具身/物理AI方向关联有限。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) allow a single model to perform clinical image analysis tasks ranging from diagnosis classification to report generation. However, joint adaptation is challenged by heterogeneous output formats, conflicting task gradients, and imbalanced training data. Hence, we present \textbf{Two-Stage Mixture-of-LoRA}, a framework built on MedGemma-1.5-4B. The framework uses a shared-specific Mixture-of-LoRA architecture comprising one shared LoRA and six task-specific expert LoRAs, together with a two-stage training procedure. In Stage 1, we jointly train the shared LoRA and all task-specific expert LoRAs on all tasks. In Stage 2, we first freeze the backbone, the shared LoRA, and all non-target experts, and refine one task expert at a time. Classification and regression then receive an additional modality-balanced continuation, in which smaller modality groups are repeated to match the largest group. In the FLARE 2026 Task 3 test sets, the proposed method achieves 0.85 balanced accuracy for classification, 0.48 micro-F1 for multi-label classification, 0.79 detection F1, and 17.39 regression MAE. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [97] [Beyond Natural Images: Rethinking AI-Generated Image Detection in Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14352)
*Zhangjie Fu,Jiazhen Yan,Yuanwen Chen,Xinquan Yu,Yanzhe Li,Hui Jiang,Lei Gao,Chenfu Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对AI生成文档图像检测被忽视的问题，构建了诊断基准AIGDoc-Pilot与更大规模数据集AIGDoc，揭示现有检测器在AI生成文档上AUC平均下降超7%，并从文档特有的空间不一致性与文本密度效应两方面解释原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测评测主要集中于自然图像，而文档（发票、报销单、证书、病历等）常出现于敏感真实场景，其AI生成版本检测严重缺乏研究，存在安全风险。

Method: 先构建受控诊断基准AIGDoc-Pilot以定量暴露性能差距；分析发现两个文档特有属性——生成伪影在局部区域呈强空间不一致性、文本密度显著影响真伪可分性（文本密集区域判别证据更强）；据此构建由多种先进生成与编辑模型产出、覆盖多样真实文档的更大规模数据集AIGDoc，并开展检测器评测与文档域训练实验。

Result: 现有检测器在AI生成文档图像上性能显著退化，AUC均值下降超过7%；在AIGDoc上，现有检测器仍难以可靠识别AI生成文档，而基于文档数据的训练可部分缩小差距。

Conclusion: AI生成文档图像检测是一个被低估且具现实意义的方向；空间不一致性与文本密度是可利用的文档特有线索，文档域数据与训练有助于提升检测器的可靠性与泛化性，代码与数据集将在论文接收后公开。

Abstract: AI-generated image detection has attracted increasing attention, but existing evaluations mainly focus on natural images, leaving AI-generated document images largely underexplored. This omission is concerning because documents often appear in sensitive real-world scenarios, such as invoices, expense reports, certificates, and medical records. In this paper, we first construct a controlled diagnostic benchmark, AIGDoc-Pilot, and reveal that existing detectors suffer substantial performance degradation on AI-generated document images, with the mean AUC dropping by more than 7%. Based on this, we further reveal two document-specific properties behind this gap: generation artifacts exhibit strong spatial inconsistency across local regions, and text density significantly affects real-synthetic separability, where text-dense regions offer stronger discriminative evidence. Motivated by these findings, we construct AIGDoc, a larger document-centric dataset containing diverse real-world documents and AI-generated counterparts produced by multiple advanced generation and editing models. Extensive experiments on AIGDoc demonstrate that existing detectors still struggle to reliably identify AI-generated documents, while document-based training partially narrows the gap. Together, these results offer valuable insights for developing dependable and generalizable detectors in document-centric scenarios. The code and datasets will be made publicly available upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [98] [Rethinking Camouflage Image Generation towards a Training-Free Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14377)
*Haodong Yang,Zhongling Huang,Gong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练伪装图像生成范式 FreeCam，基于冻结的 inpainting 扩散模型与多模态先验、内在外观线索，实现目标保持、语义兼容与外观同化的联合优化，无需任务特定训练即达 SOTA 伪装效果，并可作伪装目标检测的合成监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪装图像生成方法依赖在伪装数据集上的任务特定训练，计算开销大且泛化性受限。作者希望以无参数更新的免训练方式同时满足三个耦合需求：前景保持（目标完整性）、语义兼容（合理藏匿背景）、外观同化（降低视觉差异）。

Method: 将免训练 CIG 建模为"以藏匿为导向"的范式：不再维持目标的视觉显著性，而是降低其与合成背景的感知可分性。实例化为 FreeCam：冻结的 inpainting 扩散框架保持前景；Contextual Reasoning Module 利用冻结的多模态先验推断利于藏匿的环境以提升语义兼容；Intrinsic Appearance Module 从前景提取低层颜色/纹理线索引导背景合成实现外观同化。

Result: 大量实验表明 FreeCam 无需任务特定训练即取得 SOTA 生成质量与伪装有效性；生成图像可为伪装目标检测提供合成监督，并降低目标在通用目标检测器下的可检测性。

Conclusion: 免训练、无参数更新的伪装生成范式可行且有效，兼顾前景保持、语义兼容与外观同化，可同时服务伪装生成与检测的合成数据需求。

Abstract: Camouflage image generation (CIG) aims to synthesize realistic camouflaged images by blending foreground objects into concealment-compatible background contexts. Achieving this objective requires jointly satisfying three coupled requirements: foreground preservation to retain target integrity, semantic compatibility to select plausible concealment contexts, and appearance assimilation to reduce visual discrepancies. Recent approaches predominantly rely on task-specific training on camouflage datasets to address these requirements, incurring substantial computational cost and limiting generalization beyond the training domain. To address these limitations, we formulate training-free CIG as a concealment-oriented paradigm that preserves the target while reducing its perceptual separability from the synthesized surroundings, rather than maintaining its visual prominence, without parameter updates. We instantiate this paradigm with FreeCam based on a frozen inpainting diffusion framework to preserve the foreground. Within this framework, a Contextual Reasoning Module exploits frozen multimodal priors to infer an environment favorable to concealment, thereby promoting semantic compatibility, while an Intrinsic Appearance Module extracts low-level color and texture cues from the foreground to guide background synthesis toward appearance assimilation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FreeCam achieves state-of-the-art generation quality and camouflage effectiveness without task-specific training, while its generated images provide synthetic supervision for camouflaged object detection and reduce target detectability under general object detectors.

</details>


### [99] [Contour-Guided Spectral Routing for Robust Real-Time Pedestrian Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14383)
*Sam Williams,Yuan Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"轮廓引导频谱路由"统一视角的行人检测器：按空间→频谱→边界语义分歧的顺序路由信息，并配合小波子带训练增强，在 CityPersons 上取得 70.4/44.2 与 71.1/44.6 的 AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶场景实时行人检测受三类耦合失效模式制约：小目标判别性证据不足、遮挡削弱几何支撑、天气与光照变化扭曲外观统计量。作者认为频域处理、注意力与边界推理不应作为互相独立的附加模块，而应统一到一个显式的路由框架中。

Method: 以"轮廓引导频谱路由"统一建模，按规定顺序路由信息：先为空间证据注入全局频谱上下文，再令深层表征交换空间与频谱线索，最后以边界—语义分歧为条件进行跨尺度融合。低频谱上下文稳定全局结构，高频证据保护小目标轮廓。同时保留小波子带训练变换，独立扰动低频与高频系数以模拟雾、雨、雪、低照度造成的表观漂移。每一阶段仅暴露一个路由变量，从而区分可复用的信号变换与决定信号注入位置的任务特定策略。

Result: CityPersons 上达到 70.4 AP50 与 44.2 AP50:95，对比 RT-DETR 的 68.1 与 42.2；加入完整小波增强后进一步提升至 71.1 与 44.6。

Conclusion: 将频域、注意力与边界推理统一为有序的频谱路由管线，可在不显著增加复杂度的前提下同时缓解小目标、遮挡与外观漂移问题，并通过小波子带增强进一步提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: Real-time pedestrian detection in driving scenes is constrained by three coupled failure modes: tiny targets lose discriminative evidence, occlusion weakens geometric support, and weather or illumination changes distort appearance statistics. We formulate the detector through a unified \emph{contour-guided spectral routing} view rather than treating frequency processing, attention, and boundary reasoning as independent add-ons. The detector routes information in a prescribed order: spatial evidence is first augmented with global spectral context, deep representations then exchange spatial and spectral cues, and cross-scale fusion is finally conditioned on boundary--semantic disagreement. This ordering yields a compact representation pipeline in which low-frequency context stabilizes global structure while high-frequency evidence protects small-object contours. We further retain a wavelet-subband training transformation that perturbs low- and high-frequency coefficients independently, targeting appearance shifts caused by fog, rain, snow, and low illumination. The formulation exposes a single routing variable at each stage and distinguishes reusable signal transforms from the task-specific policy that decides where each signal is injected. On CityPersons, the proposed detector obtains 70.4 AP$_{50}$ and 44.2 AP$_{50:95}$, compared with 68.1 and 42.2 for RT-DETR, while the full wavelet-augmented configuration reaches 71.1 and 44.6.

</details>


### [100] [Newton Deep Unfolding for Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14391)
*Changhua He,Xianchao Xiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个基于二阶优化的深度展开网络 NDU-Net，用牛顿更新模块估计更新方向并生成优化状态，配合牛顿引导多尺度先验模块实现自适应特征恢复，在多种压缩感知采样率下取得更优且更鲁棒的CS重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩感知深度展开方法多基于一阶优化（如ISTA/近似梯度），收敛慢，且未充分利用重建过程中产生的优化状态信息，导致先验无法随重建阶段自适应调整。

Method: 构建牛顿深度展开网络：Newton Update (NU) 模块估计牛顿型更新方向并输出刻画当前重建过程的优化状态；Newton-guided Multi-scale Prior (MP) 模块将这些优化状态注入多尺度特征恢复，使学习到的先验随重建阶段自适应变化。

Result: 在不同CS采样率下的实验表明，NDU-Net取得有竞争力的重建性能并具有更强的鲁棒性；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 将二阶优化引入深度展开可有效提升CS重建的精度与鲁棒性，优化状态驱动的先验设计具有可行性。

Abstract: Compressed sensing (CS) reconstructs images from highly limited measurements, but existing deep unfolding methods are typically driven by first-order optimization and weakly exploit the optimization states generated during reconstruction. To address these limitations, we propose a Newton deep unfolding network (NDU-Net), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first deep unfolding framework that leverages second-order optimization for CS reconstruction. Specifically, NDU-Net introduces a Newton update (NU) module to estimate Newton-type update directions and generate optimization states that characterize the current reconstruction process.
