Conclusion: 自进化需要在人形机器人上重新定义：进化对象是包含身体在内的多维状态，进化过程须在安全与不确定性约束下受控进行。未来工作应包括构建面向自进化人形机器人的评估基准，并协同推进算法、机载系统与治理三方面研究。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are becoming an important part of embodied artificial intelligence, driven by advances in reinforcement learning for locomotion, world models for prediction, and vision-language-action models for general control. However, most of these systems remain static after deployment. A policy is trained offline for a fixed objective and then frozen, even though the tasks, environments, and robot bodies keep drifting over time. An emerging paradigm of self-evolving agents aims to address this problem by allowing systems to improve from their own post-deployment experience. Since most existing studies focus on disembodied software agents, this survey examines how self-evolution changes when an agent has a physical body. We first define self-evolution for humanoids and represent a deployed robot using a state tuple that includes its policy, perception, memory, workflow, and body. This state is updated by an evolution operator in a slow outer loop with a lifelong objective. We then organize the literature into four complementary mechanisms of self-evolution, presented in increasing order of autonomy: self-learning, self-adaptation, self-optimization, and self-generation. Since changes to a humanoid can introduce physical hazards, we treat safety and uncertainty as key design dimensions of the evolution operator, and further formulate admissible evolution as a constraint enforced by a world-model verification gate within a human-oversight envelope. Finally, we present that evaluation should track the robot's evolving trajectory rather than a fixed checkpoint, and we identify the lack of a benchmark designed specifically for self-evolving humanoids. Moreover, we outline open challenges spanning AI algorithms, on-board systems, and governance.

</details>


### [201] [GzDRL: Reproducible and Scalable Deep Reinforcement Learning with Gazebo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13243)
*Amal Dev Haridevan,Junjie Kang,Jinjun Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GzDRL: 单进程、去中间件的 Gazebo 强化学习框架，通过直接同步智能体动作与物理步进，实现确定性、高吞吐、可复现的 RL 训练与评估，并完成无微调的四旋翼真机迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于中间件（如 ROS）的 Gazebo-RL 集成存在非确定性与不可复现问题，难以支撑可扩展、可复现的机器人学习实验；现有 GPU 加速模拟器虽有吞吐优势但可及性与可复现性受限。

Method: 提出单进程 RL 框架 GzDRL，摒弃中间件通信，设计系统化的环境步进机制，将智能体动作与物理更新直接同步；支持确定性数据采集、高效向量化并行与多智能体扩展。

Result: 在评测的工作站框架中吞吐最高，在笔记本硬件上与 GPU 加速模拟器具竞争力；保持精确的智能体-环境同步、多智能体可扩展性与实验级可复现性；学习策略无需微调直接部署到实体四旋翼。

Conclusion: GzDRL 为机器人学与自动化领域的 RL 研究提供了可访问、可复现的平台，缓解了可扩展仿真实验的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: We present GzDRL, a novel single-process reinforcement learning (RL) framework for Gazebo that overcomes longstanding bottlenecks in scalable, reproducible robotics experimentation. Unlike conventional middleware-based RL-Gazebo integrations that suffer from nondeterminism and irreproducibility, GzDRL introduces a systematic, middleware-free environment-stepping mechanism that directly synchronizes agent actions and physics updates. This design enables deterministic, high-throughput data collection, efficient vectorization, and reproducible RL training and evaluation. Comprehensive benchmarks demonstrate that GzDRL achieves the highest workstation throughput among the evaluated frameworks while remaining competitive with GPU-accelerated simulators on laptop hardware, and maintains precise agent-environment synchronization, multi-agent scalability, and experiment-level reproducibility. We further validate sim-to-real transfer by deploying learned policies directly onto a physical quadrotor, without fine-tuning. Our results establish GzDRL as an accessible and reproducible platform for advancing RL in robotics and automation.

</details>


### [202] [Physical Kernel: Structured Visual Latents for Dark Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13244)
*Jinting Hang,Hong Li,Zhenhui Cai,Zhihao Zhao,Jian He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"dark manipulation"设定：仅在初始短暂点亮阶段编码一次 z0=Enc(rgb)，之后策略 π(z) 与开环动力学 f(z,a) 不再使用任何像素即可完成接触密集技能（dark_f）。在 ManiSkill StackCube 上 dark_f 达 68.1% 成功率，远超逐步重编码（35.6%）与冻结/黑屏编码（0%）；遮挡或相机对齐 GT 接触掩码会使点亮基线从 43% 降到 0%，而 dark_f 保持 82.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模仿策略需每步获取像素并在推理中持续编码，导致在遮挡、外观漂移与昂贵感知下脆弱且低效。作者希望验证：接触密集操作是否可在"一次性写入、随后暗中开环执行"的条件下完成，从而揭示视觉信息在技能执行中的真实作用点与信息瓶颈。

Method: 协议为 Write→HOLD：先短暂点亮，由 Enc 编码 z0；随后策略 π(z) 与开环动力学 f(z,a) 在无像素条件下推进。以 ManiSkill StackCube 五级链（near_A→grasped→lifted→on_B→stacked）为评测，n=160（seed packs 0/1000）。对照包括：per-step lit_reenc（每步重编码）、freeze/encode_black、Dreamer 风格与像素 null 基线。进行消融：动作 shuffle（动力学 MSE 膨胀 ~9.4x）、写时与 HOLD 期外观漂移（夜间光照）、Write 长度 Tw、提前停止、写时模糊/JPEG 压缩；并设专用 π_write 达 35% 视觉预算 stacked（n=80）；另以特权 state-RSSM MPC（9D dark 观测）作为更强观测 null 基线。

Result: dark_f 在五级链上达 68.1% stacked，per-step lit_reenc 35.6%，freeze/encode_black 0%。共享 Write→HOLD 协议（n=40）下，遮挡与相机对齐 GT 接触邻域掩码使 lit lift 由 43% 降至 0%，dark_f 维持 82.5%。动作 shuffle 使动力学 MSE 增大约 9.4x；写时外观漂移（夜间）致编码失效（0% stacked），但 HOLD 期同一漂移不影响 dark_f lift。Tw 在到达停止相后趋于平坦，更早停止及写时模糊/JPEG 显著降低 stacked。π_write 达 35% vision-budget stacked；匹配的 Dreamer/像素 null（无特权几何）为 0%；特权 state-RSSM MPC 约 35% stacked，属更强观测 null 而非匹敌的视觉基线。

Conclusion: 接触密集技能可在初始一次性视觉写入后于无像素的开环设定中完成，说明成功执行更多依赖写入时刻的接触相关状态，而非后续逐帧感知；该结果界定了"暗操作"的能力边界（对写时观测质量敏感、对 HOLD 期外观漂移鲁棒），但未提供与像素学习基线严格匹配的对照，且仅限仿真与单一任务族，泛化性有待验证。

Abstract: We study dark manipulation: after a brief lit Write encodes z0 = Enc(rgb), a policy pi(z) and open-loop dynamics f(z,a) complete contact-rich skills without further pixels (dark_f). On ManiSkill StackCube (n=160; seed packs 0/1000), dark_f attains 68.1% stacked on the five-rung chain (near_A -> grasped -> lifted -> on_B -> stacked), compared with 35.6% for per-step lit_reenc and 0% for freeze/encode_black. On a shared Write->HOLD protocol (n=40), occlusion and camera-aligned GT contact-neighbor masks drive lit lift from 43% to 0%, while dark_f holds 82.5%; shuffling actions inflates dynamics MSE by ~9.4x; write-time appearance shifts break encoding (night: 0% stacked), yet the same shifts during HOLD leave dark_f lift unchanged; Write length Tw is flat once the stop phase is reached, while earlier stops and write-time blur/JPEG sharply cut stacked. A dedicated pi_write reaches 35% vision-budget stacked (n=80); matched Dreamer-style/pixel nulls without privileged geom stay at 0%. Privileged state-RSSM MPC reaches ~35% stacked with 9D dark observations -- a stronger-observation null, not a matched visual baseline.

</details>


### [203] [Conflict-Predictive Variable Horizons in Multi-Drone Distributed Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13270)
*Linda Mümken,Michael Schwung,Stefan Lier,Andreas Schwung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种冲突预测型可变预测时域策略，各无人机本地设定时域，保持分布式MPC结构不变；通过置信漏斗外推邻居飞行线并闭式求解冲突时间，取覆盖最远预测冲突的最小可行时域，并证明递归可行性与渐近稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定预测时域在分布式MPC多机避撞中存在两难：短时域计算便宜但对逼近邻居响应滞后，长时域可提前预判但单步代价随时域超线性增长。作者希望在保持分布式MPC框架不变的前提下，让时域随冲突态势自适应伸缩，兼顾计算开销与安全性。

Method: 每架无人机基于短历史位置观测外推邻居飞行线，用随预测范围收窄的置信漏斗与自身飞行线逐一检验，闭式求得各冲突时间；时域取覆盖最远预测冲突的最小可行值，空旷空域收缩至下限、仅在存在前方冲突时增长。在最小值满足单一可计算可行性界时，证明递归可行性与渐近稳定性对所有可选时域成立；线性模型下成立，经级联内环将四旋翼平动动力学化为受扰双积分器，推广至线性化模型并作为实用稳定性推广至全非线性模型。在密集对跖交换基准上仿真验证。

Result: 在密集对跖交换基准仿真中，可变时域相比长固定时域显著降低单步求解代价与总计算量，且在每次运行中均保持安全间距；相接近单步代价的短固定时域则无法保持间距。

Conclusion: 可变预测时域可在不改变分布式MPC结构的情况下按冲突态势自适应，兼顾计算效率与避撞安全性，并具备递归可行性与稳定性理论保证，可推广至四旋翼线性化乃至非线性模型。

Abstract: In distributed model predictive control for multi-drone collision avoidance, a fixed prediction horizon forces a compromise: a short horizon is inexpensive but reacts late to approaching neighbors, whereas a long one anticipates conflicts at a per-step cost that grows superlinearly with its length. We propose a conflict-predictive variable horizon that each drone sets locally, leaving the distributed model predictive control itself unchanged. From a short history of observed positions, a drone extrapolates the flight lines of its neighbors, tests each against its own using confidence funnels that narrow with prediction range, and obtains each time to conflict in closed form. The horizon is then the smallest admissible value whose planning window covers the farthest predicted conflict. It collapses to its minimum in clear airspace and grows only when a conflict lies ahead. Provided this minimum meets a single computable feasibility bound, we prove that recursive feasibility and asymptotic stability are preserved for every horizon the policy can select. These guarantees hold for a linear model, and a cascaded inner loop reduces each quadrotor's translational dynamics to a perturbed double integrator, so they carry over to the linearized quadrotor model and, as practical stability, to the full nonlinear one. In simulation on dense antipodal-swap benchmarks, the variable horizon reduces both per-step solver cost and total computation well below those of a long fixed horizon, and it maintains separation in every run, which a short fixed horizon of comparable per-step cost does not.

</details>


### [204] [Extending the Speed Limit of Quadrupedal Locomotion via Refined Actuator Modeling and Adaptive Command Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13289)
*Yucheng Tao,Shaowen Cheng,Guorong Lan,Yanyan Yuan,Yongbin Jin,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出高速四足机器人运动框架：精细化执行器模型（电压耦合+磁饱和）减小sim-to-real差距，结合两阶段课程学习与自适应指令调度（ACS）稳定训练；36.5 kg的BlackPanther2实现跑步机13.2 m/s、户外11.65 m/s，创四足机器人速度世界纪录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速运动中执行器接近物理极限且非线性显著，但现有方法训练时忽略执行器非线性与物理约束，导致高动态运动下sim-to-real差距大、性能受限。

Method: 1) 精细化执行器模型，显式建模高速电压耦合与磁饱和，更准确刻画力矩-转速包络；2) 强化学习框架：两阶段课程学习 + 自适应指令调度（ACS），保证宽指令分布下的训练稳定性。

Result: 36.5 kg四足机器人BlackPanther2实现跑步机13.2 m/s、户外11.65 m/s，达到新的SOTA并据称创下四足机器人运动世界纪录；结果验证精确执行器建模可防止策略对非物理动力学的利用，ACS在不牺牲性能下提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 准确的执行器建模对缩小高动态运动sim-to-real差距、防止非物理策略利用至关重要；所提框架可在宽指令分布下稳定训练并取得突破性速度表现。

Abstract: Achieving high-speed locomotion in quadrupedal robots remains highly challenging, as actuators operate near their physical limits and exhibit pronounced nonlinearities. However, many existing methods neglect actuator nonlinearities and physical constraints during training, leading to a significant sim-to-real gap under highly dynamic motions and limiting achievable performance. To address this issue, we propose a high-speed locomotion framework that reduces sim-to-real discrepancies and stabilizes learning over a wide command distribution. A refined actuator model explicitly captures high-speed voltage coupling and magnetic saturation, enabling a more accurate representation of the torque-speed envelope. In addition, a reinforcement learning framework incorporating a two-stage curriculum and adaptive command scheduling (ACS) ensures stable training. Experiments on the 36.5 kg quadruped BlackPanther2 (BP2) demonstrate speeds of up to 13.2 m/s on a treadmill and 11.65 m/s outdoors, establishing a new state-of-the-art and, to the best of our knowledge, a world record for quadrupedal robot locomotion. The results further highlight the importance of accurate actuator modeling in preventing non-physical policy exploitation, and show that ACS improves robustness without sacrificing performance.

</details>


### [205] [Breaking speed scaling in quadrupedal robots via Huygens' coupled-pendulum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13290)
*Yucheng Tao,Yongbin Jin,Shaowen Cheng,Xianwei Liu,Yanyan Yuan,Yanhong Liang,Chengkai Su,Chaojie Fu,Guorong Lan,Wei Yang,Hongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受惠更斯耦合摆启发，提出通过跨腿惯性耦合重分布步态周期能量、降低峰值关节扭矩，并结合硬件参数协同优化，使四足机器人实现10.74 m/s（Froude数21.4）、百米12.2秒，首个突破10 m/s的腿足机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往提升奔跑速度主要靠控制算法提升现有硬件利用率，而速度上限本质上受快速运动所需的力/扭矩约束，通常只能靠增大执行器能力（暴力硬件缩放）解决。作者希望找到一种不依赖硬件堆叠、通过原理性利用内在动力学来突破速度极限的途径。

Method: 以惠更斯耦合摆为灵感，引入腿间惯性耦合，使能量在步态周期内重新分配，从而降低快速周期运动所需的峰值关节扭矩；将硬件参数作为额外设计变量，把该分析扩展为设计-控制协同优化框架，系统性地在机器人设计中利用惯性耦合。

Result: 在该框架指导下，四足机器人达到10.74 m/s奔跑速度（Froude数21.4），百米冲刺用时12.2秒，是首个超越10 m/s的腿足机器人。

Conclusion: 惯性耦合是支配高速腿足运动的内在机制，可有效降低力/扭矩需求，为敏捷机器人系统设计提供了新思路：通过原理性利用内在动力学而非单纯放大执行器来实现极限性能。

Abstract: Achieving biological-level running speeds has largely been pursued through advances in control algorithms, which improve the utilization of existing hardware. However, the ultimate speed limits remain governed by the underlying force and torque requirements of rapid locomotion, which are typically addressed through increased actuator capacity. Inspired by Huygens' coupled pendulums, we demonstrate that superior locomotion can emerge from principled exploitation of intrinsic dynamics rather than brute-force hardware scaling. Inter-limb inertial coupling redistributes energy across the gait cycle and reduces peak joint torque required for rapid periodic motion, thereby expanding the achievable speed without proportional increases in actuator capability. Incorporating hardware parameters as additional design variables further extends this analysis into a co-optimization framework, enabling the systematic utilization of inertial coupling in robot design. Guided by this framework, a quadruped robot achieves a running speed of 10.74 m/s (Froude number 21.4) and completes a 100-meter sprint in 12.2 seconds, representing the first legged robot to surpass 10 m/s. These results establish inertial coupling as an underlying mechanism governing high-speed legged locomotion and highlight its role in reducing force requirements, offering new insights into the design of agile robotic systems.

</details>


### [206] [Ergodic Control and Controlled Diffusion for Robot Learning: Review and Tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13295)
*Max Muchen Sun,Cem Bilaloglu,Ananya Rao,Stefan Ivic,Guillaume Sartoretti,Kathleen Fitzsimons,Ian Abraham,Sylvain Calinon,Todd Murphey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 综述性论文，系统介绍受控扩散（controlled diffusion）与遍历控制（ergodic control）如何为机器人学习提供统计机制保障，涵盖直觉、理论、数值教程与机器人应用，并展望挑战与机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散学习近年在机器人领域取得突破，但受控扩散中"遍历性"这一统计特性对机器人学习的独特价值尚未被系统梳理。作者希望阐明受控扩散与扩散学习两条脉络的融合，说明遍历性如何形式化保证最优性、支持非短视的信息搜索以及基于空间而非时间的轨迹行为规范。

Method: 综述方法：先给出受控扩散用于机器人学习的直觉，梳理其与扩散学习的联系，随后给出求解受控扩散与遍历控制问题的理论基础与数值教程，并综述其在机器人感知、控制、决策等方向的应用。

Result: 属于综述/教程类工作，主要贡献是理论框架的统一梳理、直觉阐释、数值实现教学以及跨机器人应用领域的归纳，未报告具体实验或量化结果。

Conclusion: 受控扩散诱导的遍历性为机器人学习提供了区别于常规扩散应用的三点关键意义：形式化保证统计最优性、实现面向不确定信息环境的非短视数据采集搜索、支持基于空间特征的行为规范；未来在理论与应用上仍存在诸多挑战与机会。

Abstract: Diffusion learning leverages the statistical mechanism of diffusion processes for learning, reasoning, and inferring complex distributions from data. Recent advances in diffusion learning have been transformative, with robot learning emerging as a key opportunity area, with applications spanning perception, control, and decision-making. At the same time, the statistical mechanism of diffusion processes can be controlled to shape the temporal evolution of the state distribution underlying robot trajectories, inducing ergodic behavior in robotic systems. The frameworks of controlled diffusion and ergodic control were developed around the same time as diffusion learning, and their theories and algorithms have increasingly converged. Ergodicity induced by controlled diffusion has several significant implications for robot learning, distinct from applying diffusion learning to robotics problems: it formally enforces statistical properties required to ensure optimality of robot learning, enables non-myopic search over uncertain information landscapes for data collection, and enables behavior specification based on spatial rather than temporal characteristics of trajectories. This survey introduces the intuition behind controlled diffusion for robot learning, explores its connection to diffusion learning, presents theoretical foundations and numerical tutorials for solving controlled diffusion and ergodic control problems, and reviews applications across robotics. Finally, we discuss key challenges and future opportunities in leveraging controlled diffusion for robot learning.

</details>


### [207] [IMM-based Multiple Object Tracking using a State Prediction Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13307)
*Chan-Bin Lim,Dong-Hee Paek,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PR-IMM：将Transformer位移预测器（PR）作为一个新模式集成进IMM，与CV/CA/CT并行按模式概率动态融合先验位置，利用雷达多普勒量测提升非线性运动建模，同时保持物理模型的稳定性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶避障与路径规划依赖目标跟踪；雷达在恶劣天气下仍能检测并通过多普勒直接测速，适合跟踪。但纯物理运动模型（CV/CA/CT）对非线性运动的表征能力有限，而纯数据驱动方法缺乏稳定性与可解释性，故需在保留物理模型框架的前提下引入数据驱动预测能力。

Method: 提出PR-IMM框架：1）训练基于Transformer的位移预测模型PR，输入包含雷达多普勒量测，输出目标位移预测；2）将PR作为一种新运动模式，与CV、CA、CT模型并列嵌入IMM滤波器，各模式并行滤波；3）依据模式概率对先验位置进行动态加权融合，实现数据驱动与物理模型的互补。

Result: 相比IMM，位置估计误差降低57.3%；相比PR降低16.5%；ID切换减少25.3%，IDF1提升9.6%。

Conclusion: 将数据驱动的Transformer位移预测作为IMM的一个模式并以模式概率融合，可在不牺牲物理模型稳定性与可解释性的前提下显著提升雷达目标跟踪的非线性运动建模能力。

Abstract: Object tracking is essential for autonomous vehicles to avoid obstacles and plan routes. Radar maintains detection performance even in adverse weather and can measure relative velocity through the Doppler effect, making it well suited for object tracking. In this paper, we propose a data-driven state PRedictor-based Interacting Multiple Model tracking method (PR-IMM) that improves nonlinear object-motion representation while preserving the stability and interpretability of physics-based motion models. The proposed method employs a transformer-based PRediction model (PR) that incorporates radar Doppler measurements to predict object displacement. The PR model is integrated into the IMM as a mode alongside the CV, CA, and CT motion models, and their prior positions are dynamically combined according to the mode probabilities. Experimental results show that PR-IMM reduces position-estimation error by 57.3% over the IMM and by 16.5% over the PR, while reducing ID switches by 25.3% and improving IDF1 by 9.6% over the IMM.

</details>


### [208] [Attention-DP3: Spatially Object-aware 3D Diffusion Policy via Geometry-aligned Attentional Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13318)
*Changbo Yan,Zhongbo Zhang,Zaibin Zhang,Yifan Wang,Lijun Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Attention-DP3：通过注意力注入物体级几何先验（目标性/目标内显著性/背景性三场）增强 DP3 扩散策略，在杂乱遮挡场景中显著优于 DP3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 点云观测在复杂杂乱操作场景中存在固有歧义：目标可能被部分遮挡，或与外观相似的干扰物紧密混杂。随场景复杂度上升，标准 3D 扩散策略（DP3）难以定位并利用任务相关几何，因此需要引入物体级几何线索来提升鲁棒性。

Method: 在保持 DP3 扩散骨干不变的前提下，构建空间物体感知的 3D 扩散策略 Attention-DP3。流程为：对 RGB 图像做开放词汇 2D 分割，借助标定相机几何将目标掩码提升到 3D，获得以物体为中心的几何先验；再通过 Tri-field Attentional Conditioning 构造三个互补场——目标性场（锚定目标物体）、目标内显著性场（强调目标内任务相关几何）、背景性场（抑制干扰物与杂乱）。

Result: 在 Adroit、DexArt、MetaWorld 及真实世界 SO101 平台上，相对 DP3 取得一致性提升，达到各基准的 SOTA。尤其在干扰物增多时，DP3 性能急剧下降，而 Attention-DP3 保持稳定，在重度杂乱下领先 DP3 最多 31%。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 显式注入物体级几何先验（而非仅依赖端到端点云扩散）是提升杂乱遮挡操作策略鲁棒性的有效途径；该方法与扩散骨干解耦、即插即用，并具备真实机器人平台验证。

Abstract: 3D point-cloud observations are inherently ambiguous in complex, cluttered manipulation scenes, where target objects may be partially occluded or tightly intermingled with visually similar distractors. As a result, standard 3D diffusion policies often struggle to localize and exploit task-relevant geometry as scene complexity grows. We propose \textbf{Attention-DP3}, a spatially object-aware 3D diffusion policy that injects object-level geometric cues via attention while keeping the DP3 diffusion backbone unchanged. Our pipeline performs open-vocabulary 2D segmentation on RGB images, then lifts predicted target masks into 3D using calibrated camera geometry to obtain object-centric geometric priors. We incorporate these cues through Tri-field Attentional Conditioning, which constructs three complementary fields: (i) a targetness field to anchor the target object, (ii) an intra-target saliency field to emphasize task-relevant geometry within the target, and (iii) a backgroundness field to suppress distractors and clutter. Experiments on Adroit, DexArt, MetaWorld, and the real-world SO101 platform show consistent improvements over DP3, achieving state-of-the-art performance across benchmarks. Notably, as distractor objects increase, DP3 drops sharply, whereas Attention-DP3 remains stable and outperforms DP3 by up to 31\% under heavy clutter. The code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [209] [Bridging Thought and Action: Taming Long-Horizon Instability in Open-Source LLM Agents with a MetaTool-Enhanced ROS Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13335)
*Kazi Abrar Mahmud,Nilotpaul Kundu Dhurubo,Tamal Kirttonia,Sabbir Hossain Ujjal,Mohammad Ariful Haque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在ROS-Agent架构中引入MetaTool中间机制，强制LLM在执行前生成伪代码计划并存入scratchpad，实现规划与执行分离，提升开源LLM机器人任务可靠性与效率，复杂任务提升约24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源LLM在机器人智能体框架中长期推理不稳定、动作执行低效，需要增强任务可靠性与执行效率。

Method: 在ROS-Agent基础上引入MetaTool：给定自然语言指令，诱导LLM生成工具调用的伪代码计划，存入scratchpad并全程保持，显式分离规划与执行。

Result: 在自建移动机器人平台（多模态感知+运动控制）上，真实交互任务显示任务完成度与上下文一致性提升，复杂任务较基线约+24%。

Conclusion: 结构化预规划机制可减少执行循环、提升确定性，为开源LLM驱动的机器人智能体提供实用改进；但更接近系统集成与工程优化。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled more natural human-robot interaction, but open-source models often exhibit unstable long-horizon reasoning and inefficient action execution when deployed in agentic robotic frameworks. This paper presents an enhanced ROS-Agent based architecture that improves task reliability and execution efficiency for agentic robotic systems using open-source LLMs. The proposed system introduces a novel intermediate mechanism, termed the MetaTool, which enforces structured planning prior to action execution. Given a natural-language command, the MetaTool induces the LLM to generate a pseudo-code plan of intended tool invocations, which is stored in the ROS-Agent's scratchpad and persists throughout execution. By explicitly separating planning from execution, the proposed approach reduces execution loops and improves deterministic behavior. The architecture is validated on a custom mobile robotic platform with multimodal perception and motion control capabilities. Experimental results on real-world interactive tasks demonstrate improved task completion and contextual consistency, with up to ~24% gains on complex tasks compared to the baseline framework.

</details>


### [210] [Chance-Constrained Belief-Space Maneuver Planning for Autonomous Collision Avoidance Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13428)
*Grace Ra Kim,Duncan Eddy,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将低轨交会中"是否掌握足够信息以执行规避机动"的信息-行动权衡建模为信念空间规划问题，用机会约束信念空间MCTS在TCA前推理未来跟踪更新，终端机会约束限制碰撞风险，在96个情景中约40%无需机动即可通过TCA且无风险违约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨交会频次上升，操作者不仅要判断是否需要规避，还要判断现有信息是否足以支撑一次机动决策；固定时间规则策略在推迟干预时虽能减少不必要的机动，却带来终端风险违约增加。

Method: 面向可机动主星与不可机动次星的交会问题，将不确定轨道状态表示为高斯信念，构建机会约束的信念空间蒙特卡洛树搜索（belief-space MCTS）规划器，在最近接近时刻（TCA）之前对可能的未来跟踪更新进行推理；终端机会约束限制到达TCA时超过给定碰撞风险阈值的概率，从而在等待信息性跟踪与过早干预之间权衡。基于NASA CARA数据集的8次历史交会，通过变化次星测量质量与跟踪频次生成96个评估情景。

Result: 在评估条件下，规划器约40%的回合无需机动即通过TCA，且终端碰撞风险零违约；固定时间规则策略在临近TCA推迟干预时可解决更多交会，但终端风险违约增加。无需机动的回合比例强烈依赖跟踪质量与测量频次，从高精度高频次测量下的76%降至最差跟踪条件下的约18%-20%。

Conclusion: 跟踪质量与频次不仅是碰撞风险估计的输入，还决定了干预何时成为必要；信息-行动权衡可被显式规划框架处理，并在真实历史交会数据上实现零终端风险违约。

Abstract: Increasing conjunction frequency in low Earth orbit places growing pressure on spacecraft operators to determine not only whether an encounter requires mitigation, but whether sufficient information is available to commit to a maneuver. This work formulates this information-action tradeoff as a belief-space planning problem for conjunctions between a maneuverable spacecraft and an unmaneuverable secondary object. The planner represents the uncertain orbital states as Gaussian beliefs and uses a chance-constrained belief-space Monte Carlo tree search framework to reason over possible future tracking updates before time of closest approach (TCA). A terminal chance constraint limits the probability of reaching TCA above a prescribed collision-risk threshold, allowing the planner to wait for informative tracking while intervening when deferral becomes too risky. We evaluate the approach on eight historical conjunctions from NASA's Conjunction Assessment Risk Analysis dataset. By varying the secondary-object measurement quality and tracking cadence, we generate a total of 96 distinct evaluation scenarios. Across the evaluated conditions, the planner reaches TCA without maneuvering in approximately 40% of episodes while maintaining no terminal collision-risk violations. In contrast, fixed-time rule-based maneuver policies resolve more encounters without maneuvering when intervention is deferred closer to TCA, but at the expense of increasing terminal risk violations. The fraction of episodes reaching TCA without maneuvering depends strongly on tracking quality and measurement cadence, ranging from 76% under accurate, frequent measurements to approximately 18%-20% under the poorest tracking conditions. These results show that tracking quality and frequency are not only inputs to collision-risk estimation: they can determine when intervention becomes necessary.

</details>


### [211] [STAGE: Diagnosing Semantic Transfer at Grounded Execution in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13458)
*Baosheng Jin,Yushen Liang,Hua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAT-Bench反事实基准，揭示具身语言接地中"语义-动作鸿沟"：指令语义可恢复但未被原生连续动作表达；并提出VISA执行时接口将语义转为决策，大幅降低盲执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身语言接地通常只评估指令指代物识别（语义解析），但真正要求是恢复的语义应能控制智能体动作。作者指出被忽视的"语义-动作鸿沟"：语义可被恢复却在原生连续动作中表达微弱，因此需要新的评测视角与弥补机制。

Method: 提出SAT-Bench：固定视觉场景与智能体状态，仅改变指令语义的反事实基准（目标名替换、像素接地关系替换，以及1000个组合/时序过程反事实）。用目标恢复率与动作敏感度衡量语义-动作转移，并辅以隐状态、无阈值、跨策略与rollout诊断。提出VISA轻量执行时接口，将恢复语义转为ALLOW、DEFER、目标一致性、验证选择四类决策，不更新底层策略。

Result: LIBERO目标名与像素接地关系替换下目标恢复率达100.0%与95.8%，但OpenVLA动作敏感度仅6.8%与7.7%；额外1000个反事实下整体动作敏感度6.1%。VISA将无效指令盲执行从92.7%降至2.8%，同时保留94.0%正常指令，验证选择进一步提升目标一致的action exposure。

Conclusion: 具身语言评测应衡量语义-动作转移而非仅语义解析；VISA作为策略无关的执行时接口表明可通过外部决策层弥补转移失败，为后续VLA语义接地与安全执行研究提供新方向。

Abstract: Embodied language grounding requires more than identifying the referent of an instruction: recovered semantics must also control the action an agent exposes. We study this missing link as a semantic-action gap, where instruction semantics are recoverable but weakly expressed in native continuous actions. We introduce SAT-Bench, a fixed-observation counterfactual benchmark that holds the visual scene and agent state fixed while changing only instruction semantics. On LIBERO target-name and pixel-grounded relation swaps, target recovery reaches 100.0% and 95.8%, whereas OpenVLA action sensitivity remains only 6.8% and 7.7%. The gap persists across 1,000 additional compositional and temporal/procedural counterfactuals, with overall action sensitivity of 6.1%. Hidden-state, threshold-free, cross-policy, and rollout diagnostics further support this semantic-action transfer failure. We introduce VISA, a lightweight execution-time interface that converts recovered semantics into ALLOW, DEFER, target-consistency, and verified-selection decisions. VISA reduces invalid-instruction blind execution from 92.7% to 2.8% while preserving 94.0% of normal commands, and verified selection further improves target-consistent action exposure without updating the underlying policy. Overall, embodied language evaluation should measure semantic-action transfer, not semantic parsing alone.

</details>


### [212] [Constraint-Grounded Reinforcement Learning for Variable Impedance Control in Contact-Rich Robotic Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13516)
*Lin He,Min Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出约束锚定强化学习（CG-RL）变量阻抗框架，通过将力上限与接触反馈作为策略条件、并在控制器侧把请求增益投影到可行域，实现无需重训练或人工整定的在线增益自适应插装，仿真斜插任务中成功率85.8±7.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人插装在接触不确定下，轴向力上限与合适控制器增益随任务变化，单一固定增益难以通用，传统阻抗控制器依赖人工重调参。作者希望消除人工整定，同时保证增益不越界且力上限不被违反。

Method: CG-RL：以力上限与接触反馈为条件的策略输出残差运动、插装速率与请求增益；控制器将该增益投影到可行范围，且不把范围本身暴露给策略，从而以单一策略适配不同力上限，无需重训练或手动调参。在仿真斜向插装任务上以5个训练随机种子评估。

Result: CG-RL在满足力上限的前提下达到85.8±7.7%（均值±标准差）插装成功率，且施加增益保持在可行范围内；固定中点增益基线成功率为50.1%。策略可根据指定力上限连续调整插装速率，并对训练范围以上更宽松的力上限具有泛化性；去除力上限输入的同结构actor不具备该自适应能力。增益范围满足性有保证，力上限满足性为经验验证而非形式化保证。

Conclusion: 将约束信息保持在控制器侧、策略仅学习任务自适应，是消除人工整定、实现多变约束下阻抗控制的有效且可扩展范式，但力约束的形式化保障仍待解决。

Abstract: In robotic insertion under uncertain contact, the axial force limit and the appropriate controller gain vary across tasks. As a result, a single fixed gain is unlikely to remain suitable across different task conditions, making conventional impedance controllers reliant on manual retuning. To eliminate manual retuning, we propose Constraint-Grounded Reinforcement Learning (CG-RL), a variable impedance framework for online gain adaptation. Conditioned on the force limit and contact feedback, the policy outputs a residual motion, an insertion rate, and a requested gain. The controller projects this gain into the admissible range without exposing the range itself to the policy. This separation allows a single policy to operate under different force limits without retraining or manual retuning. We evaluate CG-RL on simulated oblique insertion across five training seeds. CG-RL achieves an $85.8\pm7.7\%$ (mean $\pm$ SD) success rate of insertions without violating the force limit, while keeping the applied gain within the admissible range. As a comparison, a fixed-gain baseline using the midpoint gain achieves a success rate of $50.1\%$. The policy adapts its insertion rate continuously to the specified force limit and further generalizes to more permissive force limits above the training range. In contrast, the same actor without force-limit input does not exhibit this adaptation. The applied gain is guaranteed to remain within the admissible range, while force-limit satisfaction is validated empirically rather than guaranteed formally.

</details>


### [213] [Runtime-Incremental Transformer for Reinforcement-Learning-Based Adaptive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13545)
*Giansalvo Cirrincione,Adriano Fagiolini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在强化学习过程中动态增长与剪枝注意力头数的运行时机制，用"上下文分布有效秩"触发增长、用"单头输出幅值"触发剪枝，在Stribeck摩擦二连杆机械臂上实现全记忆区间的成功率，免去离线调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于注意力元控制器的自适应控制，其注意力头数在训练前固定、需昂贵的离线搜索；在长记忆视界下，固定容量控制器在相当比例的随机种子上会出现灾难性失败。作者希望用运行时自适应头数替代离线搜索。

Method: 在RL训练中在线增删注意力块的头：以on-policy上下文分布的有效秩作为表示容量不足信号触发增长，以每头输出幅值识别冗余头并剪枝；理论上给出增长事件处的策略连续性保证，以及剪枝事件处的定量界。

Result: 在带Stribeck摩擦的二连杆机械臂上，该方法在所有记忆区间均取得完全成功，消除了长视界失败模式，且不再需要离线确定头数。

Conclusion: 动态头数机制可替代离线容量搜索，提升长记忆视界下自适应控制的鲁棒性；其增益与剪枝准则具有可分析的理论性质。

Abstract: Learning-based adaptive control of robotic manipulators with non-observable friction memory has been addressed by attention- based meta-controllers whose number of attention heads is fixed before training and is tuned by costly offline search. At long memory horizons, such fixed-capacity controllers are prone to catastrophic failures on a sizeable fraction of training seeds. The present paper introduces a runtime mechanism that grows and prunes the heads of the attention block during reinforcement learning, governed by two signals: the effective rank of the on-policy context distribution, which triggers growth when representational capacity becomes insufficient, and the per-head output magnitude, which flags redundant heads for removal. Policy continuity at growth events and a quantitative bound at prune events are established analytically. On a two- link manipulator with Stribeck friction, the proposed mechanism attains full success across all memory regimes, eliminating the long-horizon failure mode and removing the need for offline tuning of the head count.

</details>


### [214] [An MRI-Guided Robotic System to Improve Hippocampal Access for Epilepsy Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13572)
*John E. Peters,Abby M. Grillo,Mahshid Mansouri,Daniel S. Esser,Sarah Garrow,Nithin S. Kumar,Dario J. Englot,Joseph S. Neimat,William A. Grissom,Robert J. Webster III,Eric J. Barth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MRI引导的机器人系统，经卵圆孔（foramen ovale）递送弯曲针状激光消融器进入海马体，以提升LITT治疗的穿刺覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床直线式激光间质热疗（LITT）受限于直线路径，难以充分到达海马体，穿刺覆盖率仅约50-60%，而癫痫控制效果被认为与海马体治疗体积相关。

Method: 设计包含定位、指向与弯曲针递送三阶段的机器人系统；采用经卵圆孔的弯曲针路径与弯曲针体，实现单次插入下的曲线轨迹；在体模上用MRI图像评估穿刺覆盖率。

Result: 三次曲线单次插入实验的穿刺覆盖率分别为93.5%、96.5%和60.4%，均超过报道的临床平均50-60%。

Conclusion: 该系统有望在LITT干预中治疗更大体积的海马体，从而改善癫痫控制效果。

Abstract: This paper presents an MRI-guided robotic system that improves hippocampal access by delivering a curved needle-like laser ablator through the foramen ovale, a natural opening in the base of the skull. Both the delivery path and the curved nature of the needle improve upon current clinical straight-line laser interstitial thermal therapy (LITT) as measured by hippocampal cannulation percentage. We describe the design of the robotic system which includes positioning, aiming, and curved needle deployment stages, followed by experimental results assessing cannulation percentages using MRI images in phantoms. In three curvilinear, single-insertion experiments, we achieved cannulation percentages of 93.5%, 96.5%, and 60.4%, exceeding reported clinical averages of 50-60%. Seizure control is believed by physicians to be a function of hippocampal volume treated, and our system provides a means of treating a greater volume of the hippocampus with LITT-based interventions.

</details>


### [215] [Differential Realizability of Static Control Allocation in Multirotors: An Impossibility under Nonredundant Full Actuation and a Pseudoinverse Obstruction under Redundant Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13588)
*Antonio Franchi,Mirko Mizzoni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文分析双向螺旋桨多旋翼在符号推力变量下控制分配的微分可实现性问题，揭示零转速处符号二次映射非局部微分同胚引发的两类后果，并提出可实现性判据与修复构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向螺旋桨多旋翼的力矩映射在符号推力变量下是线性的，便于用经典控制分配求解；但物理转速到推力的符号二次映射在零速处不是局部微分同胚，因此经典分配器给出的解未必能被实际转子转速实现。作者希望明确这种"分配器层面可行、物理层面不可行"的差异在何种条件下出现，并给出可判定、可修复的条件。

Method: 将任务映射与物理提升区分：定义微分可实现性为"执行器输出截面的物理提升"的正则性。据此推导精确与一阶有效性条件，区分结构性秩亏与分配器诱导的速率奇异。在非冗余全驱动下证明单桨换向会消除一个瞬时任务方向，从而在整个任务空间上不存在全局连续可微的精确静态分配器；在冗余驱动下任务映射可能保持正则，但横向伪逆零穿越要求转子转速导数无界。提出局部零空间形变修复孤立伪逆反向，并用全局固定卦限构造保证正则截面的存在性，代价是持续的任务保持内部驱动。

Result: 得到两点理论结论：(1)非冗余全驱动下无全局C¹精确静态分配器；(2)冗余驱动下存在分配器诱导的速率奇异，即伪逆零穿越导致转速导数无界。给出精确与一阶可行性判据，并证明局部零空间形变可修复孤立反向，全局固定卦限构造可保证正则截面存在，但需付出持续内部驱动的代价。

Conclusion: 双向螺旋桨多旋翼的控制分配需在物理可实现性层面而非仅代数层面审视：结构秩亏与分配器诱导奇异须区别对待。局部与全局修复方案在可解性与内部驱动开销之间存在根本权衡，为全向/过驱动多旋翼分配器设计提供了理论边界。

Abstract: Control allocation for multirotors with bidirectional propellers is commonly formulated in signed-thrust variables, where the wrench map is linear. The signed-quadratic map from physical rotor speed to thrust, however, is not a local diffeomorphism at zero speed. This work derives two distinct consequences. Under nonredundant full actuation, a single-propeller reversal removes one instantaneous task direction; hence, no global continuously differentiable exact static allocator exists over the complete task space. Under redundant actuation, the physical task map may remain regular, yet a transverse pseudoinverse zero crossing requires an unbounded rotor-speed derivative. We define differential realizability as regularity of the physical lift of an actuator-output section, derive exact and first-order validity conditions, and distinguish structural rank loss from an allocator- induced rate singularity. A local nullspace deformation repairs isolated pseudoinverse reversals, while a global fixed-orthant construction establishes existence of regular sections at the cost of persistent task-preserving internal actuation.

</details>


### [216] [From Vision to Harvest: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Multi-Arm Robotic Fruit Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13606)
*Vrishan Inukollu,Adyan Zaman,Anvi Kudaraya,Carlos Lazcano,Yuankai Zhu,Stavros Vougioukas,Xiaofan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个评测预训练VLM零样本多臂水果采摘规划的基准，使用真实果园图像对比VLM管线与传统感知-规划管线，发现前沿VLM可零样本生成有效采摘序列，但受限于3D路点生成与碰撞协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂采摘可提效并减少人工，但实际部署需跨环境泛化且需协调共享工作空间中的多臂；现有方法依赖目标环境大量数据采集或过度简化假设，限制规划质量。

Method: 构建真实苹果与柑橘果园图像基准，评测预训练VLM的零样本多臂采摘规划；VLM管线直接输出各臂采摘序列与路点，由轻量轨迹验证器做碰撞检查，并与传统感知-规划管线对比。

Result: 前沿VLM能零样本生成有效的多臂采摘计划，但准确3D路点生成与碰撞感知协调仍是实用部署的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 工作揭示预训练VLM用于多臂机器人采摘的潜力与当前局限，为后续零样本规划研究提供评测基准与问题定位。

Abstract: Multi-arm robotic harvesting offers a promising path to improve harvesting efficiency and reduce reliance on manual labor. However, practical deployment remains challenging because the system must generalize across diverse environments while efficiently coordinating multiple arms in a shared workspace. Existing methods often require substantial data collection in target environments or rely on simplifying assumptions that limit planning quality. In this work, we introduce the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) on zero-shot multi-arm fruit harvesting planning. Our benchmark uses real-world apple and citrus orchard images and compares a VLM-based planning pipeline with a traditional perception-and-planning pipeline. The VLM pipeline directly generates harvesting sequences and waypoints for each arm, while a lightweight trajectory verifier checks for collisions. Our results show that frontier VLMs can generate effective multi-arm harvesting plans zero-shot, but a practical deployment remains limited by accurate 3D waypoint generation and collision-aware coordination. These results highlight both the promise and current limitations of pretrained VLMs for multi-arm robotic harvesting.

</details>


### [217] [How Well Do Pseudo-Rigid Body Models Capture Real Plants? In-Field Validation of Simulated Blueberry Canes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13627)
*Hannah Kolano,Chelse VanAtter,Wei Yang,Cindy Grimm,Joseph R. Davidson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作用基于真实材料属性与梁弯曲理论的刚体-弹簧梁模型在MuJoCo中仿真蓝莓枝条受力变形，并用商业农场的定制测试台实测数据（RGB-D图像、力与位移）进行验证，同时开展关键建模变量敏感性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水果生产中修剪等劳动密集型任务需机器人对植株施加推、拉、弯曲等物理交互，劳动力短缺推动机器人化；此类接触交互需要可变形植株模型，而既往耦合弹簧梁模型多用于仿真学习/规划/控制，缺乏对真实植株力学性能的实验验证。

Method: 以真实材料属性与梁弯曲理论为基础的刚体模型，在MuJoCo中仿真蓝莓枝条加载变形；通过农场定制测试台探压枝条采集真实数据（RGB-D图像、力、位移）进行对比验证；对若干关键建模变量（如直径计算、先验抗弯模量估计）做敏感性分析。

Result: 仿真与真实测量结果被定量对比；模型对植株直径计算与先验抗弯模量估计高度敏感，而后者依赖季节与蓝莓品种；公开了活体蓝莓枝条变形数据集。

Conclusion: 耦合弹簧梁刚体模型可作为植物物理交互任务的可变形建模基础，但精度强依赖几何与材料参数估计，需按季节和品种标定；该数据集与敏感性结论为后续机器人修剪等接触操作的建模仿真提供参考。

Abstract: Many labor-intensive tasks in fruit production such as pruning require physically interacting with the plant (e.g., pushing, pulling, bending limbs, etc.). Due to increasing labor shortages, there is widespread interest in the adoption of robotics in this area. When the robot must physically interact with the system, a deformable model of the plant is beneficial. Coupled, spring-loaded beams have been used in prior work to build deformable plant models in simulation for learning, planning, and control, but this approach has rarely been validated against the mechanical properties of real-world plants. In this paper, we use this rigid-body model, grounded in real material properties and beam bending theory, to simulate the deformation of blueberry canes under loading. To validate the model, we simulate the canes in MuJoCo and compare their behavior with real-world data collected from probing canes at a commercial farm with a custom testbed. We perform sensitivity analysis on several key modeling variables and show that this approach is highly sensitive to the plant diameter calculations and a priori flexural modulus estimation, which is dependent on season and blueberry variety. We also share our dataset of live blueberry cane deformation, including RGB-D images and measured forces and displacements.

</details>


### [218] [Compositional Shift Algebra: Extrapolating Mixed Robot Shifts Without Mixed Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13651)
*Jinting Hang,Zhenhui Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出组合式位移代数（CSA），在模块化机器人堆栈上学习单因素位移算子（观测/策略/动力学），通过算子组合零微调外推到混合位移场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人部署中相机、动作接口、物理动力学等变化往往同时发生，而非单一机制变化；现有适配方法需针对每种混合重新微调或筛选待更新模块，缺乏可组合、可外推的适配框架。

Method: 将机器人系统建模为模块化堆栈 z=E(o), a=g(z,u), z'=f(z,a)，用精确重置探针拟合单因素观测、策略与动力学位移算子，再通过算子组合外推未见过的混合位移，不做混合位移微调；并提出残差 CSA。另设延迟交换子压力测试与干预门控适配作为负对照。

Result: ManiSkill StackCube 上残差 CSA 在未见混合位移上成功率 1.0（10 种子），约为 oracle 混合逆算子水平，较最佳单因素/零样本/参数平均基线高约 67 个百分点；RGB-D 闭环视觉组合接近 oracle；延迟交换子显示策略×延迟的顺序必要性。PickCube 上组合成功率 1.0 vs 非组合 0.33；L1 视觉控制器（无特权位姿与抓取标志）保持 0.95 vs 0.00。主赛道升级：PushCube +33pp，PegInsertion joint8/pose7 EE 各 +67pp，冻结 CSA 下薄 BC +67pp；较深 BC 与公平适配基线需组合（各 +67pp），视觉定位 BC 需组合（+56pp）。

Conclusion: 位移算子具备可组合性，使模块化机器人系统能在无混合位移微调条件下外推到复合分布变化，且组合优于单因素适配与参数平均；但结论主要建立在仿真基准上，真实机器人部署验证与更复杂动力学下的可组合性仍待检验。

Abstract: Robot deployments rarely change one mechanism at a time: cameras, action interfaces, and physical dynamics often shift together. Prior adaptation recipes either finetune a new model for every mix or attempt to select which module to update. We instead learn shift operators on a modular stack z{=}E(o), a{=}g(z,u), z'{=}f(z,a) and compose them. Compositional Shift Algebra (CSA) fits single-factor observation, policy, and dynamics operators from exact-reset probes, then extrapolates held-out mixed shifts by operator composition---without mixed-shift finetuning. On ManiSkill StackCube, residual CSA matches an oracle mixed inverse on held-out mixes (success 1.0 over 10 seeds) while beating best-single / zero-shot / parameter-average baselines by { approx}67 pp. RGB-D vision-in-the-loop composition remains near oracle and far above non-compositional arms; a delay commutator stress shows ordered necessity for policy timesdelay. On a second task (PickCube), residual CSA again reaches compose 1.0 vs. 0.33 non-compositional (n{=}10), and an L1 vision controller without privileged cube/goal poses or grasp flags in the control loop retains compose 0.95 vs. 0.00. Main-track upgrades freeze PushCube (+33 pp), PegInsertion joint8 / pose7 EE (+67 pp each), and thin BC under frozen CSA (+67 pp); deeper BC and fair adapt baselines still need compose (+67 pp each), vision-localized BC needs compose (+56 pp), and delay favors ordered/few-shot deploy. We report Intervention-Gated Adaptation as a negative control.

</details>


### [219] [Does Online Gravity Estimation Matter? Revisiting a Silent Design Split in LiDAR-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13675)
*Jie Xu,Ziyi Jin,Kangjin Yu,Can Jiang,Hongjun Huang,Tongxing Jin,Hongkun Luo,Zhongpu Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 系统对比FAST-LIO2与LIO-SAM中重力/加计零偏的状态配置，发现持续LiDAR修正下固定重力影响极小，但多秒LiDAR中断与动态起始时固定重力代价显著，建议保持重力与加计零偏在线估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIO系统在初始化后是否继续估计重力存在实践分歧，缺少受控的对照实验来量化不同重力-零偏状态配置与重力方向因子在不同LiDAR条件（距离、视场、中断、动态起始）下的实际收益与代价。

Method: 在FAST-LIO2与LIO-SAM中各自实现四种重力-零偏状态配置进行对比；另单独测试基于同一IMU（与预积分同源）的重力方向因子；在12条数据集序列上以配对方式统计垂直与3D位置误差变化并给出90%置信区间，另在4条LIO-SAM序列上验证；专门设计中/长时LiDAR中断、缩短量程与视场缩减、动态起始（不同起始相位）等压力测试场景，并做23D到21D的历史匹配状态切换实验。

Result: FAST-LIO2在12条序列上固定重力且持续LiDAR修正时，垂直与3D误差的配对平均变化90%置信区间落在±2%以内；LIO-SAM的4条序列同样未见在线重力的稳定收益。多秒LiDAR中断（区别于量程/视场缩减）暴露出与轨迹相关的固定重力代价，历史匹配的23D→21D切换使3D误差上升在LiDAR恢复更新后重现已可复现。5秒中断下，方向因子在Hall05改善精度，但在线重力的TUHH上两项误差均变差；动态起始测试显示固定零偏在特定起始相位失败。

Conclusion: 常态连续LiDAR修正下，是否在线估计重力对精度影响可忽略，但为应对多秒中断与动态起始等鲁棒性场景，建议保持重力与加速度计零偏在线；重力方向因子仅在目标工况下验证了垂直与3D精度收益后使用。

Abstract: LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO) systems differ in whether they continue estimating gravity after initialization. We compare four gravity-bias state configurations in each of FAST-LIO2 and LIO-SAM, then separately test a gravity-direction factor. Across 12 dataset sequences evaluated with FAST-LIO2, fixing gravity under continuous LiDAR correction produces mean paired changes in vertical and 3D position errors with 90% confidence intervals within $\pm 2\%$. Tests on 4 sequences with LIO-SAM likewise show no consistent benefit from online gravity. Multi-second LiDAR outages, unlike reduced range or field of view, reveal trajectory-dependent costs of fixing gravity. A history-matched 23D-to-21D switch places the repeatable 3D error increase after LiDAR updates resume. Under 5-s outages, a direction factor from the same IMU used for preintegration improves accuracy on Hall05 but worsens both errors with online gravity on TUHH. Dynamic-start tests also show fixed-bias failures at particular starting phases. We recommend keeping gravity and accelerometer bias online for robustness; use a direction factor only after verifying vertical and 3D accuracy gains under the intended operating conditions.

</details>


### [220] [How to Better Train VLAs: Lessons Learned From the REAL-I Challenge at ICRA 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13679)
*Jiaming Wang,Jizhuo Chen,Diwen Liu,Wang Song,Qiang Wang,Jie Ren,Chao Fu,Dingkun Zhu,Minchi Ruan,Hongtong Li,Yuhua Jiang,Zhiwei Xue,Yongping Pan,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是ICRA 2026首届REAL-I挑战赛的总结报告，围绕"固定演示预算下机器人策略如何更有效学习"这一问题，介绍仿真、真机评测与现场决赛的任务设定、数据与部署接口，并对比NUS-CLEAR、RCL-Lab等三支队伍的系统方案与结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略学习受限于真机演示数据预算，如何在有限示范下兼顾预训练先验与部署环境适配尚缺乏系统性比较与评测基准。

Method: 以共享双臂人形平台组织挑战赛，设置仿真、真机与现场决赛三阶段；各队结合预训练VLA模型与任务专用模仿策略，在数据整理、分阶段适配、checkpoint选择与动作空间设计上采取不同策略。

Result: 团队报告显示：适配部署环境同时保留先验能力、在合适时间尺度上处理演示质量、抑制非活动机器人部件的误差是关键；同时揭示离线动作预测指标难以预测闭环成功率。

Conclusion: 提出应将数据、适配、评测与部署整合看待的固定数据机器人学习视角。

Abstract: How can robot policies learn more effectively from a fixed demonstration budget? The first Real-world Embodied AI Learning (REAL-I) Challenge at ICRA 2026 examined this question through simulation, real-robot evaluation, and an on-site final on a shared dual-arm humanoid platform. We describe the challenge tasks, data and deployment interfaces, and competition results, then compare the approaches contributed by NUS-CLEAR, RCL-Lab, and this http URL . Their systems combined pretrained vision-language-action models and task-specific imitation policies with different strategies for data curation, staged adaptation, checkpoint selection, and action-space design. The team reports highlight the importance of adapting to the deployment environment while retaining prior capabilities, treating demonstration quality at an appropriate temporal scale, and suppressing errors in inactive robot components. They also expose the limitations of offline action-prediction metrics for forecasting closed-loop success. These observations motivate a view of fixed-data robot learning that integrates data, adaptation, evaluation, and deployment.

</details>


### [221] [GROOVE: Geometry-Guided Reduction of Operational-Space Jerk in VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13695)
*Sangho Yun,Minsoo Kim,Minwoo Cho,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GROOVE：一种无需重训练或额外VLA推理的在线调节器，在原始EEF轨迹周围搜索方向性修正区域，用QP生成参考立方体与13个方向候选，在偏差上限约束下最小化指令空间jerk，显著降低平移/旋转抖动并保持或提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块VLA策略一次查询执行多条指令，但块内及重规划边界处的jerk会导致振荡运动与执行器突变，影响平滑性与硬件寿命。已有方法多需重训练或额外推理，成本高。

Method: GROOVE在线调节器：以已交付指令为边界条件优化新chunk，在每条指令后约束相对原始规划的累计平移与局部轴角偏差；使用二次规划生成一个参考立方体与13个方向候选，在参考相对偏差上限下选择指令空间jerk最小的候选。无重训练、无额外VLA推断。

Result: 在留出LIBERO基准上，平移与旋转EEF jerk分别降低33.02%和43.42%，任务成功率95.75% vs 原始93.75%；在50对匹配UR5e实测中，TCP平移/旋转jerk降低16.39%/19.49%，关节电流变化率降低29.09%。

Conclusion: GROOVE以轻量在线QP后处理有效抑制分块VLA的边界与块内抖动，兼顾平滑性、偏差约束与任务成功率，并具备真机验证。

Abstract: Chunked vision language action (VLA) policies execute several commands per query, but jerk within chunks and across replanning boundaries can induce oscillatory motion and sharp actuator transients. We present GROOVE, an online regulator that searches directional correction regions around the raw three dimensional end effector (EEF) path, without retraining or additional VLA inference. It optimizes the new chunk using delivered commands as boundary conditions, reducing boundary and within chunk jerk while bounding cumulative translation and local axis angle deviation from the raw plan after every command. Using quadratic programs (QPs), GROOVE generates a cube reference and thirteen directional candidates, then selects the one with the lowest command space jerk under a reference relative deviation cap. On a held out LIBERO benchmark, GROOVE achieves the largest reductions among the evaluated methods, reducing translational and rotational EEF jerk by 33.02% and 43.42%, respectively, with task success of 95.75% versus 93.75% for raw execution. Across 50 matched UR5e pairs with measured execution timing, it reduces translational and rotational tool center point (TCP) jerk by 16.39% and 19.49% and joint current slew by 29.09%.

</details>


### [222] [Decentralized Multi-Robot Task Allocation Under Degraded Communication: A Benchmark of Performance, Reliability, and Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13711)
*James Lott,Vahraz Honary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在多机器人任务分配（MRTA）场景下，对六种去中心化分配器（CBAA、ACBBA、PI、HIPC、DMCHBA、DGA）进行系统基准测试，刻画路由性能、通信鲁棒性、可靠性、计算负担与规模敏感性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式自主平台选择去中心化MRTA方法时，仅看路径性能不足，需综合考虑通信退化、分配可靠性与算力约束，而现有缺乏覆盖这些维度的统一评测。

Method: 在Collaborative Visit场景中，用500组配对十目标任务，覆盖25种理想/退化通信条件（伯努利丢包、Gilbert-Elliott丢包、瑞利衰落），并额外开展预分配、执行中集成计算、网格尺寸/机器人密度/目标负载敏感性实验，对比六种分配器的MinMax/MinSum行程、发布强度、可靠性与计算耗时。

Result: 24种受损条件下DGA与DMCHBA的MinMax行程最优（24.49/24.78步）；HIPC的MinSum最优（66.95步）但DGA紧随（67.22）且两者在所有受损条件均居前二；DMCHBA发布强度最低（2.08次/团队步）。预分配中HIPC与DMCHBA在全部条件稳定可用，ACBBA、PI、DGA随通信退化失稳。十目标全协议计算中位数从4.88 ms（DMCHBA）到1.346 s（DGA）；静态与执行中集成计算的排序随任务负载增大而分化。

Conclusion: 不同分配器在路由目标、通信行为、可靠性与计算约束上形成彼此不同的运行区间，部署选择应依据具体约束匹配，而非单一指标排序。

Abstract: Selecting a decentralized Multi-Robot Task Allocation (MRTA) method for embedded deployment on autonomous platforms requires considering more than route performance alone. We benchmark six decentralized MRTA allocators (CBAA, ACBBA, PI, HIPC, DMCHBA, and DGA) in the Collaborative Visit (CV) scenario to characterize tradeoffs among MinMax and MinSum travel, communication robustness and demand, allocation reliability, computational burden, and scale sensitivity. The core study uses 500 paired ten-target instances across 25 ideal and degraded communication conditions spanning Bernoulli loss, Gilbert--Elliott loss, and Rayleigh fading, with additional campaigns examining pre-allocation, execution-integrated computation, and sensitivity to grid size, robot density, and target load. Across the 24 impaired core conditions, DGA and DMCHBA achieved the lowest mean MinMax travel at 24.49 and 24.78 steps, respectively. HIPC narrowly led mean MinSum travel at 66.95 steps, followed by DGA at 67.22, with both methods occupying the top two in every impaired condition. DMCHBA had the lowest publication intensity at 2.08 publications per team step. In ten-target pre-allocation, HIPC and DMCHBA remained viable and stable in every tested condition, while ACBBA, PI, and DGA lost stability or viability as communication degraded. Under ideal delivery, median full-protocol computation $\Cterm$ in the primary ten-target comparison ranged from 4.88 ms for DMCHBA to 1.346 s for DGA. Static route quality preserved DGA and DMCHBA as the leading MinMax methods, while DGA led MinSum at three of four target loads and HIPC led at 50 targets. Static and execution-integrated computation rankings diverged as task load increased. The results identify distinct allocator operating regions across route objective, communication behavior, reliability, and computational constraints.

</details>


### [223] [Learning In-Hand Object Reaching to General 6D Poses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13761)
*Junxiao Lin,Tianyue Wu,Jie Yin,Jia Pan,Kaifeng Zhang,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POISE：基于 sim-to-real 强化学习的灵巧手手内 6D 物体位姿到达框架，联合控制物体位置与姿态，实现连续多目标到达与扰动恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数学习型手内操作方法只关注重定向、连续旋转或平移，而大量真实任务需要同时控制物体的位置与姿态。作者将这一能力形式化为"手内 6D 位姿到达"：从已有抓取出发，通过协调手指运动把物体移动到相对手掌的目标位姿，从而避免反复重新抓取与大幅手臂运动。

Method: 提出 POISE（Palm-relative Object reaching In SE(3)）sim-to-real 强化学习框架，包含四个关键设计：多样化的稳定抓取初始化、以目标与几何为条件的控制策略、自适应 6D 目标课程学习，以及兼顾位姿到达与抓取保持的紧凑奖励设计。仿真中训练后迁移到真实多指灵巧手硬件。

Result: 仿真中，多样化初始化将留出抓取成功率从 40.1% 提升至 51.5%，掉落恢复率从 33.8% 提升至 72.9%；课程学习将全范围成功率从 6.2% 提升至 59.5%。硬件上，抓取保持奖励将三目标序列成功率从 20% 提升至 80%。真实实验中，POISE 在多种物体几何与手腕朝向下无需人工复位即可连续到达 6D 目标，并能从外部扰动中恢复。

Conclusion: POISE 首次将手内操作表述为手掌相对 6D 位姿到达并给出可迁移至真机的强化学习方案，消融实验清晰量化了各设计模块的贡献；代码将开源，为灵巧操作研究提供基础。

Abstract: In-hand manipulation allows multi-fingered dexterous hands to reconfigure grasped objects without releasing and regrasping them. This improves manipulation efficiency by reducing repeated grasp acquisition and large arm motions. However, most learning-based methods focus on reorientation, continuous rotation, or translation, whereas many tasks require joint control of object position and orientation. We formulate this capability as in-hand 6D object pose reaching: starting from an existing grasp, coordinated finger motions move the object to a palm-relative target pose. We present POISE (Palm-relative Object reaching In SE(3)), a sim-to-real reinforcement learning framework for this task. POISE combines diverse stable-grasp initialization, goal- and geometry-conditioned control, an adaptive 6D goal curriculum, and a compact reward scheme for pose reaching and grasp preservation. In simulation, diverse initialization raises held-out-grasp success from 40.1% to 51.5% and post-drop recovery from 33.8% to 72.9%; the curriculum raises full-range success from 6.2% to 59.5%. On hardware, the grasp-maintenance reward improves three-target sequence success from 20% to 80%. In real-world experiments, POISE reaches successive 6D targets without manual reset across multiple object geometries and wrist orientations, and recovers from external disturbances. To support further research in dexterous manipulation, we will release our code at this https URL .

</details>


### [224] [When Do Learned Priors Help Visual Inertial Estimation? A Controlled Study of Prior Integration, Calibration, Initialization, and Backend Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13777)
*Jinchang Zhang,Guoyu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出一个受控的"学习增强视觉-惯性估计"评测框架，通过在保持后端、标定、初始化等完全一致的前提下加入MonoViT单目运动先验，证明融合增益不能等价于学习先验的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期大量工作把学习组件（运动、深度、偏置、不确定性/置信度）嵌入几何视觉-惯性估计器，但性能提升究竟来自有用的学习先验，还是来自后端、标定、初始化、时序关联或评测基准的变化并不清楚。缺少同后端对照，学习先验的价值无法被可靠归因。

Method: 构建受控框架，将"融合增益"与"学习先验的增量价值"分离，并评估四层证据：局部运动一致性、全局轨迹精度、物理状态正确性、数值一致性。实例化方式为：在完全未改动的VINS后端中加入基于MonoViT的单目运动先验作为局部相对运动因子。在KITTI上以匹配的传感器流、时间戳、初始化、前后端设置与相机-IMU外参对比原始VINS与学习先验VINS，并分别探查标定、初始化、状态耦合、尺度、BA与回环的影响。

Result: 固定参考外参下，原始VINS平移APE RMSE为31.4 m，加入学习先验为31.8 m；四个序列上先验仅使平均APE变化-0.2%，而权值提高5倍则使误差恶化8.2%。在线外参更新分别使平均APE增加45.7%和52.1%，而平均RPE变化不足2%。

Conclusion: 仅凭融合性能无法确立学习先验的价值；可靠评测需要同后端对照，并联合分析先验兼容性、标定、初始化、全局漂移、物理状态误差与后端一致性。该工作更偏向评测方法论与失效分析，而非新估计器或新应用能力。

Abstract: Learned components are increasingly integrated into geometric visual--inertial estimators to provide motion, depth, bias, uncertainty, or confidence cues. Yet it remains unclear whether gains arise from useful learned priors or from changes in the backend, calibration, initialization, temporal association, or evaluation gauge. We present a controlled framework for learning-augmented visual--inertial estimation that separates fusion gain from the incremental value of a learned prior and evaluates four evidence layers: local motion consistency, global trajectory accuracy, physical-state correctness, and numerical consistency. We instantiate the framework with a MonoViT-based monocular motion prior added as a local relative-motion factor to an unchanged VINS backend. We compare Original VINS and learned-prior VINS under matched sensor streams, timestamps, initialization, frontend/backend settings, and camera--IMU extrinsics, while probing calibration, initialization, state coupling, scale, bundle adjustment, and loop closure. On KITTI, with fixed reference extrinsics, translation APE RMSE is 31.4 m for Original VINS and 31.8 m with the learned prior. Across four recordings, the prior changes mean APE by only -0.2%, while a five-times-higher weight worsens it by 8.2%. Online extrinsic updates increase mean APE by 45.7% and 52.1%, respectively, while mean RPE changes by less than 2%. These results show that fusion performance alone cannot establish the value of learned priors. Reliable evaluation requires same-backend controls and joint analysis of prior compatibility, calibration, initialization, global drift, physical-state error, and backend consistency.

</details>


### [225] [Force-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Hybrid Sensorless Force Estimation for Wheeled-Legged Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13779)
*Xuanqi Zeng,Jiaming Wang,Tianlin Zhang,Lingwei Zhang,Botian Xu,Weipeng Xia,Zhongyu Li,Yun-Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种力感知强化学习方法，通过混合无传感器力估计（广义动量观测+接触约束力旋量投影+时序残差学习）为轮足式移动操作提供显式力观测，并结合模式条件全身策略与轴向导纳力/位置选择器，实现自由运动、纯力控与混合力位控的统一控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力控式移动+操作（loco-manipulation）需要全身策略同时协调运动与手臂动作并调节末端交互力，但浮基动力学与变化支撑接触使其困难；尤其在无末端力/力矩传感器时，缺乏可用于控制的力感知。

Method: 混合无传感器力估计：融合广义动量观测、接触约束力旋量投影（模型部分提取全身扰动的物理结构化成分）与时序残差网络（补偿运动相关残余偏差）；将估计力作为显式观测输入模式条件全身策略，配合轴向力/位置选择器，在单一控制器内实现自由空间运动、纯力调节与混合力/位置控制。

Result: 仿真中无传感器力估计精度与力控性能提升；真机在轮足平台上完成定量阀门旋转与混合擦拭评估，以及力引导开门与零力人体引导运动实验。

Conclusion: 模型驱动与残差学习结合的混合力估计可为无末端力传感器的轮足移动操作提供有效力感知，模式条件全身策略能统一多种接触控制模式，并在真实平台验证可行性。

Abstract: Force-controlled loco-manipulation requires a whole-body policy to coordinate locomotion and arm motion while regulating end-effector interaction forces. This is challenging under floating-base dynamics and changing support contacts, particularly when end-effector force/torque sensing is unavailable for control. This paper presents a force-aware reinforcement learning approach with hybrid sensorless force estimation for wheeled-legged loco-manipulation. The proposed method provides a structured estimate of the end-effector force as an explicit policy observation, enabling force-guided contact behavior without using an end-effector force/torque sensor for control. The force estimate is obtained by combining generalized momentum observation, contact-constrained wrench projection, and temporal residual learning: the model-based components extract the physically structured part of the whole-body disturbance, while the residual network compensates the remaining motion-dependent bias. The estimated force is integrated into a mode-conditioned whole-body policy with an axis-wise force/position selector, allowing free-space motion, pure force regulation, and hybrid force/position control within one controller. Simulation results demonstrate improved sensorless force estimation and force-control performance. Hardware experiments further validate the proposed controller through quantitative valve-rotation and hybrid wiping evaluations, together with force-guided door opening and zero-force human-guided motion on a real wheeled-legged platform.

</details>


### [226] [GeomVLA: Unifying Scene, Motion, and Action in 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13812)
*Ziyin Xiong,Nikos Gkanatsios,Moritz Reuss,Katerina Fragkiadaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeomVLA 在机器人中心 3D 坐标系中统一视觉-语言-动作建模，用视觉几何注意力将场景运动 token 与流式动作去噪耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型多在 2D 图像空间或隐空间建模，缺乏几何一致性；仅预测未来运动不足以提升操作性能，需要场景表示、运动预测与动作生成在统一 3D 框架下保持几何一致。

Method: 用深度与相机标定将预训练 VLM 特征抬升为带空间定位的 3D 场景 token；引入任务条件化的 3D Scene Trajectory Denoiser 学习场景点 3D 运动的潜在表示；取其内部运动 token，通过几何感知注意力条件化 3D 流式动作去噪器，从而闭环使用运动信息而非开环执行轨迹。

Result: CALVIN 上达到 SOTA，LIBERO 与 RoboTwin2.0 上具竞争力，真实世界操作（无机器人动作预训练）优于强基线；消融显示仅做未来运动推理不足，主要收益来自整条感知到动作管线中的几何一致性。

Conclusion: 统一 3D 几何表示下的场景运动与动作联合去噪是提升 VLA 泛化与真实操作性能的关键，几何一致性比单纯预测未来更重要。

Abstract: We present GeomVLA, a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model that unifies perception, latent scene motion prediction, and action generation within a shared robot-centric 3D coordinate frame. Our approach lifts pretrained VLM features into spatially grounded 3D scene tokens using depth and camera calibration, while retaining the semantic representations learned during VLM pretraining. We further introduce a 3D Scene Trajectory Denoiser, a task-conditioned module that learns a latent representation of how scene points are expected to move in 3D. Rather than executing the predicted trajectory as an open-loop plan, GeomVLA extracts intermediate motion tokens from the trajectory denoiser and uses them to condition a 3D flow-based action denoiser through geometry-aware attention. GeomVLA achieves state-of-the-art performance on CALVIN, competitive performance on LIBERO and RoboTwin2.0, and outperforms strong baselines in real-world manipulation settings without robot-action pretraining. Extensive ablations show that future-motion reasoning alone is insufficient: the primary gains are associated with maintaining geometric consistency among scene representation, motion prediction, and robot actions throughout the perception-to-action pipeline.

</details>


### [227] [LePlanner: An Iterative Amortized Controller For World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13845)
*Saksham Bansal,Om Naphade,Chayan Aggarwal,Vrishin M*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LePlanner：在冻结世界模型潜空间中以迭代方式摊销构建与精炼动作序列的控制器，用“到达-保持”目标缓解horizon-reset拖延，性能匹配搜索式规划器而predictor调用少一个数量级、单次决策耗时低3-49倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA式世界模型能学到紧凑结构化潜表示，但潜空间规划面临两难：CEM/MPPI/iCEM等搜索式规划器需大量predictor rollout，单次决策计算与延迟高；策略式方法虽单次前向摊销推理，却在示教分布多峰的接触丰富任务上性能退化。作者希望在保留搜索结构优势的同时摊销其计算成本。

Method: 提出LePlanner——摊销式迭代控制器，通过冻结的世界模型predictor学习构造并迭代精炼潜动作序列。核心为"到达-保持"(arrival-and-hold)训练目标：促使控制器在最早可行horizon到达目标并保持驻留，以解决重复滚动时域重规划导致目标到达被持续推迟的"horizon-reset拖延"失败模式。另加action-Gaussian损失，约束生成动作落在离线数据集支撑集附近。

Result: 在导航、接触丰富操作与连续控制环境中，LePlanner匹配或超越搜索式规划器，同时predictor评估次数减少约一个数量级、单次决策墙钟时间降低3-49倍。成功率：PushT 98%、Reacher 100%、TwoRooms 100%、OGBench Cube 92%。

Conclusion: 在线搜索发现的大部分结构可被摊销进轻量级迭代策略，从而实现快速、具horizon感知、非线性的物理控制，且无需在线优化。

Abstract: World models trained with joint-embedding predictive architectures learn compact, structured latent representations from physical interaction, yet planning in these latent spaces typically relies on one of two costly approaches. Search-based planners such as CEM, MPPI, and iCEM optimize action sequences through many predictor rollouts, achieving strong performance at the cost of high per-decision compute and latency. Policy-based methods amortize inference into a single forward pass but can degrade on contact-rich tasks where the demonstration distribution is multimodal. We propose LePlanner, an amortized iterative controller that learns to construct and refine latent action sequences through a frozen world-model predictor. LePlanner is trained with an arrival-and-hold objective that encourages the controller to reach the goal at the earliest feasible horizon and remain there. This addresses horizon-reset procrastination, a failure mode in which repeated receding-horizon replanning continually postpones goal arrival. An additional action-Gaussian loss keeps generated actions near the support of the offline dataset. Across navigation, contact-rich manipulation, and continuous-control environments, LePlanner matches or exceeds search-based planners while requiring an order of magnitude fewer predictor evaluations and 3-49x lower wall-clock time per decision. It achieves success rates of 98% on PushT, 100% on Reacher, 100% on TwoRooms, and 92% on the OGBench Cube task. These results show that much of the structure discovered through online search can be amortized into a lightweight iterative policy, enabling fast, horizon-aware, nonlinear physical control without online optimization.

</details>


### [228] [ReWeight: Leveraging Human Data for VLA Post-Training via Demonstration Retrieval and Sample Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13851)
*Chenwei Wang,Dianye Huang,Match W.L. Ko,Chenjia Bai,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReWeight通过跨具身视觉运动表征与最优传输，对自我中心人类演示进行演示级检索与样本级加权，将其有效融入VLA后训练，显著提升仿真与真机成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型针对特定机器人与任务的后训练依赖域内演示，而机器人数据采集成本高昂；自我中心人类演示可扩展，但人机数据直接混合会带来跨具身差异并降低策略性能，因此需要一种筛选与加权机制。

Method: 提出ReWeight框架：学习跨具身视觉运动表征，将视觉观测与未来动作结合以度量人机演示的行为相似性；基于最优传输检索与目标机器人数据相关的人类演示，并按跨具身差异大小对样本赋予权重，差异越小权重越大。

Result: 在8个仿真任务与4个真实任务上以π0.5为基座评估：仿真平均成功率从仅机器人数据的39%、随机混合的44%提升至57%；真机平均成功率达68.8%，分别超过两类基线28.8%与13.8%。

Conclusion: ReWeight为将丰富的自我中心人类经验转化为可迁移的机器人学习监督提供了有效范式，缓解了跨具身数据差异问题。

Abstract: Post-training vision-language-action (VLA) models for specific robots and tasks requires in-domain demonstrations, yet collecting diverse robot data is costly. Egocentric human demonstrations provide a scalable alternative, but directly mixing human and robot data can introduce cross-embodiment discrepancies and degrade policy performance. To address this challenge, we introduce ReWeight, a framework that incorporates human data into VLA post-training through demonstration-level retrieval and sample-level weighting. ReWeight learns a cross-embodiment visuomotor representation that combines visual observations with future actions to measure behavioral similarity between human and robot demonstrations. Based on optimal transport, it retrieves human demonstrations relevant to the target robot data and assigns larger weights to samples with smaller cross-embodiment discrepancies. We evaluate ReWeight using $\pi_{0.5}$ across eight simulation tasks and four real-world tasks under both clean and randomized settings. In simulation, ReWeight improves the average success rate of post-trained $\pi_{0.5}$ from 39% with only robot data and 44% with randomly mixed human-robot data to 57%. In the physical experimental setting, it achieves an average success rate of 68.8%, outperforming the baselines by 28.8% and 13.8%, respectively. Overall, ReWeight provides an effective paradigm for transforming abundant egocentric human experience into transferable supervision for robot learning. (Project webpage: this https URL )

</details>


### [229] [Co-Speech with You: Training-Free Personalization of Robot Co-Speech Gestures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13876)
*Bosong Ding,Selma Ancel,Giacomo Spigler,Murat Kirtay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练个性化共语手势生成流水线：冻结音频条件扩散先验+手势风格编码器+轻量条件适配器，用约10秒注册动作单次前向提取可复用风格嵌入，在NAO机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人机器人需适配新用户的共语手势风格，而不希望重新训练模型；同时缺乏自发共语动作数据集。

Method: 冻结的音频条件扩散先验；风格编码器先做说话人身份判别训练，再与条件适配器以扩散目标联合微调；注册动作约10秒，单次前向得到风格嵌入；发布Quest 3采集应用与十名参与者自发共语动作数据集。

Result: 留出说话人上SRA由27.6%提升至69.5%，FGD 34.2（冻结先验34.8）；替换为他人注册嵌入时SRA降至11.4%；重定向到实体NAO机器人、5种TTS语音下仍保持67.3% SRA。

Conclusion: 免训练个性化可有效将风格嵌入迁移到真实机器人部署，兼顾个性化与动作质量，并开源采集应用与数据集。

Abstract: Personal robots should adapt their co-speech gesture style to a new user without requiring model retraining. We present a training-free personalization pipeline that combines a frozen audio-conditioned diffusion prior with a gesture style encoder and lightweight conditioning adapters. The encoder is first trained to discriminate speaker identities and then jointly refined with the adapters using the diffusion objective, enabling a reusable style embedding to be extracted from approximately 10 seconds of enrollment motion through a single forward pass. To support this setting, we also release a Quest~3 capture application and a dataset of spontaneous co-speech motion from ten participants. We evaluate the system on held-out speakers using Style Recognition Accuracy (SRA) and Fr'echet Gesture Distance (FGD) to measure personalization and motion quality. Our approach improves SRA from 27.6\% for the frozen prior to 69.5\% while preserving motion quality (FGD 34.2 versus 34.8), and replacing the enrollment embedding with another person's reduces SRA to 11.4\%. The generated gestures are retargeted to a physical NAO robot, and this improvement also transfers to the robot deployment setting, where speech is synthesized using five TTS voices, retaining 67.3\% SRA.

</details>


### [230] [Rethinking the Implications of Human Feedback for Preference Learning in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13982)
*Qiping Zhang,Kate Candon,Debasmita Ghose,Marynel Vázquez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IMPLIED方法，学习建模人类反馈的隐含语义（接受/拒绝标签的推导），而非依赖固定规则，从而提升机器人偏好奖励学习的准确性与行为合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准偏好学习流程用固定蕴含规则从人类直接反馈推导未选动作的接受/拒绝标签，但用户研究表明人类给出的蕴含标签常与固定规则不符，固定规则会损害偏好学习效果。

Method: 将固定规则的蕴含结果作为初始引导，同时学习随时间推断并修正动作的接受/拒绝标签；与PIE算法结合，利用人类标注的蕴含标签提升奖励学习。在人机交互轨迹数据与实体机器人披萨制作实验中，与固定规则方法与LLM基线对比，并以人类标注oracle为上限参照。

Result: IMPLIED预测人类蕴含标签的准确率高于固定规则与LLM基线，接近人类标注oracle；偏好估计误差降低，机器人动作相对组合奖励（真实偏好奖励+任务奖励）更常表现为理性行为。

Conclusion: 通过学习推理人类反馈的隐含语义，实现了更忠实、更高效的机器人行为自适应，为人类协作中的奖励建模提供了更符合真实人类标注的替代方案。

Abstract: In Human-Robot Interaction, the standard approach to learn a reward model that represents human preferences for robot behavior consists of three steps. First, the robot collects limited direct evidence from human feedback (e.g., positive or negative binary feedback). Then, the robot utilizes the direct evidence to derive accepted or rejected labels to feasible but unchosen actions using fixed implication rules. Finally, the robot updates the reward model with both the direct and derived evidence. Unfortunately, the fixed rule can hinder preference learning: in a user study with two collaborative simulation environments, human-provided implication labels often differed from the standard fixed rule, and using the human labels substantially improved reward learning with the Preference Learning from Implicit and Explicit Feedback (PIE) algorithm. Consequently, we propose IMPLIED, an implication modeling method that treats fixed-rule implications as an initial guide while learning to infer and revise accepted and rejected action labels over time. Across evaluations on recorded human-robot interaction trajectories and a physical robot pizza-making study, IMPLIED predicts human implications more accurately than the fixed rule approach and LLM baselines, approaching the performance of a human-label oracle. In turn, IMPLIED reduces preference-estimation error and leads to robot actions that are more often rational with respect to a combined reward (which includes the true preference reward and a task-specific reward) compared to baselines. By learning to reason about the implications of human feedback, this work enables more faithful and efficient robot behavior adaptation during human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [231] [What Makes an Efficient VLA? Navigating Action-Head Design, Scaling, and Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13984)
*Luoyang Sun,Guoyang Xia,Fengfa Li,Lei Ren,Xinyu Cui,Haifeng Zhang,Fangxiang Feng,Kaike Zhang,Kun Zhan,Yan Xie,Jun Wang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在固定骨干与训练流程、并配对实测端侧延迟的条件下，系统消融VLA的动作头设计与模块规模，发现动作头性能主要由初始化（把语言骨干最后几层Transformer复制进动作头）决定，对齐后容量与推理预算的收益才出现且迅速饱和，据此提出紧凑模型EffVLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型由视觉编码器、语言骨干和动作头组成，但三者相对贡献从未在受控、延迟配对条件下被确立；现有工作常把架构、损失、推理预算等轴混淆比较，难以判断性能提升究竟来自哪一部分，本文希望在固定骨干族与训练管线的前提下厘清动作头的关键杠杆。

Method: 固定骨干（SigLIP2与Qwen2.5）与训练流程，扫描动作头设计与模块规模，并将每个配置与实测端侧延迟配对；核心干预为初始化方式（复制语言骨干最后若干Transformer层到动作头），并对比解码器架构、损失（flow matching）、推理开销等轴；通过表征分析（指令名词注意力、权重空间距离）解释现象。

Result: 动作头初始化是最大且零延迟代价的单一杠杆，且在全部模块尺度上都有效；flow matching与更重的解码器仅在动作头未对齐时有效、对齐后反向；增加推理次数无收益。容量只有在对齐之后才有回报，且在对齐后的动作头收益最高，但在当前π系列VLA已使用的规模附近迅速边际递减。据此得到的EffVLA在LIBERO上匹配最强开源VLA，在LIBERO-Plus多数扰动轴上领先且延迟更低，并零改动迁移到真实SO-ARM101机械臂。

Conclusion: 作者把结果组织为“表征迁移”解释：对齐的动作头持续关注指令中的物体名词并在权重空间上贴近骨干，而非从头学动作；但作者明确声明对齐仅通过初始化达成，因此该解释是组织测量结果的最佳假说而非已证因果，并给出可判定因果的对照实验设计。整体为VLA设计空间提供了受控、延迟感知的经验规律与实用紧凑模型。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models combine a pretrained vision encoder, a language backbone, and an action head, but their relative contribution has not been established under controlled, latency-paired conditions. We fix the backbone families (SigLIP2 and Qwen2.5) and the training pipeline, sweep action-head design and module scale, and pair each configuration with measured on-device latency. The study yields three findings. First, action-head performance is governed primarily by initialization rather than decoder architecture, loss, or inference budget: copying the last transformer layers of the language backbone into the head is the single largest lever, at no latency cost, and the only axis that helps at every module scale. Alignment also explains the other axes: flow matching and a heavier decoder pay off only while the head is misaligned and reverse once it is aligned, and extra inference passes give no measurable benefit; expressiveness appears to substitute for missing alignment. We read this as representation transfer: the aligned head keeps attending to the instruction's object nouns and stays close to the backbone in weight space rather than relearning to act from scratch. Because we reach alignment only through initialization, we offer this as the account that best organizes the measurements, not a demonstrated cause, and name the control that would settle it. Second, capacity pays only after alignment: the aligned action head is the highest-return module to scale. Third, those returns diminish sharply near the size today's $\pi$-series VLAs already use, so further growth buys little in-domain accuracy for its latency. These specify EffVLA, a compact model matching the strongest open-source VLAs on standard LIBERO, leading on most LIBERO-Plus perturbation axes at lower latency, and transferring to a real SO-ARM101 arm with the recipe unchanged.

</details>


### [232] [Mobile Multi-Robot Navigation under Runtime Uncertainty via Koopman Operator Learning and Nonlinear Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14058)
*Xiaobin Zhang,Konstantinos Karydis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于Koopman算子学习的非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）框架，用于多机器人目标到达与编队控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人导航场景中存在运行时不确定性（如随机打滑）且难以获得真实动力学模型，需要兼顾预测精度与闭环控制鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 用Koopman算子理论将仿射输入系统提升为双线性模型，以数据驱动方式学习提升空间动态；在此模型上构建并求解NMPC问题，实现对受扰动与不确定性影响的系统预测与闭环控制。

Result: 在目标到达和编队控制两类任务中，于不同障碍环境、不同打滑程度且未知真实动力学的前提下，经大量数值仿真验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 学习式Koopman双线性模型可有效嵌入NMPC以处理运行时不确定性，实现多机器人导航与编队控制；但验证仅限于仿真，缺少真机实验。

Abstract: In this work, we developed a nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) framework that employs learned dynamics via the Koopman Operator theory for mobile multi-robot navigation. We formulated and solved NMPC problems using a lifted bilinear Koopman-based model that accurately predicts affine input systems affected by perturbations and uncertainties. Two exemplary multi-robot navigation problems are considered: target reaching and formation control. The output of our method enables closed-loop multi-robot navigation and formation control in environments populated with obstacles, whereby the Koopman operator-based model used in the NMPC formulation addresses runtime uncertainties, namely, various degrees of random wheel slipping. We validated the effectiveness of our method for both problems via extensive numerical simulations in different environments with wheeled robots affected by different amounts of slip and without knowledge of their true dynamic models.

</details>


### [233] [Gradient-Free Neural Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability for Scalable Safety-Critical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14087)
*Zeyuan Feng,Ali Fuat Sahin,Santiago Thorup,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出离散时间神经可达性框架，用贝尔曼-艾萨克斯值传播学习后向可达管与可达-规避管，通过利用最优安全干预的 bang-bang 结构构造无梯度教师动作，把对抗式 actor 学习转为监督策略学习，并用窗口化时间课程从终端边界反向稳定长时程值传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HJ 可达性可合成安全证书与鲁棒控制器，但高维非线性系统上面临维数灾难、连续时间神经求解器对空间值梯度精度要求高，以及强化学习类方法边界锚定弱、对抗策略优化非平稳等问题。

Method: 面向控制-扰动仿射系统的离散时间神经可达性：采用 Bellman-Isaacs 值传播，将方程驱动自监督与结构化策略学习结合。避免显式求解 PDE 梯度，利用最优安全干预的 bang-bang 结构，从无梯度值探测构造近似教师动作，把对抗 actor 学习转为监督策略学习；同时用学到的 actor 引导值函数从终端边界反向训练，采用窗口化时间课程，每个窗口作为下一窗口的边界条件。

Result: 在最高 80 维的基准问题上，学习到准确的可达性值函数，且稳定性优于现有基于学习的求解器；在 F1-tenth 赛车任务上展示了观测空间可扩展性，输入为超过 16,000 维的自我中心观测；学到的安全滤波器零样本泛化到未见赛道，并迁移到真实 RC 小车，实现实时鲁棒避碰。

Conclusion: 该工作为高维安全关键机器人系统提供了一种无需显式 PDE 梯度的离散时间神经可达性求解思路，在维度扩展、稳定性与观测空间泛化上取得进展，并具备真机实时避碰部署能力。

Abstract: Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability provides a principled framework for synthesizing safety certificates and robust controllers for safety-critical robotic systems. However, applying reachability analysis to high-dimensional nonlinear systems remains challenging: classical grid-based solvers suffer from the curse of dimensionality, continuous-time neural solvers require accurate spatial value gradients, and reinforcement-learning-based approaches often suffer from weak boundary anchoring and non-stationary adversarial policy optimization. We propose a discrete-time neural reachability framework for control-disturbance-affine systems that learns backward reachable tubes (BRTs) and backward reach-avoid tubes (BRATs) through Bellman-Isaacs value propagation. Our key idea is to combine equation-driven self-supervision with structured policy learning: rather than computing explicit PDE-gradients, we exploit the bang-bang structure of optimal safety interventions to construct approximate teacher actions from gradient-free value probes, converting adversarial actor learning into supervised policy learning. To stabilize long-horizon value propagation, we leverage the learned actor to train the value function backward from the terminal boundary using a windowed temporal curriculum, where each window is used as the boundary condition for the next window. Across benchmark problems up to 80 dimensions, our method learns accurate reachability value functions while improving stability over existing learning-based solvers. We further demonstrate observation-space scalability on F1-tenth racing with over 16,000-dimensional egocentric inputs. The learned safety filter generalizes zero-shot to unseen tracks and transfers to a physical RC car, achieving real-time robust collision avoidance.

</details>


### [234] [Vision-Force Admittance Learning for Peg Insertion into a Movable Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14133)
*Yuzhong Chen,Yongqing Liang,Yunzhi Xu,Irving Fang,Chase Kidder,Hui-ping Wang,Raihan Haque,Yubiao Zhang,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VFAL框架，融合异步视觉反馈与高频力控模型，以视觉位姿估计作正则项，实现动态环境下的毫米级精密插孔操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境下（移动基座或目标未知运动）的精密操作存在显著不确定性，与peg-in-hole等高精度任务的紧公差需求相冲突，传统静态假设方法难以兼顾实时适应性与精度。

Method: 构建Vision-Force Admittance Learning（VFAL）框架：将低频视觉位姿估计作为正则项融入高频力基导纳模型，实现异步视觉-力反馈融合；利用视觉基础模型（vision foundation models）获取鲁棒位姿；并加入失败恢复机制提升鲁棒性。

Result: 真实机器人实验验证，在多类轴件（peg）与动态环境设定下取得高成功率，并展现对动态运动的强适应性与毫米级精度。

Conclusion: 视觉正则化的力-导纳学习可有效解决动态精密装配中的不确定性，视觉基础模型+在线策略自适应+失败恢复构成可行的工程范式；局限在于未给出定量误差/成功率指标与泛化边界。

Abstract: Precise manipulation in dynamic environments, whether induced by a mobile robot base or a target with unknown motion, remains a major challenge in robotics. Manipulation in dynamic environments introduces substantial uncertainty, which fundamentally conflicts with the tight precision requirement of precise tasks such as peg-in-the-hole. We propose a Vision-Force Admittance Learning (VFAL) framework that fuses asynchronous visual feedback with a high-frequency force-based model, using visual pose estimations as a regularization term. VFAL adapts insertion strategies online to dynamic motion while maintaining millimeter-level precision. To obtain robust, low-frequency pose information, we employ state-of-the-art vision foundation models for visual pose estimation. Additionally, we incorporate failure recovery mechanisms to enhance overall robustness. We validate our approach in real-world experiments, demonstrating high success rates and strong adaptability to various pegs and dynamic environments.

</details>


### [235] [When Faster VLA Deployment Changes Closed-Loop Behavior: Task Success-Latency Analysis of SmolVLA Across PyTorch and ONNX Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14146)
*Rafiqul Islam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在 RTX 2060 (6GB) 上将 HuggingFaceVLA/smolvla_libero 从 PyTorch+AMP 部署到 ONNX Runtime CUDA EP，在 LIBERO Spatial/Object 上做 100（主）/300（配对）episode/suite 闭环评测；发现所谓 FP16/INT8 的 ONNX artifact 实为字节相同的 FP32 图，Spatial 成功率从 70% 掉到约 40%，而语言上下文宽度（16/24/32）是关键贡献因子，24 宽可在约一半延迟下追平 baseline。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
