Motivation: VLA 部署常以推理延迟为单一指标，但加速可能改变闭环任务行为；作者希望厘清 ONNX Runtime 加速在 VLA 上的真实延迟收益与成功率代价，并排查接口约束（如语言上下文宽度）与 artifact 真实精度的影响。

Method: 同一 VLA 模型（smolvla_libero）在 LIBERO Spatial/Object 两个 suite 上做闭环 rollout（MuJoCo 3.3.2、LeRobot 0.6.1、seed 42），对比 PyTorch+AMP 与 ONNX Runtime CUDA EP；主评测 100 episode/suite，配对 rollout 300 episode/suite；对 ONNX artifact 做 graph audit（检查是否真的为 FP16/INT8 图）；再做静态语言上下文宽度消融（16/24/32 tokens）；统计上使用 Wilson 区间与两比例卡方检验。

Result: PyTorch+AMP：Spatial/Object 成功率 70.0%/88.0%，p99 1181 ms。标称 FP16 与 INT8 的 ONNX 将 tether-inspect p99 降到 601 ms 与 532 ms，但 Spatial 掉到 41.0%/40.0%，Object 保持 89.0%。Graph audit 显示二者为字节相同的 FP32 图，即标称 INT8 并非算子级量化。语言宽度消融：Spatial 在 16/24/32 下为 41.0%/75.0%/71.0%，Object 与延迟基本稳定；Width-24 ONNX 的 Spatial 与 PyTorch+AMP 在约一半延迟下统计可比（区间重叠，p=0.53）。

Conclusion: 在该技术栈中，语言上下文宽度是闭环成功率的重要贡献因子，但不能解释全部 PyTorch 与 ONNX 的差异；VLA 部署评测应同时报告延迟、artifact 检查、接口约束与闭环成功率。作者提供代码链接，并指出需要进一步定位残余差异来源。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) deployment can reduce inference latency while changing closed-loop task behavior. We evaluate HuggingFaceVLA/smolvla_libero on an RTX 2060 (6 GB) in LIBERO Spatial and Object (MuJoCo 3.3.2, LeRobot 0.6.1, seed 42), comparing PyTorch+AMP with ONNX Runtime CUDA Execution Provider (CUDA EP). The main evaluation uses 100 episodes/suite; a paired rollout uses 300 episodes/suite. PyTorch+AMP reaches 70.0%/88.0% Spatial/Object success at 1181 ms p99. Requested-FP16 and requested-INT8 ONNX reduce tether-inspect p99 to 601 ms and 532 ms, while Spatial success falls to 41.0% and 40.0% and Object remains at 89.0%. A graph audit shows those artifacts are byte-identical FP32 graphs, so the requested-INT8 row is not operator-level INT8 quantization. A static language-width ablation (16/24/32 tokens) yields Spatial success of 41.0%, 75.0%, and 71.0%; widths 24 and 32 recover much of the Spatial drop while Object success and uniform-bench latency stay approximately stable. Width-24 ONNX Spatial success is comparable to the PyTorch+AMP baseline at roughly half the latency (Wilson intervals overlap; two-proportion chi-squared p=0.53). Context width is an important contributor in this stack; it does not account for every PyTorch-vs-ONNX difference. Deployment evaluation should jointly report latency, artifact inspection, interface constraints, and closed-loop success. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [236] [Visible Touch: Rendering Contact for Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14156)
*Metin Alp Dogan,Edward Sun,Feng Xu,Daniel Wu,Allen Peng,Dennis Hong,Yuchen Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 "Visible Touch"：将接触信号以场景同一空间帧（图像叠加/可视化形式）呈现，使任意图像条件策略无需改架构即可用触觉；配套开源低成本磁性接触传感器（参数化CAD到模具流程）。LIBERO上BC-Transformer平均+15.7pp，微调miniVLA +25pp，π0.5在四个真实接触密集任务+30pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉对稳健操作至关重要，但主流VLA/视觉运动策略仅依赖视觉与本体感知。已有触觉融合方案需专用硬件、独立触觉编码器或非图像策略骨干，与当前基于预训练2D视觉表征的图像条件策略范式不兼容。作者的关键洞察是：瓶颈不在接触信息本身，而在于其"交付方式"——只要接触信号与策略已关注的场景处于同一空间帧，就能被任意图像条件策略直接使用。

Method: 1) 将接触信号渲染/投射到与场景相同的空间帧（可见化），使其作为图像条件输入被现成策略直接消费，无需修改架构或增加触觉编码器；2) 设计低成本磁性接触传感器，由现成零件经参数化CAD-to-mold流程制造并开源；3) 在LIBERO基准（1-view/2-view）与BC-Transformer上做受控对比，验证接触信号集成方式的影响；4) 微调预训练VLA（miniVLA、π0.5）并在四个真实世界接触密集任务上评测。

Result: LIBERO 2-view下BC-Transformer成功率平均提升15.7个百分点，1-view设置类似增益；受控实验表明接触信息集成策略显著影响触觉利用效率；微调预训练VLA同样有效：miniVLA在LIBERO平均+25pp，π0.5在四个真实接触密集任务上配合自研传感器平均+30pp。

Conclusion: 无需专用触觉编码器或非图像骨干，仅改变接触信息的空间呈现方式，即可让图像条件策略（含预训练VLA）有效利用触觉，且硬件低成本、可开源复制；该结论在仿真基准与真实机器人任务、多种策略骨干上均成立。

Abstract: Integrating contact information into visuomotor policies remains an open problem. Touch is essential to robust manipulation, yet most modern policies, including pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models, operate from vision and proprioception alone. Existing approaches to closing this gap require specialized tactile hardware, add separate tactile encoders, or commit to non-image policy backbones, all incompatible with the modern paradigm of image-conditioned policies built on pretrained 2D visual representations. Our key insight is that the bottleneck is not the contact information itself, but how it is delivered: when contact signals are exposed in the same spatial frame as the scene the policy already attends to, they become directly usable by any image-conditioned policy without architectural changes. We operationalize this insight in Visible Touch, paired with a custom low-cost magnetic contact sensor that is open-sourced and fabricated from off-the-shelf parts via a parametric CAD-to-mold pipeline. Across the LIBERO benchmark, Visible Touch improves BC-Transformer success by 15.7 percentage points on average in the 2-view setting, with similar gains in the 1-view setting; controlled comparisons show that the contact-integration strategy strongly affects how effectively tactile information is used. The pattern holds when fine-tuning pretrained VLAs: miniVLA on LIBERO gains 25 percentage points on average, and $\pi_{0.5}$ on four real-world contact-rich tasks gains 30 percentage points with our custom sensor.

</details>


### [237] [GIFT: Glove-Inferred Force Transfer: Force-Aware Human-to-Robot Skill Transfer from a Wearable Sensing Glove to a Robot Hand Without Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14173)
*Tzah Sarusi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GIFT 通过物理量（牛顿）而非共享触觉硬件实现人-机器人技能迁移：人侧用数据手套标定指尖力，机器人侧用电机电流残差估计力，在杯子抓取任务中力输入使握持力中位数降低53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传感手套到机器人的技能迁移依赖共享硬件：同一只触觉手套或两个触觉传感器间的对齐学习，缺乏跨本体、跨传感模态的通用接口。本文希望把人与机器人之间接口定义为物理单位（牛顿）而非共享传感器。

Method: 构建 GIFT 流水线。数据采集阶段：可穿戴手套记录手指屈曲、标定指尖力与腕部朝向，头戴相机记录演示，无需机器人在场。部署阶段：机器人通过相对自由空间基线的执行器电流残差，经标定映射到牛顿来估计力，因此任何可上报电机电流的位置控制手均可作为平台。策略使用手套空间状态并预测手指位置目标；机器人仅通过两个标定适配器接入：重定向解码器与力估计器。用同一演示训练两个 action-chunking 策略，一个保留指尖力输入，一个将力输入置零。

Result: 在杯子抓取-保持任务上进行 50 次 rollout（样本量与指标在评分前固定）：两个策略 25/25 全部成功；有力度输入时，每次 rollout 保持阶段握持力估计中位数降低 53%（1.20 N 对 2.55 N，单侧 Mann-Whitney U，p<0.0001）。消融：纯视觉策略 0/15 抓取成功，给出手部指令状态的策略能完成抓取，力输入决定握持力度。实现从五个手套通道到七个机器人执行器的重定向映射，两侧之间不共享任何传感器。

Conclusion: 在人手上测得的力通道可无缝迁移到机器人手，无需任何触觉硬件，验证了以物理单位（牛顿）为接口的人-机器人技能迁移范式，且该范式可推广到能汇报电机电流的位置控制手。

Abstract: Human-to-robot skill transfer from sensing gloves has so far relied on shared hardware: the same tactile glove worn by the demonstrator and the robot, or a learned alignment between two tactile sensors. We present GIFT (Glove-Inferred Force Transfer), a pipeline in which the interface between human and robot is a physical unit rather than a shared sensor: fingertip force is measured in newtons on the human side and estimated in newtons on the robot side. A wearable glove records finger flexion, calibrated fingertip force, and wrist orientation, while a head-mounted camera records the demonstration; no robot is present. At deployment, the robot estimates force from actuator-current residuals relative to a free-space baseline, through a calibrated mapping to newtons, so any position-controlled hand that reports motor current can serve as the deployment platform. The policy uses a glove-space state and predicts finger-position targets; the robot enters only through two calibrated adapters, a retargeting decoder and a force estimator. We evaluate GIFT on a cup grasp-and-hold task with two action-chunking policies trained on the same demonstrations, with fingertip-force inputs retained in one and zeroed in the other. In a 50-rollout evaluation with sample size and metrics fixed before scoring, both policies succeeded in all 25 rollouts. The median of the per-rollout hold-phase grip-force estimates was 53% lower with force inputs: 1.20 N versus 2.55 N (one-sided Mann-Whitney U, p<0.0001). In an observation ablation, a vision-only policy achieved 0/15 grasps, policies given hand-command state acquired the grasp, and the force inputs determined how hard the policy held. A force channel measured on the human hand thus transfers to a robot hand with no tactile hardware, through a retargeting map from five glove channels to seven robot actuators, with no sensor shared between the two.

</details>


### [238] [DreamSat-Bench: Development and Initial Testing of a Testbed for AI-Based Pose Estimation from 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14183)
*Alex Posadas-Nava,August Berne,Giovanni Lavezzi,Kareena Shah,Alejandro Carrasco,Josiane Uwumukiza,Giacomo Battaglia,Paolo Panicucci,Minduli C. Wijayatunga,Victor Rodriguez-Fernandez,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamSat-Bench 是一个模块化交会与接近操作（RPO）测试床，整合 MuJoCo、Isaac Lab、LeRobot 与机械臂硬件在环流水线，用于基准测试基于 AI 的相对导航；并评估 DreamSat（单视图生成式 3D 重建）+ FoundationPose（零样本 6-DoF 跟踪）的端到端视觉导航管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏先验几何模型的非合作空间资产自主导航中，仿真到现实的差距难以量化，且缺少统一、可软硬件在环切换的基准平台来评测 AI 相对导航方法。

Method: 构建 DreamSat-Bench 模块化 RPO 测试床：软件在环（MuJoCo、Isaac Lab、LeRobot）与硬件在环（机械臂追踪 3D 轨迹）统一管线，支持轨道环境与光照定制；评测端到端视觉导航管线 = DreamSat 生成式单视图 3D 重建 + FoundationPose 零样本 6-DoF 位姿跟踪；开展参数化研究（重建延迟、网格分辨率、轨道距离、光照几何）与初步硬件在环验证。

Result: 在定点保持与绕飞等任务代表段上完成参数化研究，量化了重建延迟、网格分辨率、轨道距离和光照几何对位姿估计精度的影响；硬件在环初步实验定性验证了管线物理部署，识别出目标对称性与累积跟踪漂移为鲁棒导航的关键因素。

Conclusion: DreamSat-Bench 为在无先验几何模型条件下成熟化自主导航提供了严谨框架，实现了从数字仿真到物理现实的连贯过渡。

Abstract: This paper presents the development and initial testing of DreamSat-Bench, a modular rendezvous and proximity operation testbed designed to benchmark AI-based relative navigation techniques. By integrating a software- and hardware-in-the-loop robotic pipeline, the platform enables a seamless transition from digital simulation to physical reality. DreamSat-Bench unifies state-of-the-art robotic learning tools such as MuJoCo, Isaac Lab, and LeRobot into a single benchmarking platform, utilizing robotic arms to trace 3D trajectories. The platform allows for extensive customization of orbital environments and lighting to evaluate the simulation-to-reality gap. We demonstrate the testbed's utility by evaluating an end-to-end vision-based navigation pipeline that pairs DreamSat, a generative AI framework for single-view 3D reconstruction, with FoundationPose for zero-shot 6-DoF tracking of unseen spacecraft. Initial testing explores mission-representative orbital segments, including fixed-point station-keeping and fly-around characterization. Through a series of parametric studies, we quantify the impacts of reconstruction latency, mesh resolution, orbital range, and illumination geometry on pose estimation accuracy. Finally, a preliminary hardware-in-the-loop campaign qualitatively validates the physical deployment of the pipeline, identifying target symmetry and accumulated tracking drift as critical factors for robust navigation. DreamSat-Bench provides a rigorous framework for maturing autonomous navigation with unprepared space assets in the absence of prior geometric models.

</details>


### [239] [Novel Ex-vivo Calf Brain Model with Integrated Sub-Skull Force Sensors to Access Simulated Neurosurgical Procedures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14198)
*Hamad Binhammad,Matheus Ballestero,Mohammed Babgi,Seana Shaka,Nima Hemati,Rothaina Saeedi,Aiden Mazidi,Bianca Giglio,Rukun Dou,Houssem-Eddine Gueziri,Amir Hooshiar,Rolando F. Del Maestro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并验证了一种便携式离体力感知平台，在3D打印颅骨模型与新鲜牛脑组织上量化模拟神经外科中工具-组织交互力，验证显示高精度（R=0.9997，RMSE<0.005 N）、可检测9.8 mN低力且长时间稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术组织操作对精度要求极高，但真实条件下工具-组织操作力的大小极少被量化，缺乏客观的力反馈评估手段，这限制了基于力度的技能训练与绩效评价。

Method: 构建便携式离体力感知平台：新鲜牛脑组织作为脑实质生物替代物置入3D打印人颅骨模型，配6自由度力/力矩传感器与实时数据采集系统；设计五项验证协议——与真值比对的静态精度与线性（0.5–50 g 标定砝码）、最小可测力、不同解剖区域的空间一致性、手术铺巾影响、长时间稳定性。

Result: 测量力与参考载荷高度一致（R=0.9997），RMSE<0.005 N，平均相对误差<2%；可靠检测低至1 g（9.8 mN）的力；手术铺巾未引入有意义的信号失真；长时间记录漂移极小。

Conclusion: 该框架为基于新鲜牛脑组织的技能训练与绩效评估提供了客观、高保真的力量化手段，可作为其他外科手术基于力的评估基础；后续将集成临床器械提升拟真度，并推进临床试验评估可用性、教学效果与转化价值。

Abstract: Surgical tissue manipulation demands precision; however, tool-tissue manipulation force magnitudes under realistic conditions are rarely quantified. To address this gap, we proposed and validated a portable ex-vivo force-sensing platform that measures tool-tissue interaction forces across the skull-brain interface during simulated neurosurgery. The system involves fresh calf brain tissue, used as a biological surrogate for brain parenchyma, placed in a 3D-printed human skull model equipped with a 6 degree-of-freedom force/torque sensor and a real-time data acquisition system. Five validation protocols assessed the accuracy and dynamic fidelity of the platform against ground-truth measurement, static accuracy and linearity using calibrated weights (0.5-50 g), minimum detectable force, spatial consistency across different anatomical regions, effect of surgical draping, and long-duration stability. Across protocols, measured forces showed excellent agreement with reference loads (correlation R = 0.9997), with RMSE < 0.005 N and mean relative error under 2%. The platform reliably detected low-magnitude forces down to 1 g (9.8 mN), while surgical drapes introduced no meaningful signal distortion and prolonged recordings exhibited minimal drift. Overall, the proposed framework provides objective, high-fidelity force quantification for skill training and performance assessment using fresh calf brain tissue and may serve as a foundation for force-based evaluation across other surgical procedures. Future work will integrate clinically used surgical instruments to increase procedural realism and will progress toward clinical trials to evaluate usability, educational impact, and translational relevance in practice-adjacent settings.

</details>


### [240] [ITA-LaCAM: A Complete and Scalable TAPF Solver via Assignment-Aware Configuration-Space Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14208)
*Yimin Tang,Han Zhang,Shao-Hung Chan,Junsoo Kim,Erdem Bıyık,Sven Koenig,Jingkai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ITA-LaCAM 提出一种结合目标分配与多智能体路径规划的完整 TAPF 求解器，在每个联合配置节点上携带"智能体-目标"匹配，通过增量修复被移动智能体的匹配并用目标引导 PIBT 后继生成，从而避免显式枚举组合分配空间，同时保留 LaCAM 的完备性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TAPF 需要同时完成目标分配与无碰撞路径规划，两者耦合导致搜索空间组合爆炸。已有方法（如 IR-TAPF + DBS-Hungarian）在可扩展性与求解率上仍有不足，作者希望在保留 LaCAM 完备性和大规模可扩展性的同时实现自适应重分配，避免枚举整个分配空间。

Method: ITA-LaCAM 受 LaCAM 与 ITA-CBS 启发：搜索树的每个 joint-configuration 节点都附带一个 agent-to-target matching；生成后继时对被移动的智能体做增量匹配修复（类似 ITA-CBS 的增量目标分配），并用当前匹配目标引导 PIBT 的后继生成，从而在搜索过程中自适应调整分配，而不显式枚举组合分配空间。

Result: 在 8 张地图、5–200 个智能体的 9,760 个基准实例上，ITA-LaCAM 求解率 100%，而使用 DBS-Hungarian 配置的 IR-TAPF 为 95.6%；在 84.0% 的对比中更快找到初始解，在两者均求解的实例中有 65.0% 取得更低的 sum of costs。

Conclusion: 增量匹配修复 + 目标引导 PIBT 的设计有效：在不牺牲完备性的前提下实现了更好的求解率、初始解速度与解质量，并将可扩展性推广到 200 智能体规模，可作为通用 TAPF 求解框架。

Abstract: Combined Target Assignment and Path Finding (TAPF) requires assigning targets for agents while simultaneously planning collision-free paths. We present ITA-LaCAM, a complete and scalable TAPF solver inspired by LaCAM and ITA-CBS. In ITA-LaCAM, each joint-configuration node carries an agent-to-target matching. When a successor is generated, ITA-LaCAM incrementally repairs the matching for the agents that moved and uses the targets to guide PIBT successor generation. This design enables adaptive reassignment without explicitly enumerating the combinatorial assignment space, while preserving LaCAM's completeness and scalability. Across 9,760 benchmark instances on eight maps with 5--200 agents, ITA-LaCAM solved 100% of the instances, compared with 95.6% for IR-TAPF configured with DBS-Hungarian. ITA-LaCAM found an initial solution faster in 84.0% of the comparisons and achieved a lower sum of costs in 65.0% of the instances solved by both methods.

</details>


### [241] [Task-Specified Active Metrological Inspection with Measurement-Steered VLA Manipulation and Deterministic Evidence Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14219)
*Zhiling Chen,Jingzhan Ge,Ruimin Chen,Matthew P. Castanier,David Gorsich,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出任务规约式主动计量检测问题，构建FRAME层级双臂框架，将检测指令与结构化规格转化为可追溯的符合性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高混合低批量制造需检测系统适应零件、规格与工单变化，而现有检测自动化假设固定传感序列，通用机器人智能体仅优化任务完成而非计量证据的完整性与有效性。

Method: FRAME将学习式操作与标定激光轮廓测量结合：任务管理器对需求进行grounding与调度；主动表面配准验证"实物—规格"定位；证据记忆追踪测量溯源、可采信性与覆盖率。学习组件仅提议检测目标与物理接近动作，确定性基准约束测量、可采信性检查、覆盖审计与符合性评估共同阻止不完整或未验证证据授权PASS。

Result: 系列物理实验表明FRAME在端到端检测可靠性上更高、误接受更少、任务完成时间更短。

Conclusion: 通过将学习型操作与确定性计量验证分层解耦，FRAME为多变工况下的机器人检测提供了可追溯、可审计的证据驱动范式。

Abstract: High-mix low-volume (HMLV) manufacturing requires inspection systems to adapt to changing parts, specifications, and work orders without repeated task-specific programming. Existing inspection automation typically assumes predefined sensing sequences, while general purpose robot agents optimize task completion rather than the completeness and validity of metrological evidence. We formulate task-specified active metrological inspection and propose From Requirements to Admissible Metrological Evidence (FRAME), a hierarchical dual-arm framework that converts an inspection instruction and structured specification into traceable conformance evidence. FRAME coordinates learned manipulation with calibrated laser profilometry: a task manager grounds and schedules requirements, active surface correspondence verifies physical-to-specification localization, and evidence memory tracks measurement provenance, admissibility, and coverage. Learned components may propose inspection targets and physical access actions, but deterministic datum-grounded measurement, admissibility checks, coverage auditing, and conformance evaluation prevent incomplete or unverified evidence from authorizing PASS. A series of physical experiments shows that FRAME achieves higher end-to-end inspection reliability, fewer false accepts, and shorter task completion time.

</details>


### [242] [VGFM: Expressive Robot Policies via Dense Value Guidance in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14261)
*Prajwal Koirala,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VGFM 将离线 RL 策略参数化为动作空间的条件流匹配模型（x-prediction），使每个中间流步都输出合法机器人动作，从而可用标准离线 critic 直接评估并施加密集价值引导，且无需 BPTT 或蒸馏/辅助网络。在 OGBench 的运动与操纵任务上表现强劲，推理时可通过改变流 ODE 离散化步数调节算力而无需重训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表达性强的生成式多步动作策略在离线 RL 中难以改进：常需 BPTT、辅助架构或蒸馏损失，训练复杂度高、扩展性差。作者希望在不牺牲生成式策略表达力的前提下，实现简单、可扩展的价值引导式策略改进。

Method: 策略为条件流匹配模型，直接在动作空间做 x-prediction（预测干净动作），因此任意中间流时刻 t 的输出都是可直接执行的有效动作，可被标准离线 RL critic 评估得到 Q 值。价值引导因而可在随机采样的流时刻施加，无需对整条生成轨迹反传（避免 BPTT），也不需要额外网络或蒸馏损失。推理时仅改变流 ODE 的离散化步数即可在精度与算力间折衷，无需重训。

Result: 在 OGBench 的机器人运动与操纵任务上，在严格评估协议下于广泛任务范围内取得强性能；超参数调优极少，说明方法稳定且易于扩展。

Conclusion: VGFM 为长时序、目标导向的机器人控制提供了一个简单、可扩展且有效的表达性策略学习框架，验证了“动作空间流匹配 + 免 BPTT 密集价值引导”这一设计在离线 RL 中的可行性。

Abstract: Recent robot learning paradigms increasingly rely on large offline datasets of robotic interactions to train control policies. Expressive generative models enable rich and multimodal action representations, expanding the capability of this paradigm for complex robotic control. However, policy improvement with multi-step generative actors remains challenging. In offline reinforcement learning (RL), incorporating value-based objectives along generative trajectories often introduces substantial training complexity, including backpropagation through time (BPTT), auxiliary architectures, or distillation losses. We propose Value-Guided Flow Matching (VGFM), a scalable offline RL framework that enables dense value-guided shaping within a flow-based policy while avoiding BPTT and additional algorithmic overhead. VGFM parameterizes the policy as a conditional flow-matching model in action (x-prediction) space, ensuring that each intermediate flow step produces a valid robot action that can be directly evaluated by a standard offline RL critic. This design allows value guidance to be applied at randomly sampled flow times without differentiating through the entire generative trajectory, while preserving inference-time flexibility by varying the discretization of the underlying flow ODE without retraining. Evaluated on robotic locomotion and manipulation tasks in OGBench, VGFM achieves strong performance across a wide range of tasks under rigorous evaluation protocols. With minimal hyperparameter tuning, these results demonstrate that VGFM provides a simple, scalable, and effective approach for expressive policy learning in long-horizon, goal-oriented robotic control.

</details>


### [243] [Learning Communication-Conditioned Generative Policies for Decentralized Multi-Agent Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14268)
*Prajwal Koirala,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化、以通信为条件的生成式多智能体避碰框架：用全局状态下的特权离线示范训练 flow-matching 策略，生成短时域动作序列，并让智能体学习交换、聚合编码交互意图的隐层消息，实现测试期无条件独立生成与条件化协同生成，结合滚动时域推理实现单步推断并对通信丢包鲁棒。仿真接近专家水平，可泛化到更密集未知场景并零样本迁移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体避碰在部分可观测下需要协同，但集中式规划难以在真实执行时部署，且示范数据中往往不含显式通信信号。作者希望利用特权离线示范学习可去中心化执行、通信可插拔、且对丢包鲁棒的协同避碰策略。

Method: 以流匹配（flow matching）生成策略在特权全局状态示范上训练，生成短时域动作序列；训练时无显式通信标签，通过潜变量消息交换与聚合学习交互意图表征；测试期以通信为条件进行生成，无条件生成退化为独立行为；执行仅依赖局部观测与学习到的消息，配合滚动时域推理实现高效单步推断，并对通信中断具备优雅退化能力。

Result: 仿真中达到接近专家的避碰性能，对更密集、未见过的多智能体场景有较强泛化，并实现到真实机器人实验的零样本迁移；滚动时域与单步推断带来推理效率提升，通信丢包下性能平滑下降。

Conclusion: 通信条件下的生成式策略可在完全去中心化执行下实现协同避碰，且无需显式通信标注、无需集中规划，兼具泛化性与真机可迁移性，为多智能体具身系统的可扩展协同提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a decentralized communication-conditioned generative framework for multi-agent collision avoidance. Agents generate short-horizon action sequences using a flow-matching policy trained from privileged offline demonstrations with access to global state. The demonstrations do not include explicit communication signals; instead, agents learn to exchange and aggregate latent messages that encode interaction-relevant intent under partial observability. This formulation supports flexible inference at test time, where unconditioned generation corresponds to independent behavior and communication-conditioned generation enables coordinated interaction without centralized planning. The resulting policies operate in a fully decentralized manner at execution time, relying only on local observations and learned messages. Combined with a receding-horizon inference scheme, the proposed approach enables efficient single-step inference of short-horizon action sequences and degrades gracefully under communication dropouts. Extensive simulation results demonstrate near-expert collision avoidance performance and strong generalization to denser, unseen multi-agent scenarios, along with zero-shot transfer to real-robot experiments.

</details>


### [244] [Multi-Task Visual Perception Network with LLM Conditioning for Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14297)
*Praveen Kumar,K. R. Guruprasad,Tushar Sandhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种交互式导航框架，用LLM解析文本/语音指令，基于局部视觉线索和自我中心几何指令生成顺序动作计划，摆脱对全局一致地图的依赖，以最小漂移累积策略实现长期导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人长期导航中里程计漂移与传感器误差不断累积，2D地图逐步退化，导致A*、RRT*、DiPPer、ViT-A*等传统路径规划算法随时间失效；同时维护全局一致地图开销大，需要一种消除全局地图依赖的方案。

Method: 融合视觉感知与LLM，通过文本或语音输入解释用户命令；不依赖易漂移的全局地图，而是根据局部视觉线索与自我中心几何指令生成顺序动作计划（sequential action plan）；按序执行各动作，并相对即时目标重置定位，形成最小累积漂移策略。

Result: 在真实世界与仿真数据上的实验显示，相比其他方法有显著提升；可实现准确、高效、无碰撞的导航，且无需传统建图的维护开销。源码已公开。

Conclusion: 以"局部感知+LLM指令理解+序列动作执行"替代全局地图范式，可在已知与未知环境中安全长期导航，为服务机器人长期自主性提供了一条低维护成本的实用路径。

Abstract: Long-term navigation for service robots faces crit- ical challenges like the accumulation of odometry drift and sensor error, which progressively degrade 2D maps and renders traditional path planning algorithms (e.g., A*, RRT*, DiPPer, ViT-A*) ineffective over time. To address this, we propose a user-friendly, interactive framework that eliminates the reliance on globally consistent maps. Our approach integrates visual perception with Large Language Models (LLM) to interpret user commands via text or voice. Instead of relying on a drift- prone global map, the system generates a sequential action plan based on local visual cues and egocentric geometric instructions. These action plans are executed sequentially, allowing the robot to navigate known and unknown environments safely. By reset- ting localization relative to immediate targets, our framework effectively works with a minimum accumulation drift strategy, ensuring accurate, efficient, and collision-free navigation without the maintenance overhead of traditional mapping. Experiments on real-world and simulated data have shown significant improve- ments over other methods. Our source code is publicly accessible at this https URL .

</details>


### [245] [VLBiMan++: Expanding the Generalization Boundary of Vision-Language Anchored One-Shot Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14310)
*Huayi Zhou,Wei Gao,Yiyang Han,Kui Jia,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLBiMan++ 提出以单次人类演示为起点的双臂操作框架，通过任务感知分解提取可复用技能组件，并以视觉-语言锚定的几何适配实现无重训练的技能迁移，在任务、物体、场景、本体与长时部署五个维度上系统扩展泛化边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用双臂操作若依赖大规模遥操作数据与反复策略重训练，成本高昂。作者希望构建一种可复用的任务先验，使其能跨任务、物体、场景、本体与执行条件持续保持有效，从而摆脱数据与训练的成本瓶颈。

Method: 从单次人类演示出发，进行任务感知分解，识别可复用且可适配的技能组件；采用视觉-语言锚定的几何适配，将这些技能迁移到新配置而无需重训练。为支撑更广范围，引入物体状态感知适配与轻量级轨迹优化，以应对超出简单刚体6-DoF位姿变化的情形，并保持双臂协调的可靠性。

Result: 大量真实世界实验表明，VLBiMan++ 在任务构成、未见类别与复杂形变/关节物体、杂乱遮挡与动态干扰场景、异构双臂平台以及重复外部扰动下的长时闭环执行等日益严苛设定中，仍能保持较强的任务成功率与适配能力。

Conclusion: VLBiMan++ 将单次演示的双臂操作从孤立的可迁移性展示，推进为一个覆盖任务、物体、场景、本体与长期部署条件的更系统、可扩展的泛化框架。

Abstract: Generalizable bimanual robotic manipulation requires a reusable task prior that can persist across increasingly diverse tasks, objects, scenes, embodiments, and execution conditions, thus avoiding the prohibitive cost of large-scale teleoperated demonstrations and policy retraining. In this work, we present VLBiMan++, an extended framework that expands the generalization boundary of vision-language anchored one-shot bimanual manipulation. Starting from a single human demonstration, VLBiMan++ performs task-aware decomposition to identify reusable and adaptable skill components, and employs vision-language grounded geometric adaptation to transfer these skills to novel configurations without retraining. Building on this foundation, we systematically extend generalization along five dimensions: task generalization through diverse and long-horizon skill compositions; object generalization across unseen categories, varying geometries, and more complex articulated or deformable objects; scene generalization under clutter, occlusion, and dynamic interference; embodiment generalization across heterogeneous dual-arm robotic platforms; and deployment generalization through prolonged closed-loop execution under repeated external perturbations. To support this broader scope, we further introduce object-state-aware adaptation and lightweight trajectory optimization mechanisms that accommodate changes beyond simple rigid 6-DoF pose variations while preserving reliable bimanual coordination. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that VLBiMan++ maintains strong task success and adaptation capability across these increasingly challenging settings. Overall, VLBiMan++ advances one-shot bimanual manipulation from demonstrating isolated transferability toward a more systematic and scalable framework for generalization across tasks, objects, scenes, embodiments, and long-term deployment conditions.

</details>


### [246] [BIG-CBF: Behavior-Imagination-Guided Control Barrier Function with Shared Uncertainty for Mobile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14343)
*Shibo Li,Zhongcheng Wang,Jiahe Cao,Jianhua Yang,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BIG-CBF双速率导航架构：低频段想象并评估六种闭环反馈行为，高频段用共享不确定性的CBF安全滤波，兼顾任务推进与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小干预CBF滤波器缺乏任务级机动意识，在几何歧义环境中可能选错避让方向，导致"安全但停滞"的行为。

Method: 低频机动选择＋高频CBF滤波的双速率架构；基于短时域内六个闭环反馈行为的想象，用解析CBF兼容性加轻量目标（任务进度、冻结、平滑、切换）评估；imagining与执行层共享相对运动延迟、障碍预测、零阶保持运动、指令执行残差等不确定性来源；硬CBF为最终安全裁决。

Result: 3,600回合、9场景对比基准中总体任务成功率99.78%（最高），并显著降低下游CBF干预；Jetson Orin Nano机载的全向实体机器人15次评估运行无接触事件；与无共享变体的匹配硬件对比显示CBF干预能量与激活频率更低。

Conclusion: 共享不确定性使机动选择与安全关键执行保持一致，可在不牺牲安全的前提下缓解CBF滤波的停滞问题，实现可靠的实体导航。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) provide a mathematically grounded framework for enforcing local collision-avoidance constraints in autonomous mobile robots, commonly through optimization-based safety filters. However, a minimum-intervention CBF filter lacks task-level maneuver awareness and may fail to select a productive avoidance direction when multiple distinct maneuvers are locally viable, leading to safe but stalled behavior in geometrically ambiguous environments. This paper presents BIG-CBF, Behavior-Imagination-Guided Control Barrier Function with shared uncertainty, a two-rate navigation architecture that separates low-rate maneuver selection from high-rate safety filtering. Over a short horizon, six closed-loop feedback behaviors are imagined and evaluated using analytic CBF compatibility together with a lightweight objective accounting for task progress, freezing, smoothness, and switching. To reduce planning-execution mismatch, the imagination and execution layers share consistent uncertainty sources for relative-motion delay, obstacle prediction, zero-order-hold motion, and command-execution residuals, while a hard CBF remains the final safety authority. In a 3,600-episode comparative benchmark across nine scenarios, BIG-CBF achieves the highest overall task success rate of 99.78% while substantially reducing downstream CBF intervention. On a physical omnidirectional robot with onboard Jetson Orin Nano computation, BIG-CBF completes all 15 evaluation runs without a recorded contact event. Matched hardware comparisons against the non-shared variant further show lower CBF intervention energy and activation frequency, supporting improved consistency between maneuver selection and safety-critical execution.

</details>


### [247] [Orientation Control of Soft Robots via Adiabatic Spectral Submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14376)
*Aron Karakai,Roshan S. Kaundinya,Mike Yan Michelis,Robert Katzschmann,George Haller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将绝热谱子流形（aSSM）的辨识扩展到增广可观测量数据集，并升级 aSSM-MPC 方案；在压力驱动软臂的高保真有限元仿真上，位置与姿态跟踪误差相比现有数据驱动基线降低 60% 以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人在安全关键的精细交互中需要精确的位置与姿态控制，而模型预测控制（MPC）依赖既准确又计算廉价的模型；软体机器人本质上是无限维非线性动力学系统，精确模型的构建困难。近期 aSSM 理论提供了数据驱动的降维途径，但已有 aSSM-MPC 在可观测量利用与控制性能上仍受限。

Method: 基于绝热谱子流形理论，将 aSSM 辨识从原有可观测数据集扩展到增广可观测量（非线性可观测函数构成的高维特征空间）；据此重构数据驱动的低维降阶模型，并升级现有 aSSM-MPC 控制器，使其在降阶坐标下进行预测与优化控制。

Result: 在压力驱动软臂的高保真有限元仿真中，所提控制器的位置与姿态跟踪误差较现有数据驱动基线下降超过 60%。

Conclusion: 增广可观测量与升级后的 aSSM-MPC 能显著提升软体机器人位置/姿态控制精度，验证了 aSSM 降维在软体控制中的建模与控制双重价值；但验证仅限单一有限元仿真，尚缺实机与多场景泛化证据。

Abstract: Soft robots are commonly sought for safety-critical interactions in delicate environments, where accurate position and orientation control is imperative. Model predictive control (MPC) offers a solution, but it requires a model of the robot's infinite-dimensional nonlinear dynamics that is at once accurate and computationally cheap. Recent theory on adiabatic spectral submanifolds (aSSMs) and their applications to soft robots provide data-driven model-reduction methods to construct such models. Here, we extend these methods to identify aSSMs from enlarged observable datasets and upgrade the currently available aSSM-MPC schemes. Evaluated on a high-fidelity finite-element simulation of a pressure-actuated soft arm, our controller reduces position and orientation tracking error by more than 60% compared to existing data-driven baselines.

</details>


### [248] [Learning-Based Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance for a UAV Using Only Three Range Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14426)
*Mohammad Reza Ranjbar Divkoti,A. Pedro Aguiar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种行为栅格地图与PPO深度强化学习协同设计的框架，使仅具备偏航单自由度、类似固定翼的非完整约束无人机，在未知动态环境和极端部分可观测条件下，仅用三个低成本方向测距传感器即可实现静态与动态障碍实时避障和目标到达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在未知动态环境中飞行时面临极端部分可观测、仅偏航单自由度的非完整约束，且需避免依赖LiDAR或视觉等昂贵感知手段，要求用极稀疏的低成本测距信息实现实时避障与导航。

Method: 将行为栅格地图表示与深度强化学习结合，采用PPO进行连续控制的稳定策略学习；核心是状态表示与控制策略的协同设计，行为栅格地图将稀疏测距测量动态聚合成结构化局部表示，支撑实时避障与目标到达决策。

Result: 在不同规模与障碍密度的仿真中，所提标准与增强方法成功率高于PPO变体和MPC：小尺度高拥挤场景94% vs 79-90%，大尺度高拥挤场景83% vs 62-71%，且保持实时性；四个真实场景实验验证了实际可行性，均能稳定到达目标且无碰撞。

Conclusion: 稀疏测距传感与结构化局部地图表示和强化学习策略的协同设计，可在强约束、部分可观测条件下实现可靠无人机避障导航，并在仿真与真实实验中验证了有效性与实时性。

Abstract: We present a learning-based approach to kinodynamic online motion planning for an Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) operating at a fixed altitude in unknown dynamic environments, where real-time avoidance of both static and dynamic obstacles must be achieved under conditions of extreme partial observability. The UAV is controlled with a single degree of freedom (yaw only), resulting in constrained, nonholonomic motion similar to fixed-wing platforms. The proposed framework integrates a behavior grid map representation with Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL), using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) for stable policy learning in continuous control. The key idea is the co-design of a state representation and control policy that enables reliable navigation using only three low-cost directional range sensors, without reliance on dense sensing modalities such as LiDAR or vision-based systems. The behavior grid map dynamically aggregates sparse measurements into a structured local representation that supports real-time decision-making for obstacle avoidance and target reaching. Extensive simulations across environments of varying sizes and obstacle densities demonstrate that the proposed standard and enhanced methods achieve higher success rates than PPO variants and Model Predictive Control (MPC) (94\% vs. 79--90\% in small-scale high-congestion scenarios, and 83\% vs. 62--71\% in large-scale high-congestion scenarios), while maintaining real-time performance. Real-world experiments across four scenarios further confirm practical feasibility, with consistent target-reaching behaviour and no collisions under the tested conditions.

</details>


### [249] [EMoG: Emotion-Modulated Gait Generation for Expressive Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14432)
*Yi Lu,Tianhao Jiang,Honglong Tian,Yumeng Zhang,Qingrui Zhao,Zhengtao Wang,Xiao-Xiao Long,Qiu Shen,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EMoG：一种情绪调制的人形机器人步态生成框架，通过可连续调节强度的情绪风格编码驱动轻量MLP实时生成周期步态轨迹，并由统一RL策略跟踪执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人运动系统主要关注稳定性与任务执行，缺乏将表达性（情绪）与显式运动控制结合的能力；如何在保持指令跟踪的同时实现可参数化的情绪化步态是人机交互中的空白。

Method: 1) 引入具有连续可调强度的情绪-风格码；2) 以该码与物理指令为条件，用轻量MLP实时生成表达性且与指令一致的周期步态轨迹；3) 通过统一的强化学习策略跟踪轨迹以完成物理执行；4) 采集专业表演者的大规模情绪标注步态数据集，并构建自动化流水线提取物理一致的周期步态循环；5) 集成基于LLM的解析器，将自由语言转换为情绪风格与运动参数，实现交互式控制。

Result: 实验显示可实现连续的步态风格调制，产生可感知的表达性线索，同时保持指令跟踪性能。

Conclusion: EMoG为人机交互提供了参数化情绪风格行走的实用方法，将表达性与显式运动控制统一到同一框架中。

Abstract: Existing humanoid locomotion systems primarily focus on stability and task execution, while integrating expressiveness with explicit locomotion control remains challenging. We propose EMoG, an emotion-modulated gait generation framework for expressive humanoid locomotion. EMoG introduces an emotional-style code with continuously adjustable intensity. Conditioned on this code and physical commands, a lightweight MLP generates expressive, command-consistent periodic gait trajectories in real time, which are tracked by a unified reinforcement learning policy for physical execution. To support training, we collect a large-scale emotion-annotated gait dataset from professional performers and develop an automated pipeline to extract physically consistent periodic gait cycles. EMoG also integrates an LLM-based parser that converts free-form language into emotional style and motion parameters for interactive control. Experiments demonstrate continuous gait-style modulation with perceptible expressive cues while maintaining command tracking. EMoG provides a practical approach to parameterized emotional-style walking for human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [250] [EcoBoat: Design and Experimental Validation of an Autonomous Body-Board Boat For Cleaning Water Bodies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14450)
*M. Aman Ansari,Saifullah Khan,Rahul Kulkarni,PB Sujit*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EcoBoat：基于改装船体的低成本自主水面清洁载具，室内用超声波+随机游走/边界跟随，室外用GPS电子围栏+随机游走；核心设计为受特斯拉阀启发的收集筐，前进时进屑、转向/制动时不外泄；泳池与湖泊实地实验验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 清理泳池、湖泊等水体漂浮垃圾通常依赖大量人工或成本高昂、传感器密集的机器人系统，缺乏低成本、可在室内外通用部署的自动化方案。

Method: 在改装船体上搭建低成本无人水面艇；室内环境以超声波传感器检测边界与障碍，采用随机游走结合边界跟随策略；室外环境以GPS电子围栏约束范围并配合随机游走实现区域覆盖；关键硬件为受特斯拉阀（无运动部件单向流动）启发的收集筐，实现前进吸入、转向/制动时封闭防逃逸。

Result: 在泳池与湖泊开展实地实验，通过迭代改进验证设计；结果表明极简的感知与计算方案（超声/GPS + 随机游走）即可在多种水体中实现有效的垃圾收集。

Conclusion: 面向水面漂浮垃圾清理的极简感知-计算范式具备可行性，低成本自主水面载具可兼顾室内与室外水体，特斯拉阀式被动收集机构是提升收集效率的关键设计。

Abstract: Cleaning water bodies such as swimming pools and lakes typically demands significant manual effort or reliance on costly, sensor-intensive robotic systems. This paper presents EcoBoat, a low-cost autonomous surface vehicle built on a modified hull, designed to collect floating debris in both indoor and outdoor water bodies. For indoor environments, EcoBoat uses ultrasonic sensors to detect boundaries and obstacles, combining random-walk motion with boundary-following behavior. For outdoor environments, it relies on GPS-based geofencing paired with a random-walk strategy for area coverage. A key design feature is a Tesla-valve-inspired collection basket that allows debris intake during forward motion while preventing its escape during turning or braking. Field experiments in pools and lakes validated the design through iterative refinement, demonstrating that a minimalist sensing and computation approach can achieve effective, versatile debris collection across diverse water bodies.

</details>


### [251] [Fault Diagnosis for Underwater Vehicles using Moving Horizon Estimation and Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14539)
*Fotis Panetsos,Kostas J. Kyriakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于模型的水下航行器执行器故障检测与诊断框架，用移动视界估计器(MHE)估计包含未建模动态与故障的集总扰动，用高斯过程(GP)预测未建模动态的均值与不确定性，通过广义似然比检验(GLRT)评估残差，实现故障检测、隔离与幅值定量估计，并在实验室水箱中完成开环与闭环实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下航行器的故障诊断常因未建模动态（水动力非线性、环境扰动）与故障效应耦合而误报、漏报；已有方法多将二者混为一谈，缺乏对未建模动态显式建模与不确定性量化的能力，导致诊断鲁棒性不足。

Method: MHE在线估计集总扰动；GP以工况相关数据拟合未建模动态，输出均值与预测方差（不确定性）；在线运行时对"MHE集总扰动估计 − GP预测"的残差做GLRT统计检验，从而分离故障与未建模动态，实现故障检测、隔离及幅值定量估计；实验室水箱中在开环与闭环控制下进行实验验证。

Result: 实验验证表明该方法在开环与闭环条件下均能可靠完成故障检测与隔离，并对故障幅值给出较准确的定量估计，且对未建模动态具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式解耦未建模动态与故障效应的MHE+GP+GLRT框架可提升水下航行器故障诊断的鲁棒性与准确性，具备实际平台可部署性；后续可扩展到多类故障、更强非线性与真实水域环境。

Abstract: This work proposes a model-based fault detection and diagnosis framework for underwater vehicles subject to actuator faults that explicitly accounts for the presence of unmodeled dynamics. To this end, a Moving Horizon Estimator (MHE) is developed to estimate the lumped disturbance, capturing both unmodeled and fault effects. Gaussian Processes (GPs) are employed to approximate the unmodeled dynamics, providing predictions of the corresponding mean and uncertainty across diverse operating conditions. During online operation, the residual between the MHE lumped disturbance estimate and the GP prediction is evaluated using a Generalized Likelihood Ratio Test. By incorporating GP-based predictions within the diagnostic framework, robustness to unmodeled dynamics is achieved, enabling effective fault detection and isolation as well as accurate quantitative estimation of fault magnitude. The proposed methodology is experimentally validated in a laboratory water tank, demonstrating reliable diagnostic performance under both open-loop and closed-loop control.

</details>


### [252] [NavPatch: Evidence-Guided Object-Level Costmap Correction with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14543)
*Shiji Sun,Xingyu Tao,Hao Wang,Ling Wang,Zhengyi Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavPatch 以视觉语言模型周期性理解场景，在物体层面为导航相关类别生成 ADD/REMOVE/EXTEND 修正补丁，并结合 LiDAR/RGB-D 三维支撑与跨帧维护机制，提升移动机器人避障导航的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何地图的障碍物表示与"物体应如何影响导航"不总一致：低矮线缆被漏检、柔性帘布造成虚假阻塞、交通锥需要比观测足迹更大的排斥区域，因此需要在物体语义层面对几何地图做修正。

Method: 用开放词汇定位（vision-language model grounding）定位物体实例，LiDAR 与 RGB-D 提供三维支撑；为导航相关物体类别分配 ADD、REMOVE、EXTEND 三类修正；通过观测质量过滤与跨帧维护决定补丁的提交、替换或撤销。

Result: 在 5 种布局、50 次真机试验中总体成功率达 86.0%；四组配置共 200 次运行的消融实验显示，相比仅用当前观测的更新方式，成功率由 70.0% 提升至 86.0%，错误提交率由 68.4% 降至 40.7%。

Conclusion: 在几何地图之上引入物体级语义修正层，可有效弥合"几何障碍表示"与"导航语义需求"之间的差距，且跨帧维护是降低误提交的关键。

Abstract: Mobile robots typically rely on geometric maps for obstacle avoidance and path planning, but the resulting obstacle representation does not always match how an object should affect navigation. A low lying cable may be missed, a flexible curtain may create spurious blockage, and a traffic cone may require an exclusion region larger than its observed footprint. We present NavPatch, an object level correction layer that assigns ADD, REMOVE, or EXTEND to navigation relevant object categories through periodic scene understanding with a vision-language model. Open vocabulary grounding localizes object instances, and LiDAR and RGB-D observations provide 3D support. Observation quality filtering and cross frame maintenance determine when each correction patch is committed, replaced, or revoked. In 50 real robot trials across five layouts, NavPatch achieves an overall success rate of 86.0%. An ablation study of four configurations with 200 runs in total shows that NavPatch improves the success rate from 70.0% to 86.0% and reduces the false commit rate from 68.4% to 40.7% compared with updates based only on the current observation.

</details>


### [253] [Language-Grounded Semantic Target Navigation for Autonomous Surface Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14558)
*Yuqing Lin,Youngroung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SGNav框架，使自主水面艇（ASV）能依据操作员的自然语言描述在港口环境中识别并接近目标，通过文本语义接地、港口感知候选过滤、CLIP语义验证与PPO闭环控制实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASV导航方法主要执行预定义的几何目标或特定任务目标，缺乏对语言描述目标的接地能力；而港口/港湾场景中操作员更倾向用语言描述导航目标而非固定坐标或ID，因此需要语言接地的目标指定与接近能力。

Method: SGNav集成五个模块：(1) 文本引导的语义接地；(2) 港口感知候选过滤以抑制视觉/语义干扰物；(3) 基于CLIP的语义验证；(4) 接地的目标控制状态构建，将选中目标转换为紧凑的控制导向表示；(5) 基于PPO的闭环控制，在机载RGB观测上完成目标定位与接近。

Result: 仿真港口环境中，三类代表性目标到达任务成功率达97.0±1.2%、92.0±1.5%、90.0±1.8%，语义目标准确率>97%，错目标率<3%；在留出港口布局上保持97.7–98.7%成功率。Task 3消融显示，移除港口感知过滤或语义一致性后成功率分别降至40.4±2.6%和50.4±3.1%。

Conclusion: 语义接地、港口感知过滤与语义验证对可靠的语言接地ASV导航至关重要；该感知到控制框架可支持复杂港口环境下的语言接地目标接近机动。

Abstract: Autonomous Surface Vehicles (ASVs) are increasingly expected to operate in ports and harbour environments, where operators may specify navigation targets through language-based descriptions rather than predefined coordinates or fixed target identifiers. However, existing ASV navigation methods mainly execute predefined geometric goals or task-specific objectives and give limited attention to language-grounded target specification. This study proposes Semantically Grounded Navigation (SGNav), a framework that enables an ASV to identify and approach a maritime target from an operator-provided description. SGNav integrates text-guided semantic grounding, harbour-aware candidate filtering, CLIP-based semantic verification, grounded target control-state construction, and Proximal Policy Optimisation-based closed-loop control. It grounds the target description in onboard RGB observations, suppresses visually or semantically irrelevant distractors, and converts the selected target into a compact control-oriented representation for policy execution. Experiments in simulated port environments show that SGNav achieves success rates of $97.0\pm1.2\%$, $92.0\pm1.5\%$, and $90.0\pm1.8\%$ across three representative target-reaching tasks, with semantic target accuracy above $97\%$ and wrong-target rates below $3\%$. SGNav also maintains $97.7$--$98.7\%$ success rates across held-out port layouts. In the Task~3 ablation study, removing harbour-aware filtering or semantic consistency reduces the success rate to $40.4\pm2.6\%$ and $50.4\pm3.1\%$, respectively. These findings demonstrate the importance of semantic grounding, harbour-aware filtering, and semantic verification for reliable language-grounded ASV navigation. These results indicate that the proposed perception-to-control framework can support language-grounded target approach manoeuvres of ASV under the complex port environments.

</details>


### [254] [GLAM: Training a latent world model over global spatiotemporal memory for active exploration and navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14561)
*I-Tak Ieong,Ruizhi Feng,Zhaoyang Lu,Yifei Cao,Jiayao Zhao,Leon Li,Senhua Zhu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GLAM：面向目标条件的全局时空潜世界模型，预测未来地图表征与机器人中心航点潜变量，配套 GLAM NAV 导航系统，在 HM3D-ObjectNav 复现设定上优于 BSC-Nav 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动探索与语义导航要求具身智能体从部分观测构建记忆、预测空间记忆演化如何支持未来运动，并将预测转化为可执行计划。现有方法往往依赖 RGB 重建或分离的表征空间，难以在同一表征中同时推断未来空间上下文与导航意图。

Method: 采用 JEPA 式潜空间预测范式，直接在全局时空地图级潜 token 上训练目标条件世界模型 GLAM：输入历史地图 token、导航目标与当前机器人位姿，联合预测未来地图表征和机器人中心航点潜变量。使用预训练航点编解码器对导航计划进行监督与解码，构成完整系统 GLAM NAV。训练数据通过回放 Habitat 中 HM3D v0.2 场景上的 ObjectNav 专家轨迹并切分为多时间尺度样本获得。

Result: 在受控的 HM3D-ObjectNav 子集复现设置下，GLAM NAV 在成功率（SR）与路径长度加权成功率（SPL）两项指标上均优于复现的 BSC-Nav 基线。

Conclusion: 工作表明在全局时空地图潜空间中进行联合未来预测与意图推断可有效支撑目标条件导航，属于世界模型与具身导航交叉方向的积极探索，但当前证据限于仿真复现设定，缺少真实机器人验证与更大规模泛化评估。

Abstract: Active exploration and semantic navigation require an embodied agent to build memory from partial observations, predict how the evolution of observed spatial memory may support future motion, and convert that prediction into actionable plans. We present GLAM, a goal-conditioned latent world model trained over global spatiotemporal memory, and GLAM NAV, the complete navigation system built around it. Given historical map tokens, a navigation goal, and the current robot pose, GLAM jointly predicts future map representations and robot-centric waypoint latents, allowing future spatial context and navigation intent to be inferred in a shared representation space. The model follows a JEPA-like latent prediction paradigm, operates directly on map-level latent tokens rather than RGB reconstruction, and uses a pretrained waypoint encoder-decoder to supervise and decode navigation plans within GLAM NAV. Training data are collected by replaying ObjectNav expert trajectories in Habitat over HM3D v0.2 scene assets and slicing them into multi-timescale prediction samples. On a controlled HM3D-ObjectNav subset reproduction setting, GLAM NAV improves over a reproduced BSC-Nav baseline in both success rate and success weighted by path length.

</details>


### [255] [Learning Multi-Agent Task Assignment and Navigation in the Factory: from Simulation to Real Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14567)
*Abdalwhab Bakheet Mohamed Abdalwhab,Giovanni Beltrame,David St-Onge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FMAPPO，将2D LiDAR与任务状态特征融合，实现多机器人多机床看护的分散式MARL部署，并完成仿真到现实全流程验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习在工业多机器人系统上的真实部署仍具挑战，现有方法在安全分散决策、传感融合与真实环境鲁棒性方面缺乏系统性验证。

Method: FMAPPO融合2D LiDAR测量与任务状态信息，基于MAPPO改进为分散式策略，输出多机器人任务分配与导航动作；构建高保真仿真到ROS2再到物理移动机械臂平台的完整sim-to-real流水线，并分析指令更新频率对策略的敏感性。

Result: 仿真中FMAPPO显著优于MAPPO与SMAPPO：零件配送提升106%/21%，零件收集提升48%/11%，机床利用率提升31/10个百分点，碰撞降低18%/15%，安全分提升14/6个百分点；真实物理平台上多机器人可协同服务多机床并保持安全运行。

Conclusion: 特征融合式分散MARL在工业多机器人场景中具备真实可部署性，且策略对命令更新频率敏感，需在部署时权衡控制频率与安全性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown considerable promise for robotic decision-making, yet deploying multi-agent RL (MARL) on physical multi-robot systems in industrial environments remains challenging. This paper investigates the real-world applicability of decentralized MARL for multi-robot multi-machine tending. We propose Feature-fusion Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (FMAPPO), which fuses 2D LiDAR measurements with task-specific state information to enable safe decentralized multi-robot task assignment and navigation. A complete simulation-to-reality pipeline was developed using high-fidelity robotic simulation and ROS2 and deployed on physical mobile-manipulator platforms operating under realistic real-world conditions, with the robotic arms disabled during the experiments. We further investigate the sensitivity of the learned policy to command update frequency, an important consideration for real-world deployment. Comparative evaluation in simulation demonstrated that FMAPPO significantly outperformed state-of-the-art baselines with a large effect size, achieving improvements of 106\% and 21\% in parts delivery and 48\% and 11\% in parts collection over MAPPO and SMAPPO, respectively. FMAPPO also increased machine utilization by 31 and 10 percentage points, respectively, while reducing collisions by 18\% and 15\% and increasing the safety score by 14 and 6 percentage points compared with MAPPO and SMAPPO, respectively. Furthermore, real-world experiments demonstrated that the learned decentralized policies can coordinate multiple robots to service multiple machines while maintaining safe operation under real-world sensing and control constraints. Videos of the real-world experiment are available online this https URL .

</details>


### [256] [Robotic Servo Tracking of Moving Targets with Dynamic Imitation Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14589)
*Yazhe Luo,Sipu Ruan,Yifei Li,Diansheng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于模仿轨迹约束的伺服跟踪方法,通过时间可伸缩变形与形状/幅值调制实时生成约束轨迹,实现移动目标的动态避障与高精度收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉伺服跟踪通过视觉残差到控制速度的映射实现快速响应,但对中间运动过程约束弱,导致轨迹不连续、振荡或行为保守,难以施加显式轨迹约束。

Method: 建立机器人接近移动目标的动态模型并分析收敛性;引入时间可伸缩变形机制与含形状、幅值分量的轨迹调制,实时生成一系列轨迹,并自适应确定跟踪点形成动态约束;由目标位姿微分或跟踪关键特征计算机器人速度以跟随约束轨迹。

Result: 仿真与真实实验表明,相比多种先进方法,该方法在复杂环境中能实现动态避障与高精度收敛。

Conclusion: 模仿轨迹约束为视觉伺服提供了对中间运动过程的显式约束能力,兼顾响应速度与轨迹连续性,适用于移动目标跟踪下的动态避障与精确收敛。

Abstract: Imposing explicit trajectory constraints in robot visual servoing remains challenging. Existing tracking methods achieve fast responses by mapping visual residuals to control velocities, but they have weak constraints on the intermediate motion process, which lead to trajectory discontinuity, oscillation, or conservative behaviors. To enable constrained tracking for moving targets, this paper proposes a servo tracking method based on imitation trajectory constraints. A dynamic model describing the robot approaching a moving target is formulated and analyzed for convergence. A time-scalable deformation mechanism and a trajectory modulation incorporating shape and amplitude components are introduced to generate a series of trajectories in real time, from which tracking points are adaptively determined to form dynamic constraints. The robot velocity is then computed from target pose differentials or tracked key features to follow the constrained trajectory. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed method can achieve dynamic obstacle avoidance and high-precision convergence compared with several state-of-the-art methods in complex environments.

</details>


### [257] [REVOLVE: An Automated Closed-Loop Framework for Evolving Robot Manipulation with Minimal Human Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14633)
*Hanyu Liu,Qian Li,Yizhu Ding,Jiayi Wen,Keqiang Ren,Yunsheng Ma,Tao Jian,Zhihua Wang,Zhuofan Yu,Xinran Li,Zhigong Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REVOLVE是一个自动化闭环机器人操作进化框架，整合数据采集、策略训练部署、故障恢复与持续学习，通过自动重置与纠正（ARC）及双循环进化（DLE）机制，在四个真实操作任务中5轮迭代将策略成功率提升18.5%、智能体判断准确率提升8.5%，人力投入降低约94%–95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界机器人策略部署仍高度依赖人工进行失败评估、纠正和环境重置，且模型难以从失败与纠正经验中持续学习。作者希望构建最小人工干预的闭环进化系统，让策略与监督模型能不断累积并利用执行经验。

Method: 基于统一软件平台构建闭环工作流，包含：（1）Automated Reset and Collection（ARC）架构，自动重置环境并在策略失败时介入纠正；（2）Dual-Loop Evolution（DLE），将真实交互与失败—纠正数据回灌策略学习，并利用外部错配记忆（mismatch memory）精炼智能体判断。二者协同实现策略与智能体的持续共同进化。

Result: 在四个真实操作任务的实验表明：经过五次迭代后，策略平均成功率提升18.5%，智能体判断准确率提升8.5%；数据采集与部署测试的人力投入分别减少94.4%和95.1%。

Conclusion: REVOLVE将真实世界部署转变为闭环学习过程，可持续累积并利用执行经验，以大幅减少人工干预的方式实现策略与监督模型的持续进化。

Abstract: Recent advances in data-driven robot manipulation policies have substantially improved task execution and generalization. However, real-world deployment still relies heavily on humans for failure assessment, correction, and environment reset, while models often fail to continually learn from failures and corrective experience. We present REVOLVE (Robot Evolving via Orchestrated Loops, Verification, and Experience), an automated closed-loop framework for evolving robot manipulation with minimal human intervention. Built on a unified software platform, REVOLVE integrates data collection, policy training and deployment, failure recovery, and continual learning into a single closed-loop workflow. Its Automated Reset and Collection (ARC) architecture automatically resets the environment and intervenes to correct policy failures. Dual-Loop Evolution (DLE) continually improves the manipulation policy and agent by feeding real-world interaction and failure--correction data back into policy learning and using an external mismatch memory to refine agent judgments. Experiments across four real-world manipulation tasks show that, after five iterations, REVOLVE improves average policy success rate by 18.5% and agent judgment accuracy by 8.5%, while reducing human effort in data collection and deployment testing by 94.4% and 95.1%, respectively. These results demonstrate that REVOLVE transforms real-world deployment into a closed-loop learning process that continually accumulates and uses execution experience, enabling continual evolution of both the policy and supervisory model with substantially less human intervention.

</details>


### [258] [Skill Composition for Legged Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14647)
*Daniel Gigliotti,Flavio Maiorana,Fabio Patrizi,Luca Iocchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文主张将"独立子策略的可靠组合(composition)"确立为独立研究问题,而非实现细节;其核心洞见在于:若能随时安全地切换控制权,则"下一步做什么"可交给规划器/自动机/符号控制器等可预先验证的组件,策略只负责"执行",从而使机器人行为可信且可验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单技能控制器训练快、收敛稳定、可独立验证,而端到端单一策略难以覆盖全部行为;真正脆弱的环节是技能之间的切换/衔接,现有工作把它当作随手可用的实现细节来对待,缺少系统研究。

Method: 立场/观点型论文(推测):把sub-policy组合形式化为研究问题,探讨在任意时刻安全移交控制权的机制,并论证此时高层决策可由性质不同的组件(planner、automaton、symbolic controller)承担。

Result: 摘要为论点陈述,未给出实验数据、具体算法或系统实现,仅有概念性论证与主张。

Conclusion: 可靠的组合机制是把零散技能变成可复用、可扩展、可共享技能库的关键;若控制权移交可验证,策略仅需承担"行动"角色,机器人可信行为将变得可验证。

Abstract: Robots, and humanoid robots in particular, are increasingly competent at individual behaviors, each obtained by training a specialized controller. A specialized skill is quick to train, converges reliably because the problem it faces is narrow, and can be validated on its own, none of which is true of a single end-to-end policy asked to cover everything. What remains fragile is the transition between them. We argue that the composition of independent sub-policies deserves to be treated as a research problem in its own right, rather than as an implementation detail left to whatever mechanism happens to be at hand. Reliable composition is what turns a collection of separate skills into a repertoire that can be used, extended and shared. More fundamentally, if control can be passed between specialized policies safely, and at any moment, the choice of what the robot should do next can be delegated to a component of an entirely different nature, such as a planner, an automaton or a symbolic controller, whose behavior can be inspected in advance. The policies would then only ever have to act, and what the robot can be trusted to do would become verifiable.

</details>


### [259] [A Novel Robot-Assisted Learning Pedagogy for Children with ASD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14710)
*Laura Boccanfuso,Erin Barney,Marilena Mademtzi,Claire Foster,Quan Wang,Colette Torres,Lisa Chen,Brian Scassellati,Pamela Ventola,Frederick Shic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究将"以教促学"教学法引入机器人辅助自闭症干预，比较机器人与人类同伴（confederate）条件下儿童社交技能任务表现与投入度。结果显示80%参与者在机器人条件下表现更优（63% vs 37%），90%参与者投入度显著更高（61% vs 32%），且机器人条件下的投入度与表现提升相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往机器人辅助自闭症干预多将机器人作为教师、临床助手或更有能力的同伴，依赖专业治疗师保障疗效，但很少有研究把主流课堂中已验证的教学法纳入干预设计；而绝大多数ASD儿童在普通班级就读、80%以上时间处于普通课堂环境，存在研究空白。

Method: 提出一种融入"以教促学"（learning-by-teaching）教学法的机器人辅助干预方法，面向学龄ASD儿童的社交技能训练；采用被试内对比设计，让儿童分别在机器人条件与人类同伴（confederate）条件下完成同一组社交技能任务，并测量任务表现与参与度。

Result: 80%的参与者在机器人条件下表现更好（机器人条件平均表现63%，人类同伴条件37%），且与情境顺序无关；90%的参与者在机器人条件下参与度显著更高（机器人61%，人类同伴32%）；该效应未在人类同伴条件中出现，分析表明机器人条件下的整体参与度促成了表现提升。

Conclusion: 在"以教促学"情境中使用机器人，可能有助于提升ASD儿童的参与度并改善其在简单社交技能任务上的表现，为将课堂验证教学法引入机器人辅助自闭症干预提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Interaction paradigms used in robot-assisted autism intervention have historically employed robots as teachers, clinical assistants, or more-abled peers to promote a variety of social skills. These modalities often leverage the expertise of trained practitioners to ensure that child-robot interactions are productive or clinically grounded to yield positive therapeutic benefits for children across the autism spectrum. Yet, despite the fact that the majority of children with autism spectrum disorder (ASD) attend mainstream schools and spend 80% or more of their time in the general classroom [27], there is a paucity of research incorporating validated classroom teaching pedagogies into robot-assisted autism interventions. In this work, we introduce a novel teaching methodology for advancing social skills in school-aged children with ASD. We evaluate the effectiveness of a novel robot-assisted autism intervention which incorporates the learning-by-teaching pedagogy and explores the comparative benefits of employing a robot versus a human confederate for improved performance on a set of social skills tasks. Results show that 80% of study participants performed better in the robot condition (mean performance in the robot condition=63%, mean performance in the confederate condition=37%), irrespective of the scenario order. Further, 90% of all participants were significantly more engaged in the robot condition (mean engagement: robot=61%, confederate=32%) and, while the effect did not result in the confederate condition, analyses indicate that overall engagement in the robot condition contributed to improved performance. These results suggest that robots employed in a learning-by-teaching context may help enhance engagement and improve performance on a simple social skills task for children with ASD.

</details>


### [260] [Beyond Dead Reckoning: A Point of View on Camera--DAS--GNSS Continuity in Road Tunnels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14748)
*Lyes Saad Saoud,Hesham A. Rakha,Mona Jaber,Moussa Ayyash*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将隧道内定位重新定义为"基础设施辅助的跨模态轨迹连续性"问题，用DAS、相机、车载传感与边缘计算实现完整性约束的连续定位，并给出可证伪的研究部署议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路隧道遮蔽卫星信号，而网联自动驾�驶车仍需要连续、可归属、带完整性边界的定位；将隧道定位视为对最后一次可信卫星定位的外推既不安全也不可验证。

Method: 观点性（Point of View）论文，无实验方法。提出架构：可信的洞口卫星解作为全局锚点，分布式声学传感（DAS）提供连续运动证据，相机提供稀疏身份与车道锚点，车载传感提供短期动力学，边缘计算负责关联、融合、完整性监测与受控重捕获；真值仅用于离线标定与验证。

Result: 属于立场/议程型文章，未报告实验结果或定量性能指标，主要产出是一个可证伪的研究与部署路线图。

Conclusion: 隧道定位应从卫星外推转向基础设施辅助、跨模态、完整性感知的轨迹连续性框架，并以离线真值做标定与验证。

Abstract: Road tunnels remove satellite visibility where connected and automated vehicles still require continuous, attributable, and integrity-bounded positioning. This Point of View argues that tunnel localization should be treated as infrastructure-assisted cross-modal track continuity, not as extrapolation from the last trusted satellite fix. A trusted portal satellite solution provides the global anchor, distributed acoustic sensing (DAS) continuous motion evidence, cameras sparse identity and lane anchors, onboard sensing short-term dynamics, and edge computing association, fusion, integrity monitoring, and guarded reacquisition. Ground truth is reserved for offline calibration and validation. The article develops a falsifiable research and deployment agenda for progressing from synchronized multimodal evidence to validated, integrity-aware tunnel positioning continuity.

</details>


### [261] [An Adaptive Fixed-Time Line-of-Sight Guidance Scheme for 3D Path Following of Underwater Vehicles: Theory and Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14756)
*Hanzhi Yang,Jalil Chavez-Galaviz,Nina Mahmoudian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出固定时间自适应LOS制导框架，用于AUV三维路径跟踪，收敛时间与初始状态无关，并含时变前视机制，仿真与Iver 3实船实验验证优于SOTA自适应LOS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LOS制导为渐近收敛，受扰后恢复慢且跟踪性能不可预测；现有鲁棒控制方法需改动底层控制器，难以在商用AUV平台上应用。因此需要一种仅在制导层实现、可保证预设时间内收敛且能快速补偿洋流侧滑扰动的方案。

Method: 将固定时间稳定性理论与LOS制导结合，构建固定时间自适应LOS制导律；设计固定时间自适应估计器快速补偿时变侧滑扰动；引入时变前视距离机制以改善曲线路径跟踪；通过Lyapunov分析证明框架的固定时间稳定性。

Result: 仿真中曲线路径跟踪下时变前视变体将横向与垂向跟踪RMSE分别降低69.37%和67.46%；Iver 3 AUV现场实验中，直线与曲线路径平均跟踪误差较常规自适应LOS分别降低56.35%与27.59%，跟踪精度、收敛速度和抗干扰能力均优于SOTA。

Conclusion: 该框架为复杂海洋环境下AUV可靠自主导航提供了实用的制导层解决方案，无需修改底层控制器即可部署，具备工程可行性。

Abstract: Reliable path tracking is crucial for autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) operating in dynamic and uncertain marine environments. However, traditional line-of-sight (LOS) guidance methods rely on asymptotic convergence, resulting in slow disturbance recovery and unpredictable tracking performance. Existing robust control methods typically require modifications to the underlying vehicle controller, limiting their practical application on commercial AUV platforms. This paper proposes a robust fixed-time adaptive LOS guidance framework for 3D path tracking for AUVs. By combining fixed-time stability theory with LOS guidance, this method guarantees path tracking convergence within a preset time range, with the convergence time independent of initial conditions. Furthermore, a fixed-time adaptive estimator is developed to rapidly compensate for time-varying sideslip disturbances caused by ocean currents. A time-varying look-ahead mechanism is also introduced to improve tracking performance on curved paths. Lyapunov analysis proves the fixed-time stability of the proposed framework, and numerical simulations and physical experiments demonstrate that, compared to state-of-the-art adaptive LOS methods, this framework exhibits superior tracking accuracy, convergence speed, and anti-interference capability. In simulation, the time-varying look-ahead variant reduced cross-track and vertical-track RMSE by 69.37\% and 67.46\%, respectively, during curved-path tracking. In field experiments with an Iver 3 AUV, the proposed fixed-time guidance reduced average tracking error by 56.35\% in straight-path evaluation and 27.59\% in curved-path evaluation compared with conventional adaptive LOS this http URL proposed method provides a practical guidance-level solution for achieving reliable autonomous navigation of AUVs in complex marine environments.

</details>


### [262] [A Personalized Dynamic Balance Evaluation Paradigm for Hip Exoskeleton-Assisted Walking under Unexpected Ground Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14765)
*Yun Chen,Oluwasegun T. Akinniyi,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出参与者个体的复合平衡代价函数，融合7项生物力学子指标并通过单纯形上的非负权重学习，结合经验贝叶斯层次模型估计各辅助条件的P(best)与高概率候选集，在3名受试者、46种髋关节助力条件下验证可显著缩小个性化实验的候选集规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 髋关节外骨骼有望改善对突发步态扰动的恢复能力，但助力个性化困难：平衡是多维概念、人在回路实验样本少且噪声大，缺少能综合多维度平衡指标并适合小样本统计推断的代价函数。

Method: 构建包含7个生物力学子指标（稳定裕度、质心动力学、全身角动量）的参与者特异复合平衡代价；子指标转为方向对齐的无量纲代价特征，在单纯形上学习非负融合权重；与经验贝叶斯层次模型耦合，估计每个条件的后验最优概率P(best)及规模为K_0.8的高概率候选集；3名受试者以1.1 m/s行走、单侧皮带滑移扰动、46种髋助力条件，每条件4次重复，与等权融合、PCA融合对比，并做留一重复重拟合。

Result: 全预算分析中学习型选择器将80%后验概率集中在46个条件中的1–5个，而等权融合为2–12个、PCA融合为4–37个；被选条件下观测复合代价低于零力矩条件（P2、P3名义p<0.05）；留一重复重拟合在所有参与者上得到正的留出秩相关，学习权重与候选集具有中等稳定性。

Conclusion: 概念验证结果表明，参与者特异的复合平衡评估可用于扰动式人在回路实验中的候选条件筛选，有望缩短个性化实验、减少受试者反复暴露于扰动的次数；样本量小（3人）为主要局限。

Abstract: Hip exoskeletons may improve recovery from unexpected gait perturbations, yet personalizing assistance remains difficult because balance is multidimensional and human-in-the-loop experiments are small-sample and noisy. We present a participant-specific composite balance cost that integrates seven biomechanical sub-metrics spanning margin of stability, center-of-mass dynamics, and whole-body angular momentum. The sub-metrics are converted to direction-aligned, dimensionless cost features, and nonnegative fusion weights are learned on the simplex. Coupled with an empirical-Bayes hierarchical model, the learned-composite selector estimates each tested condition's posterior probability of being best, P(best), and a high-probability candidate set with size $K_{0.8}$. The framework was evaluated with three participants walking at 1.1 m/s during unilateral belt-slip perturbations across 46 hip-assistance conditions. In the full-budget analysis (B = 4 repeats per condition), the selector concentrated 80% of the posterior probability within 1 to 5 of 46 conditions, compared with 2 to 12 for equal-weight fusion and 4 to 37 for principal component analysis fusion. This smaller candidate set could shorten personalization experiments and limit participants' exposure to repeated perturbations in future studies. Selected-condition trials showed lower observed composite costs than no-torque trials, with nominal p < 0.05 for P2 and P3. Leave-one-repeat-out refits yielded positive mean held-out rank correlations for all participants and moderate stability of the learned weights and candidate sets. These proof-of-concept results support participant-specific composite balance evaluation for candidate selection in perturbation-based human-in-the-loop experiments.

</details>


### [263] [Language-Guided Representation Learning for Robust Cross-Sensor Material Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14783)
*Mashood M. Mohsan,Muhayy Ud Din,Binzhao Xu,Ahmad Abubakar,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出语言引导的蒸馏框架，用语言作为跨硬件不变的监督信号，学习传感器鲁棒的触觉表征，在少样本与跨传感器迁移上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉难以推断软硬度、纹理、接触稳定性等触觉属性，机器人操作需要触觉。但视触传感器因光学、弹性体、光照差异，对同一材料产生不同观测，导致单/多传感器训练时泛化性差。需要一种与传感硬件无关的表征学习监督信号。

Method: 构建39K样本的触觉-语言数据集（含人工标注材料标签）；训练触觉编码器，将传感器特定的触觉图像与语言嵌入对齐到共享语义空间，实现语言引导的蒸馏式跨传感器表征学习；在少样本学习与跨传感器迁移上评估，并在六个已有触觉数据集上基准测试。

Result: 100-shot设置下达到95%准确率；跨传感器迁移平均提升13.3%准确率；在六个已有触觉数据集上最多获得19%准确率增益。代码与数据集已公开。

Conclusion: 语言引导蒸馏可实现可扩展、硬件无关的触觉表征学习，为跨传感器触觉感知提供通用范式。

Abstract: Robots need touch to manipulate objects safely and reliably, as many properties, such as softness, texture, and contact stability, are hard to infer from vision alone. However, vision-based tactile sensors yield different observations of the same material due to variations in optics, elastomer properties, and illumination, leading to poor generalization when trained on a single or multiple sensors. We propose a language-guided distillation framework for learning sensor-robust tactile representations. Language encodes high-level semantic properties of touch (e.g., rough, soft, slippery) that remain invariant across sensing hardware, providing a natural sensor-agnostic supervisory signal. We construct a 39K-sample touch-language dataset with human-annotated material labels and train a tactile encoder to align sensor-specific tactile images with language embeddings in a shared semantic space. We evaluate our approach for few-shot learning and cross-sensor transfer and benchmark it on six existing tactile datasets. Our method achieves 95% accuracy in the 100-shot setting, improves cross-sensor transfer by an average of 13.3% accuracy, and yields up to 19% accuracy gains across six existing tactile datasets. These results demonstrate that language-guided distillation enables scalable and hardware-agnostic tactile representation learning. Code and dataset are available at this https URL

</details>


### [264] [Belief-Adaptive Online Autonomy for Quadrotor UAV Navigation under GNSS Degradation in Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14806)
*Deepak Kumar Panda,Weisi Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"信念自适应"在线自主框架：在EKF中显式建模GNSS信任度，用二阶在线信念自适应与延迟感知的乱序测量处理，实现无需环境先验或离线训练的GNSS-IMU融合鲁棒性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空域中GNSS受多路径、遮挡与延迟影响，误差呈非平稳且时间相关，使常规GNSS-IMU融合性能退化；现有反应式协方差调整缺乏前瞻性与稳定性，且依赖环境先验或离线训练。

Method: 以EKF为骨架，引入隐信念状态表征GNSS信任度，调制量测权重与多路径偏置不确定性；利用EKF一致性信号在线更新信念，采用二阶信念自适应实现前瞻稳定调节；并加入延迟感知的乱序量测处理机制。保留经典融合结构，可直接嵌入现有飞控流程。

Result: 在城市空中交通仿真场景（相关多路径、随机延迟、障碍约束）中，相比朴素、自适应及一阶基线，信念收敛更好、轨迹更平滑，估计与跟踪误差降低。

Conclusion: 该框架在GNSS退化环境下提升了在线自主导航的鲁棒性，结构与现有GNSS-IMU融合及飞控管线兼容，便于工程集成。

Abstract: Reliable online autonomy is critical for quadrotor operation in urban airspaces, where global navigation satellite systems (GNSS) measurements suffer from multipath, blockage, and latency issues, introducing non-stationary, temporally correlated errors that degrade conventional GNSS-IMU fusion. This paper presents a belief-adaptive online autonomy framework that augments an extended Kalman filter (EKF) with explicit GNSS trust modelling, second-order online belief adaptation, and latency-aware out-of-sequence measurement handling. GNSS trust is represented as a latent belief state that modulates measurement weighting and multipath bias uncertainty, and is updated online using EKF consistency signals. Unlike reactive covariance tuning, the proposed approach enables proactive and stable sensor trust adaptation without prior environmental knowledge or offline training. Evaluation in simulated urban air mobility scenarios with correlated multipath, stochastic latency, and obstacle constraints demonstrates improved belief convergence, smoother trajectories, and reduced estimation and tracking errors compared to naive, adaptive, and first-order baselines. The framework preserves classical GNSS-IMU fusion structure and can be integrated directly into existing flight control pipelines, supporting robust online autonomy in GNSS degraded environments.

</details>


### [265] [Primitive-Informed Sampling-Based MPC for Multi-Fingered Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14868)
*Emek Barış Küçüktabak,Karankumar Patel,Jinda Cui,Zhaodong Yang,Kazuhiro Sasabuchi,Jun Takamatsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于低维操作基元的采样式MPC框架，用于多指灵巧手操作，通过基元偏置采样并结合关节残差优化，配合rollout约束提升采样效率，在Allegro真手+MuJoCo孪生平台上验证了连续手内旋转、抓取、重定向与臂手协同任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样式MPC可避开复杂接触动力学的梯度问题，但在高维关节空间直接探索效率低，且性能强烈依赖采样分布；现有方法缺乏对手指协同运动的低维结构利用，导致高维采样预算利用率差。

Method: 用编码协同手指运动的低维操作基元偏置采样分布，同时在线优化关节级残差使基元适配当前手-物构型；在前向仿真中施加任务相关rollout约束以剔除不可行轨迹，提升有效采样比例。基元来源包括仿真训练策略与人类手部运动数据，实验在真实Allegro手与同步MuJoCo数字孪生上进行并做消融。

Result: 消融显示基元与残差二者缺一不可，仅增大采样预算无法恢复协同性；rollout约束显著提升成功率；单一物体提取的基元可跨物体尺寸并在模型失配下保持有效；框架还支持抓取、物体重定向及臂-手伸手-抓取-搬运的协同任务。

Conclusion: 低维操作基元与关节残差的组合是采样式灵巧操作MPC的有效归纳偏置，显著优于纯高维采样；基元具有跨物体与跨模型误差的泛化能力，且可从仿真策略或人类示教中获取，具备多任务扩展潜力。

Abstract: We present a primitive-informed sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) framework for multi-fingered dexterous manipulation. Sampling-based MPC avoids the need for gradients through complex contact dynamics, but direct exploration of the high-dimensional joint space is inefficient and makes performance strongly dependent on the sampling distribution. Our framework biases sampling using low-dimensional manipulation primitives that encode coordinated finger motions, while simultaneously optimizing joint-level residuals to adapt these motions to the current hand-object configuration. Task-related rollout constraints reject infeasible trajectories during forward simulation, improving the effective use of the sampling budget. We evaluate the approach on a physical Allegro hand using a synchronized MuJoCo digital twin. Ablations show that both the primitive and residual are necessary for reliable continuous in-hand rotation, that increasing the sampling budget alone does not recover this coordination, and that rollout constraints substantially improve success rate. A primitive extracted from a single object remains effective across object sizes and under model mismatch, and the framework further supports grasping, object reorientation, and coordinated arm-hand reach-grasp-transport, using primitives extracted from both a simulation-trained policy and human hand-motion data.

</details>


### [266] [Real-World Reinforcement Learning with MPC Scaffolding for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14878)
*Emek Barış Küçüktabak,Karankumar Patel,Zhaodong Yang,Jinda Cui,Kazuhiro Sasabuchi,Jun Takamatsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用采样型MPC作为"脚手架"为真实世界灵巧强化学习提供结构化先验与在线引导：少量MPC轨迹预填回放池并预训练actor-critic，在线阶段MPC间歇性引导数据采集，配合off-policy SAC，控制器逐步交权给学习策略。Allegro手上实现7分钟在线训练后100%成功率、20分钟后转速超MPC 5倍、110分钟1000次连续旋转无掉落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界灵巧操作RL可从物理交互中直接习得策略，但早期探索低效、失败代价高（物体掉落、硬件磨损、人工复位），且不依赖人类示范或纠错动作时很难获得任务导向的初始经验。需要一个能提供结构化先验、降低探索风险与人工介入的框架。

Method: 以采样型MPC（sampling-based MPC）作为学习脚手架：1) 用少量（20条、硬件上12分钟采集）MPC轨迹填充offline replay buffer并预训练actor与critic；2) 在线阶段MPC间歇性介入引导数据采集，off-policy Soft Actor-Critic同时从历史MPC经验与新采集的真实交互中学习；3) 控制权从MPC逐步过渡到学习策略。任务为16-DoF Allegro手的连续in-hand旋转，另做不同物体几何形状的快速适配与目标条件重定向，并通过消融验证MPC预训练、保留MPC经验、在线MPC引导三者的互补作用。

Result: 硬件实验：20条MPC轨迹（12分钟采集）初始化后，仅7分钟在线RL即达到policy-only评估100%成功（5/5 trials）；在线训练平均约3次物体掉落；20分钟在线学习后策略旋转速度达MPC控制器的5倍以上；连续1000次旋转、超110分钟无掉落。消融显示MPC预训练、保留MPC经验与在线MPC引导三部分均有互补增益；并成功迁移到不同物体几何与目标条件重定向任务。

Conclusion: 采样型MPC可有效充当真实世界灵巧RL的脚手架，在无人类示范、低人工干预条件下实现高效、鲁棒的实机学习与快速任务适配，说明"模型控制先验+在线RL"的混合范式在接触丰富操作中具备实用价值。

Abstract: Real-world reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising route to dexterous manipulation policies that can adapt directly from physical interaction, but learning is hindered by inefficient early exploration and costly failures. We propose a framework that uses sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) as scaffolding for real-world dexterous RL, providing structured prior experience and task-directed guidance during learning without human demonstrations or corrective actions. A small set of MPC trajectories is first used to populate an offline replay buffer and to pretrain the actor and critic. During online learning, MPC intermittently guides data collection while an off-policy Soft Actor-Critic learner trains from both prior MPC experience and newly collected physical interaction, with control gradually transitioning to the learned policy. On continuous in-hand rotation with a 16-DoF Allegro hand, the method reaches 100\% success in policy-only evaluation (5/5 trials) after 7 minutes of online RL, following initialization with 20 MPC trajectories collected on hardware in 12 minutes. Online training incurs about three object drops on average. After 20 minutes of online learning, the policy achieves more than five times the rotation speed of the MPC controller. It completes 1000 consecutive rotations over more than 110 minutes without a drop. Ablations show complementary benefits from MPC-based pretraining, retained MPC experience, and online MPC guidance. We further demonstrate rapid adaptation to different object geometries and successful goal-conditioned reorientation, showing that the framework enables efficient, low-intervention, real-world dexterous RL.

</details>


### [267] [HydroMap: Probabilistic Water Surface Elevation Mapping for Semantic Scene Representation in Inland Waterways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14903)
*Zhongbi Luo,Yunjia Wang,Herman Bruyninckx,Peter Slaets*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HydroMap：里程计解耦框架，用双目观测重建水面高程并与LiDAR结构图融合，形成持续的2.5D概率高程与语义地图，面向内河航道自主水面航行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内河作业的自主水面航行器需要同时对周边结构与水面进行持续表征，但LiDAR对水面返回稀疏或缺失，导致重建场景中缺少该作业面。

Method: 与里程计解耦的框架：由双目观测恢复水面高程；每帧水点与传播的立体与位姿不确定性构成联合栅格观测，逐帧融合为持续的概率高程图；再经语义地图转换，将高程图与结构几何统一为包含水面、边界、结构物与上方区域的2.5D表达。

Result: 在Pohang Canal与Leuven Vaart数据集上，相对同一地图坐标系下的LiDAR参考，高程RMSE低于5 cm；高程图与语义图分别以2 Hz与1 Hz发布。

Conclusion: HydroMap以持续的水面表征补全LiDAR地图，为内河航道下游导航提供支持。

Abstract: Autonomous surface vehicles operating in inland waterways require a persistent representation of both surrounding structures and the water surface. LiDAR-based simultaneous localization and mapping often produces sparse or missing water returns, leaving this operational surface absent from the reconstructed scene. We propose HydroMap, an odometry-decoupled framework that reconstructs water surface elevation from stereo observations and integrates it with the structural map. Per-frame water points form joint cell observations with propagated stereo and pose uncertainty, and successive observations are fused into a persistent probabilistic elevation map. Semantic map conversion then combines the elevation map with structural geometry in a unified 2.5D representation of water, boundaries, structures, and overhead regions. On the Pohang Canal and Leuven Vaart datasets, the elevation RMSE remains below 5 cm relative to LiDAR references expressed in the same map frame. The elevation and semantic maps are published at 2 Hz and 1 Hz, respectively. HydroMap thereby complements LiDAR maps with a persistent representation of the water surface for downstream navigation in inland waterways.

</details>


### [268] [Exact Feasibility Certification and Optimal Responsibility Allocation for Multi-Robot CBF Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14935)
*Chandan Kumar Sah,Jishnu Keshavan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多机器人CBF安全滤波器的精确可行性证书，将安全约束需求与执行器供给分离，量化可行性余量，并可定位冲突代理与交互；进一步提出最大化最差局部可行性余量的共享安全约束最优分配算法（多面体输入集下为线性规划）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人CBF安全滤波器可能不可行，但QP求解失败本身不说明冲突原因，也不指示如何解决；需要一种机制诊断不可行性来源并指导修复（调增益、增执行能力或重新分配约束）。

Method: 针对异质控制仿射动力学与凸输入集的多智能体CBF滤波器，构造精确可行性证书：将安全约束施加的"需求"与执行器"供给"分解，得到可行性储备。据此分析CBF增益调整或增加驱动的可解/不可解边界，并识别导致冲突的智能体与交互对。另提出共享安全约束的最优分配算法，最大化最差局部可行余量；对多面体输入集可化为线性规划求解。

Result: 320组配对闭环仿真中，所提分配将不可行控制步从约50%降至6.2%，安全违规运行从118/160降至24/160；在52次不可行事件中，证书在94%情形下识别出放松后可恢复可行性的交互。

Conclusion: 该可行性证书给出了CBF不可行性的可解释、可计算的诊断与归因，并能指导约束分配优化，显著提升多机器人安全滤波的可行性率与安全性，同时为增益调节与执行能力提升的适用边界提供理论判据。

Abstract: Multi-robot Control Barrier Function (CBF) safety filters can become infeasible, but a failed quadratic program (QP) does not indicate why the conflict occurred or how to resolve it. To address this, we develop an exact feasibility certificate for multi-agent CBF filters with heterogeneous control-affine dynamics and convex input sets. The certificate quantifies a feasibility reserve by separating the demand imposed by safety constraints from the available actuator supply. This decomposition shows when CBF gain tuning or increased actuation can and cannot resolve infeasibility, and identifies the agents and interactions responsible for the conflict. We further propose an algorithm to optimally allocate shared safety constraints by maximizing the worst local feasibility margin, yielding a linear program for polyhedral input sets. In $320$ paired closed-loop simulations, the proposed allocation reduces infeasible control steps from roughly $50\%$ to $6.2\%$, and reduces safety-violating runs from $118/160$ to $24/160$. In addition, across $52$ infeasibility events, the certificate identifies an interaction whose relaxation restores feasibility in $94\%$ of cases.

</details>


### [269] [Comparing Trajectories from Positions Alone: Curvature-Based Time Alignment and Drift Error Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14936)
*Effie Daum,Daniele De Martini,Claire Dune,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套标准化轨迹评估协议，结合基于曲率信号的时间对齐方法与按行驶距离归一化的误差指标，并量化时间同步、采样对齐和外参标定的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域缺乏比被评估估计更精确的独立大规模参考轨迹，导致ATE/RPE指标的隐含假设与参数常不公开，错误结果易误导且阻碍公平比较。

Method: 将基于曲率信号的新颖时间对齐方法与按行驶距离归一化的误差度量结合，显式处理时间同步、采样对齐与外参标定，并通过敏感性分析量化三者影响。

Result: 通过敏感性分析量化了时间同步、采样对齐和外参标定对轨迹评估结果的影响程度（摘要未给出具体数值结果）。

Conclusion: 该协议有助于实现更严谨、可复现、标准化的轨迹评估。

Abstract: In field robotics, acquiring independent large-scale reference trajectories more accurate than the evaluated estimates remains an open challenge. The domain is widely reliant on Absolute Trajectory Error (ATE) and Relative Pose Error (RPE), computed with automated tools, that rest on assumptions and evaluation parameters rarely made explicit. When unreported, the errors can be misleading and hinder fair comparisons. This paper introduces a trajectory-evaluation protocol for standardized and reliable accuracy assessment in state estimation, localization, and Simultaneous Localization And Mapping (SLAM). The approach combines a novel temporal alignment method based on curvature signals with an error metric normalized by travelled distance. We explicitly account for temporal synchronization, sampling alignment, and extrinsic calibration, quantifying their influence through a sensitivity analysis.
