TL;DR: 提出 WLA³ 通用策略框架，用世界潜动作模型（WLAM）从多模态世界状态变化中学习统一的潜动作表示，并复用于语义、动力学与运动学，实现跨人类视频与机器人数据的可扩展监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用策略模型规模扩展受限于缺乏统一、低噪声的动作监督。人类第一视角视频充足，但仅有少部分带高质量手部动作标签；而世界状态转移是跨数据源共有的动作相关监督信号。

Method: WLAM 在局部时间窗内学习多模态世界状态如何变化，将同步相机视图与可得的具身状态变化编码为紧凑的局部潜动作与更丰富的过渡特征；通过部分模态重建与重叠窗口一致性增强表示的鲁棒性。WLA³ 将潜动作复用于三方面：局部潜动作支撑动作敏感的物理动力学建模；片段级特征经语义潜聚合（SLA）直接监督 VLM；动作专家联合预测潜动作与具身相关机器人控制。

Result: 在 LARYBench 上，最终 32D 潜动作平均分类准确率达 67.89%；在六个真实机器人任务上平均成功率 81.9%，对比 π₀.₅ 的 66.2%。性能随通用策略模型中期训练数据量增加而提升，人类视频支持人到机器人的迁移。

Conclusion: 世界潜动作可作为跨异构数据的统一动作监督，兼顾语义、动力学与运动学，为通用策略模型的可扩展训练与人类视频到机器人迁移提供有效路径。

Abstract: Scaling generalist policy models with heterogeneous data is limited by the lack of unified, low-noise action supervision. Human egocentric videos are abundant, but only a small fraction comes with high-quality hand-action labels. Observed world transitions offer a common source of action-related supervision across data sources. We introduce WLA$^3$ (World Latent Action Modeling for Semantics, Dynamics, and Kinematics), a unified generalist policy model framework built around representations learned by a World Latent Action Model (WLAM). WLAM first learns how multimodal world states change over a local interval, encoding synchronized camera views and available embodiment-state changes into a compact local latent action and a richer transition feature. Reconstruction from partial modalities and consistency across overlapping windows encourage robust transition representations. WLA$^3$ reuses them across semantics, dynamics, and kinematics: local latent actions support action-sensitive physical-dynamics modeling, segment-level features directly supervise the VLM through a Semantic Latent Aggregate (SLA), and an action expert jointly predicts latent actions together with embodiment-specific robot controls. Human videos provide scalable transition supervision, while robot trajectories ground the shared representation in executable native controls. On LARYBench, the final 32D latent action reaches 67.89\% average classification accuracy. WLA$^3$ achieves 81.9% average success across six real-robot tasks versus 66.2% for $\pi_{0.5}$. Performance improves as generalist policy model mid-training data scales, and human videos support human-to-robot transfer. Project page can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [305] [Goal-Oriented Communications for Physical AI: Design and Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15895)
*Shutong Chen,Wenkai Zhang,Adnan Aijaz,Miao Guo,Yansha Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作构建了面向Physical AI的端到端GoC（goal-oriented communication）测试床，通过5G OpenAirInterface连接PiPER机械臂（RGB-D相机+5G模组）与Jetson AGX Orin边缘服务器，实现并对比了3D边界框、2D场景图、3D场景图三类语义表征传输框架，任务完成时间最多降低52.6%、成功率最多提升45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Physical AI依赖高频、低时延视频流以感知和交互物理世界，现有5G难以承载如此高的数据量；GoC通过只传输任务相关的语义表征被视为有前景的解法，但已有GoC框架几乎只在仿真中评估，缺乏在真实Physical AI应用部署中的有效性验证。

Method: 搭建端到端GoC测试床：PiPER机械臂+RGB-D相机+5G模组，经5G OpenAirInterface网络接入NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin边缘服务器；提出并实现三种GoC框架，分别传输3D bounding box、2D场景图与3D场景图三类语义表征；共用模块包括语义提取、全栈5G传输、语言模型推理、数字孪生校验与机器人控制，构成闭环Physical AI应用。

Result: 在自建测试床上大量实验显示，相比周期性传输原始图像的基线框架，GoC框架将任务完成时间最多缩短52.6%，任务成功概率最多提升45%。

Conclusion: 结果验证了GoC框架的实际有效性，为未来6G网络上高效、可靠的Physical AI应用提供了可行路径；同时首次将GoC从仿真推进到真实机器人部署验证。

Abstract: Physical AI relies on frequently-updated, latency-sensitive video stream to perceive, reason, and interact with the physical world, resulting in strict latency requirements with much higher data volumes that existing 5G networks cannot support. Goal-oriented communication (GoC) offers as a promising approach to solve this challenge by transmitting only task-relevant semantic representations. However, existing GoC frameworks were mainly evaluated in the simulations while their effectiveness has never been validated in a practical deployment of physical AI application. In this work, we develop an end-to-end GoC testbed for Physical AI, which connects a PiPER robot arm equipped with an RGB-D camera and a 5G modem to an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin edge server through a 5G OpenAirInterface network. We propose and implement three GoC frameworks that transmit 3D bounding boxes, 2D scene graphs, and 3D scene graphs, as three types of semantic representations, respectively. They share the common functional modules designed for closed-loop Physical AI applications, including semantic extraction, full stack 5G transmission, language model inference, digital twin validation, and robotic control. Extensive experiments on our testbed show that our GoC frameworks reduce the task completion time by up to 52.6% and improve task success probability by up to 45%, compared to the traditional framework that periodically transmits the raw image data. These results validate the practical effectiveness of our GoC framework and pave the way for efficient and reliable Physical AI applications over future 6G networks. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [306] [SlipSense: Multimodal Tactile Learning for Low-Latency and Generalized Slip Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15910)
*Tong Jian,Aditya Thurvas Senthil Kumar,Xinyi Li,Ziling Chen,Tianyu Dai,Ali Sengul,Matteo Grimaldi,Wenjie Lu,Saleh Nabi,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SlipSense 多模态触觉滑动检测框架，基于 TacV5 传感器（32×32 压阻阵列 240 Hz + 3 轴 MEMS 加速度计 8 kHz），实现 96.7% Macro F1、误报率 <1.6%、23.1 ms 内检出 76% 滑动，并具备跨手、跨传感器、跨平台的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动检测是灵巧操作的基础，但现有系统普遍缺乏对检测延迟的精确定量刻画以及跨平台泛化能力的验证，限制了其在真实机器人操作中的可用性。

Method: 硬件上构建 TacV5 紧凑传感器，融合 240 Hz 压阻阵列（空间压力分布）与 8 kHz 加速度计（摩擦振动），两类信号互补。算法上采用模态特定编码、传感器内融合与跨模态注意力，并在 240 Hz 下进行因果时序预测，保证低延迟在线推理。

Result: 在覆盖 37 类物体、140 万帧的数据集上验证两模态互补性；达到 96.7% Macro F1、误报率低于 1.6%，23.1 ms 内检出 76% 的滑动事件。仅在 UMI 数据上训练后，无需再训练即可零样本迁移至 Tesollo 灵巧手，跨未见物体、不同传感器单元与不同机器人平台均有效。

Conclusion: 该工作表明高频振动与低频压力阵列的跨模态融合能显著提升滑动检测的准确性与时效性，且具备实用的跨平台零样本迁移能力，为触觉驱动的灵巧操作提供了可部署的感知基础。

Abstract: Slip detection is fundamental to dexterous manipulation, yet existing systems often lack precise characterization of detection latency and cross-platform generalization. We present SlipSense, a multimodal tactile slip-detection framework built on TacV5, a compact sensor integrating a $32 \times 32$ piezoresistive array operating at 240 Hz and a 3-axis MEMS accelerometer operating at 8 kHz. The piezoresistive array captures spatial pressure distributions, while the accelerometer captures friction-induced vibrations, providing complementary slip cues. The framework performs modality-specific encoding, intra-sensor fusion, and cross-modal attention with causal temporal prediction at 240 Hz. Experiments on a dataset of 1.4 million frames spanning 37 objects demonstrate the complementarity of the two modalities. SlipSense achieves 96.7% Macro F1 with a false-positive rate below 1.6%, detecting 76% of slip events within 23.1 ms. When trained solely on UMI data, SlipSense generalizes zero-shot to a Tesollo dexterous hand, transferring across unseen objects, distinct sensor units, and robotic platforms without retraining.

</details>


### [307] [Touch2Trace: Tactile-Driven Imitation Learning for Dexterous Cable Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15921)
*Matteo Grimaldi,David Klee,Ziling Chen,Tong Jian,Wonju Lee,Wenjie Lu,Tao Yu,Saleh Nabi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Touch2Trace: 触觉驱动的模仿学习系统，实现灵巧线缆循迹（拇指-食指反复捏合-卷曲送线）；系统刻画了编码器预训练、控制频率、时序上下文与空间分辨率对策略性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体灵巧操作需要指尖级别的压力、摩擦与初始滑移持续调控，而线缆循迹是其中最具挑战的场景之一；同时触觉驱动系统中各关键设计参数（预训练、控制率、时序上下文、触觉分辨率）缺乏系统性的真实世界表征研究。

Method: 硬件为 Tesollo DG-5F 灵巧手 + 定制 32×32 压阻式触觉传感器 TacV5；触觉编码器经自监督学习预训练，策略为轻量 Transformer，通过行为克隆在遥操作演示上训练，60 Hz 闭环部署；无视觉、无显式线缆状态估计，仅触觉 + 本体感知。

Result: 相较仅本体感知基线，平均追踪距离从 0.2 cm 提升至 20.1 cm，成功率从 0% 提升至 93%，并实现向未见线缆与走线条件的零样本迁移；同时量化了预训练、控制频率、时序上下文与空间分辨率的影响。

Conclusion: 触觉反馈在无视觉条件下即可显著提升可变形物体灵巧操作性能，所给出的"获胜配方"与参数消融为触觉驱动系统设计提供了可复用的实证依据。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation of deformable objects demands continuous fingertip-level regulation of pressure, friction, and incipient slip. We study one of the most challenging cases: dexterous cable tracing, feeding a cable through the hand with repeated pinch-and-curl motions of the thumb and index finger. We introduce Touch2Trace, a tactile-driven imitation-learning system for this task, and provide, to our knowledge, the first systematic real-world characterization of how encoder pretraining, control rate, temporal context, and spatial resolution each shape policy performance. The winning learning recipe combines a tactile encoder pretrained for a custom 32 x 32 piezoresistive sensor (TacV5) via self-supervised learning with a lightweight transformer policy trained on teleoperated demonstrations via behavior cloning, deployed at 60 Hz on a Tesollo DG-5F hand. Tactile feedback without vision or explicit cable-state estimation significantly improves tracing performance versus a proprioception-only baseline: from 0.2 cm to 20.1 cm mean distance and 0% to 93% success rate, with zero-shot transfer to unseen cables and routing conditions. The results quantify the influence of key parameters in tactile-driven systems for reliable dexterous deformable object manipulation.

</details>


### [308] [Beyond Single-Axis Testing: Paired Evaluation of Compound Robustness in Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15940)
*Hiroki Sawada,Shunichi Kasahara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LIBERO-CTRL六轴基准，通过单轴条件与匹配的同时条件配对评估，揭示VLA策略复合鲁棒性中"涌现失败"与"补偿成功"两种相反结果变化可相互抵消，导致聚合成功率掩盖真实的行为改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略通常逐个扰动评估，反映其对单一分布偏移的敏感性，但实际部署常面对多个偏移同时发生。核心疑问是：单轴鲁棒性度量能否推断复合鲁棒性？聚合成功率可能因两类相反转变抵消而看似一致。

Method: 构建LIBERO-CTRL六轴基准，将同一初始状态分别置于单轴条件和匹配的同时条件进行配对评估。定义并统计两类相反结果变化：涌现失败（所有单轴 rollout 成功但同时条件失败）与补偿成功（至少一个单轴失败但同时条件成功）。在6个策略、3个严重度等级上量化转变率与结果变化比例，并对随机策略做独立重评估验证转变率稳定性。

Result: 两种相反转变在聚合上大幅抵消：即使两类转变率之差与零无统计显著差异，仍有多达29.0%匹配初始状态发生结果变化；在最受影响条件下，结果变化达34.5%。两类转变的相对占比随策略与严重度变化，而转变率在随机策略独立重评估下保持相近。

Conclusion: 复合鲁棒性无法仅由聚合单轴成功率刻画，必须采用匹配的逐实例评估才能揭示联合扰动如何改变行为，否则个体结果差异会被聚合数值掩盖。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies are typically evaluated one perturbation at a time, providing a useful diagnosis of their sensitivity to individual distribution shifts. Real-world deployment, however, may involve several shifts simultaneously, and it remains unclear how these individual robustness measurements compose. We ask whether compound robustness can be inferred from single-axis evaluations. We introduce LIBERO-CTRL, a six-axis benchmark that pairs each initial state across single-axis conditions and a matched simultaneous condition. This design reveals two opposing outcome changes that aggregate success rates cannot distinguish: emergent failures, where all single-axis rollouts succeed but the simultaneous rollout fails, and compensated successes, where at least one single-axis rollout fails but the simultaneous rollout succeeds. Because one transition decreases compound success while the other increases it, they can cancel, making aggregate compound performance appear consistent with single-axis measurements even when individual outcomes differ substantially. These opposing transitions can largely cancel in aggregate: even when the difference between the two transition rates is not statistically distinguishable from zero, as many as 29.0% of matched initial states still change outcome. Across six policies and three severity levels, such outcome changes reach 34.5% in the most affected condition. The relative prevalence of the two transitions varies across policies and severities, while the transition rates remain similar under independent re-evaluation of stochastic policies. Compound robustness therefore cannot be characterized from aggregate single-axis success rates alone; matched per-instance evaluation is needed to reveal how joint perturbations alter behavior.

</details>


### [309] [MessyMem: Learning-from-Doing Memory for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15976)
*Anuva Banwasi,William Muckelroy III,Priya Sundaresan,Linfeng Zhao,Jeannette Bohg,Cherie Ho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MessyMem：面向移动操作机器人的持久记忆系统，用空间锚定的3D场景图+交互经验+视觉观测链接，实现跨任务知识复用，25任务连续仿真达80.0%进度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作机器人在多房间、多次访问场景中应随经验提升，但现有系统把每个任务当新任务：紧凑场景表示丢弃交互知识，原始视频难以查询，VLM规划器推理时不更新持久知识。

Method: 维护空间锚定的3D场景图（物体与位置），用交互中学到的属性和结果进行增强，并链接视觉观测以支持细粒度回忆；作为持久记忆供跨任务复用。

Result: 在仿真与真实移动操作平台上评测。25任务连续仿真（超3小时）达80.0%任务进度，比最强消融高14.8个百分点，比最强外部基线高28.9个百分点，并能从数千关键帧、超一小时历史中检索任务相关证据。

Conclusion: 显式持久记忆（带交互增强的3D场景图+可查询视觉历史）显著优于无记忆/VLM即时推理方案，支持长时序跨任务知识复用。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators deployed across many rooms and visits should improve with experience: after discovering that a cabinet is locked or finding an object in a drawer, the robot should reuse that knowledge rather than start each task from scratch. Yet today's robots often treat each task as new: compact scene representations omit interaction-derived knowledge, raw video histories are difficult to query, and VLM planners reason at inference time without persistently updating what the robot knows. We present MessyMem, a persistent memory system that enables mobile manipulators to learn from experience and reuse that knowledge across future tasks. It maintains a spatially grounded 3D scene graph of objects and locations, augments it with properties and outcomes learned through interaction, and links visual observations for fine-grained recall. We evaluate MessyMem in simulation and on a real mobile manipulator. In a continuous 25-task simulation spanning over 3 hours, MessyMem achieves 80.0% task progress, outperforming the strongest ablation by 14.8 percentage points and the strongest external baseline by 28.9 points, while retrieving task-relevant evidence from thousands of stored keyframes and over an hour into the past.

</details>


### [310] [ResSafe: Learning Safety Filtering with Residual Reinforcement Learning for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15988)
*Gechen Qu,Tong Zhang,Bike Zhang,Yen-Jen Wang,Koushil Sreenath,Claire Tomlin,Jason Jangho Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出用残差强化学习作为隐式安全滤波机制：将标称策略（仅关注任务性能）与残差策略（学习安全修正）解耦，从而改善性能-安全Pareto权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人动力学维度高、接触丰富且对扰动敏感，安全控制困难；单一策略难以同时兼顾性能、安全与鲁棒性，且需要精心调节多个相互竞争的奖励项。

Method: 将任务策略与安全策略解耦：标称策略只优化任务性能，另训练一个残差策略学习安全修正动作，叠加在标称动作之上，使残差策略充当隐式安全滤波器。

Result: 摘要层面声明该解耦方式带来更好的性能-安全Pareto权衡，避免单一策略中多奖励项的繁琐调参，残差策略可等效为隐式安全滤波；摘要未给出具体量化指标。

Conclusion: 残差强化学习可作为一种隐式安全滤波机制用于人形机器人安全控制，性能与安全的解耦是有前景的设计范式。

Abstract: Safe control of humanoid robots remains challenging due to their high-dimensional dynamics, contact-rich interactions, and sensitivity to disturbances. Although reinforcement learning has enabled effective locomotion and motion tracking, learned policies can still generate unsafe actions that lead to instability or falls. In this work, we propose residual reinforcement learning as an implicit safety-filtering mechanism for safe humanoid control. Instead of relying on a single nominal policy to simultaneously balance performance, safety, and robustness, we decouple performance and safety. The nominal policy focuses solely on task performance, while a residual policy learns safety corrections. This decoupling leads to a better performance--safety Pareto trade-off and avoids the need for careful tuning of multiple competing reward terms within a single policy training. We show that the residual policy can act as an implicit safety filter.

</details>
